‘tvāmagne’ ityekādaśarcaṃ caturthaṃ sūktamātrasya vasuśrutasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamāgneyam |

tvāmagne vasupatiṃ vasūnām pra mande adhvareṣu rājan | tvayā vājaṃ vājayanto jayemābhi ṣyāma pṛtutīrmartyānām || 1 || tvām | agne | vasu'patim | vasūnām | abhi | pra | mande | adhvareṣu | rājan | tvayā | vājam | vāja'yantaḥ | jayema | abhi | syāma | pṛtutīḥ | martyānām || 1 ||
he agne vasūnāṃ vasupatim |
vṛttyavṛttibhyāṃ vasūnāṃ bāhuvyaṃ tatsvāmitvaṃ coktam |

darśapūrṇamāse patnīsaṃyāje gṛhapateryājyaipā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘havyavāḻbhirajaraḥ pitā na iti patnīsaṃyājāḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 1. 10) iti |
devasuvāṃ haviḥpvapyagneyaiva yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca— ‘havyavāḻbhirajaraḥ pitā naraśca ca soma no vaśaḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 4. 11) iti ||
havyavāḻbhirajaraḥ pitā nau vibhurvibhāvā sudṛśīko agne | sugārhapatyāḥ samidho didīhyasmadyaksaṃ mimīhi śravāṃsi || 2 || havya'vāṭ | agniḥ | ajaraḥ | pitā | naḥ | vi'bhuḥ | vibhā'vā | su'dṛśīkaḥ | asme iti | su'gārhapatyāḥ | sam | idhaḥ | didīhi | asmadyak | sam | mimīhi | śravāṃsi || 2 ||
ayaṃ havyavāḻagniḥ ajaraḥ arjāṇaḥ san naḥ pitā bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
kiṃca vibhuḥ vyāptaḥ vibhāvā dīptimān sudṛśīkaḥ śobhanadarśanaḥ asme asmākaṃ bhavatu |
atha pratyakṣavādaḥ |

vātasya patanasādhane'ntarikṣe gacchantau ᵥdaivyā devāgnerādityācca samudbhūtau ᵥhotārā devānāmāhvātārī yuvāṃ ᵥmanupaḥ manuṣyasya ᵥimaṃ ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥyajñamā ᵥgatam āgacchatam |
no manuṣo yajñamiti vyadhikaraṇe ṣaṣṭhyau |
asmākaṃ yajamānasya yajñamityarthaḥ ||
1. pa-rakṣaṇāya |
2. pa-la-abhimāninyau devyau |

vuta āgamya ca vtmanā ātmanā svayameva vyajñeyajñe sarveṣu yajñeṣu vnaḥ asmān vudava utkṛṣṭaṃ rakṣa ||

tatra havyāni gāmaya ||

' agni
1. pa-athavaitāstisraḥ |
2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha- vā devyaḥ |
3. pa-agnaye idaṃ haviḥ svāhā karomi varuṇāya svāhā indrāya marudbhyaḥ svāhā svāhutaṃ karomītyarthaḥ |
evaṃ svāhākṛtistutiḥ |
kha-ga- gha-ca-ṇa-na-bha-sa- ' tebhyaḥ ' nāsti |
taṃ manya iti pāṅktam' ( āśva. śrau. 4. 13 ) iti hi sūtritam |
āśvamedhike' madhyame'hanīdameva ājyaśastram |
sūtritaṃ ca—' agniṃ taṃ manya ityājyaṃ tasyaikāhikamupariṣṭāt ' ( āśva. śrau. 10.10 ) iti |
ādya ābhipluvikeṣvatheṣu maitrāvaruṇasyānurūpastṛcaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—' agniṃ taṃ manye yo vasurāme sthūraṃ rathi bhara ' ( āśva. śrau. 7. 8 ) iti' |
agniṃ taṃ manye yo vasṷrastaṃ yaṃ yanti dhenavaḥ |
asta̭marva̭nta ā॒śava̭'staṃ ni॒tyāso॒ vāji̭na i॒ṣaṃ stotṛ̭bhya ā bha̭ra ||
1 ||
agnim | tam | manye | yaḥ | vasṷḥ | asta̭m | yam | yanti | dhenavaḥ | asta̭m | arva̭ntaḥ | ā॒śava̭ḥ | asta̭m | ni॒tyāsa̭ḥ | vāji̭naḥ | i॒ṣam | stotṛ̭bhyaḥ | ā | bhara || 1 ||
1tam 2agni 3manye staumi |

10astam uktalakṣaṇam 11arvantaḥ araṇavanto'śvāḥ |
12āśavaḥ śīghragāmino yanti |

1. na-prātaranu ( vāka ) nyāyeneti ca |
ābhipluvikeṣvatheṣu tṛtīyasavane maitrāvaruṇasya agni taṃ manye ityādiko vaikalpiko'nurūpastṛcaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca-agni taṃ manye yo vasurā te vatso mano yamaditi |
mahāpitṛyajñe gārhapatyopasthāne agni taṃ manye iti |
tathā ca sūtritaṃ-gārhapatyamagni taṃ manye iti |
āśvamedhike madhyame'hani idamevājyaśastram |
tasya viśeṣān vakṣyāma iti khaṇḍe sūtritaṃ ca-agni taṃ manye idamajyamiti |
2. kha-ga- gha-ṅa-ca-pa-pha-bha-vājino nityapravṛttahavirlakṣaṇāśvavantaḥ; na-la-nityapravṛtta ( la-tti ) havirlakṣaṇāśvavantaḥ ; sa-nityāḥ pravṛttahavirlakṣaṇāśvavantaḥ |
3. pa-sujātāsaḥ satkulotpannāḥ |
4. pa-medhāvinaśca yajamānādayaḥ |
agni̭ṃ vā॒jina̭ṃ vi॒śe da̭dāti vi॒śvaca̭rṣaṇiḥ | a॒gnī rāyḙ svā॒bhuva̭ṃ sa prī॒to yā̭ti॒ vārya̭mi॒ṣaṃ sto॒tṛbhya̭ ā॒ bhara̭ || 3 || a॒gniḥ | hi | vā॒ji॒nam | vi॒śe | da॒dā॒ti | vi॒śva'ca̭rṣaṇiḥ | a॒gniḥ | rāyḙ | su'ā̭bhuvam | saḥ | prī॒taḥ | yā॒ti | vārya̭m | i॒ṣam | sto॒tṛ'bhya̭ḥ | ā | bhara̭ || 3 ||
agniṃ ayamagniḥ 1 khalu viśe yajamānāya vājinam aśvavantaṃ putramaśvamaśvaṃ vā dadāti | viśvacarṣaṇiḥ | viśve gharṣaṇayo manuṣyā rakṣaṇīyā arcakā vā yasya sa tathoktaḥ | yadvā | paśyatikamaitata | sarvasya draṣṭā agniḥ | rāye dhanāyine | athavā dvitīyārthe caturthī | dhanaṃ svābhuvaṃ suṣṭhu sarvatra vyāptaṃ vāyaṃ sarvairviraṇīyaṃ prīta san yāti yamayati | dātuṃ vā gacchati | ipamityādi gatam ||
ābhipluvikeṣviṣu tṛtīyasavane stoniyatasya ‘ā te agna idhīmahi ’ ityādike prathamā dvitīye |

ā tḙ a॒gna i̭dhīmahi yu॒manta̭ṃ de॒vamaja̭ram | ya॒dya॒ syā tḙ pa॒rnīya̭sī sa॒middī̭dayati gha॒vīṣai̭ sto॒tṛbhya̭ ā॒ bhara̭ || 4 || ā | tḙ | a॒gne | i॒dhī॒ma॒hi | yu॒manta̭m | de॒vam | a॒ja॒ram | yat | ha॒ | syā | tḙ | pa॒rnīya̭sī | sa॒m'it | dī॒da॒ya॒ti | gha॒vīṣai̭ | i॒ṣam | sto॒tṛ'bhya̭ḥ | ā | bhara̭ || 4 ||
he agne deva dyumantaṃ dīptimantam ajaram ajīrṇaṃ te tvām ā sarvataḥ idhīmahi dīpayāmaḥ |

ā tḙ a॒gna ṛ॒cā ha॒viḥ śu॒krasya̭ śo॒ciṣa̭spate | su॒ścandra̭ da॒sma vi̭śvapate ha॒vyavā̭ṭ tu॒bhya̭ṃ hūya॒ta i॒ṣaṃ sto॒tṛbhya̭ ā॒ bhara̭ || 5 || ā | tḙ | a॒gne | ṛ॒cā | ha॒viḥ | śu॒krasya̭ | śo॒ciṣa̭ḥ | pa॒te | su॒ścandra̭ | da॒sma | vi॒śva॒pa॒te | ha॒vya'vā̭ṭ | tu॒bhya̭m | hū॒ya॒te | i॒ṣam | sto॒tṛ'bhya̭ḥ | ā | bhara̭ || 5 ||

prātaranuvāke
1. kha-ga-gha-ca-ṇa-pa-pha-bha- arcayantaḥ | 2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pa-pha-bha- 'dame...yojyaṃ' nāsti | 3. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pha-sūtritaṃ ca | 4. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-pha-bha-sa- sevasi | 5. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-kāle; sa-phāle | 6. gha-eva evam; ca-na-la-sa-evam | 7. ta-anuṣaktaṃ saṃbaddham | 8. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pa-pha-bha-anu- gacchanti | 9. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pa-pha-bha-ajayumaḥ | 10. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-bha-sa-'aju0...samāsaḥ' nāsti | 11. pa-ātreyasuśruta ṛṣiḥ |
āgneye kṛtāvānuṣṭubhe chandasyāśvinaśastre cottamavarjamidaṃ sūktam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘sakhāyaḥ saṃ vasvāmahe haviṣmanta iti sūkte’ (āśva. śrau. 4. 13) iti |
sakhāyaḥ saṃ vaḥ samyañcamiṣaṃ stomaṃ cāmayai | varṣiṣṭhāya kṣitīnāmūrjo naptre sahasvate || 1 || sakhāyaḥ | sam | vaḥ | samyañcam | iṣam | stomam | ca | agnaye | varṣiṣṭhāya | kṣitīnām | ūrjaḥ | naptre | sahasvate || 1 ||
he sakhāyaḥ sakhibhūtā ṛtvijaḥ vaḥ yūyaṃ saṃ saṃskuruta |
upasargaśruteryogakriyādhyāhāraḥ |
kiṃca samyañcam iṣam annaṃ stomaṃ stotraṃ cāmaye varṣiṣṭhāya atiśayena pravṛddhāya kṣitīnāṃ yajamānānāmarthāya varṣiṣṭhāya pravṛddhatamāya ūrjaḥ balasya naptre naptre balasya na pātayitre tatputrāya vā sahasvate balavate saṃskuruteti ||
kutrā cit yasya saṃmṛtī raṇvā narau nṛṣadane | arhantaścidyamindhate saṃjanayanti jantavaḥ || 2 || kutra | cit | yasya | sam'ṛtau | raṇvāḥ | naraḥ | nṛ'sadane | arhantaḥ | cit | yam | indhate | sam'janayanti | jantavaḥ || 2 ||
so'gniḥ kutra cit vartate iti śeṣaḥ |
kutrāste |
yasya agneḥ saṃmṛtī saṃprāptau raṇvāḥ ramamāṇāḥ naraḥ netāra ṛtvijaḥ |
nṛṣadane yāgagṛhe arhantaścit |
ciditi pūjāyām |
arhantaḥ pūjyāḥ pūjayanto vā santaḥ yamindhate dīpayanti |
tadarthaṃ saṃjanayanti ca araṇyāṃ jantavaḥ prāṇinaḥ |
sa kutreti sambandhaḥ ||
saṃ yadiṣo varnāmahe saṃ havyā mānuṣāṇām | uta dyumnasya śavasa ṛtasya raśmimā dade || 3 || sam | yat | iṣaḥ | varnāmahe | sam | havyā | mānuṣāṇām | uta | dyumnasya | śavasā | ṛtasya | raśmim | ā | dade || 3 ||
yat yadā iṣaḥ annāni saṃ vanāmahe saṃjāmahe |
yadā ca mānuṣāṇāṃ manuṣyāṇām asmākaṃ havyā havīṃṣi agniḥ saṃ sevate uta tadānīṃ dyumnasya dyotamānasyāsya śavasā balena
1. na-la-sa- ‘saṃ’ nāsti |
2. na-la-sa- ‘manuṣyāṇāṃ’ nāsti |
ṛ. 2-94
sāmadheyana ᵛṛtasya udakasya grāhakaṃ ᵛraśmimā ᵛdade ādatte'gniḥ |
phalajanakaṃ¹ tejaḥ¹ svīkṛtavān ityarthaḥ |
yadvā |
ᵛṛtasya yajñasya raśmi raśmivat grāhakaṃ stavam ā dade svīkaroti² ||
sa smā kṛṇoti ketumā naktaṃ cid dūra ā sute | pāvako yadvanaspatīnpra smā minātyajaraḥ || 4 || saḥ | sma | kṛṇoti | ketum | ā | naktam | cit | dūre | ā | sute | pāvakaḥ | yat | vanaspatīn | pra | sma | mināti | ajaraḥ || 4 ||
ᵛsa ᵛsma sa khalvagniḥ ᵛkṛṇoti karoti ᵛketuṃ prajñānam ᵛā sarvataḥ |
kasyetyucyate |
cida- pyarthe |
ᵛnaktaṃ ᵛcit rātrāvapi ᵛdūre ᵛā ᵛsate |
ā iti cārthe |
dūre'pi vartamānāya manuṣyāya |
kadā |
ᵛyat yadā ᵛpāvakaḥ ayamagniḥ ᵛajaraḥ ajīrṇo'nabhibhāvyaḥ san ᵛvanaspatīn ḷdhāṃsi ᵛpra ᵛmbha ᵛmināti prakarṣeṇa dahati |
smeti pūraṇaḥ ||
ava sma yasya veṣaṇe svedaṃ pṛthisu juhvati | abhīmaha svajainyaṃ bhūmā pṛṣṭherva ruruhuḥ || 5 || ava | sma | yasya | veṣaṇe | svedam | pṛthisu | juhvati | abhi | īm | aha | svajainyam | bhūma | pṛṣṭhā'iva | ruruhuḥ || 5 ||
ᵛyasya³ agneḥ ᵛveṣaṇe paricaryāyāṃ ᵛpṛthisu homamārgeṣu ᵛraśmipu ᵛsvedaṃ svadājyam⁴ ᵛava adhomukhaṃ ᵛjuhvati adhvaryavaḥ⁵ tadānīm ᵛīm enamagnim ᵛabhi ᵛruruhuḥ ᵛaha |
abhyārohantyevājya- dhārāḥ |
aha iti vinigrahārthīyaḥ |
ᵛsvajainyaṃ svorūpaṃ ᵛbhūma |
bahu⁶ bhavatīti bhūmāpatyam |
tata ᵛpṛṣṭheva pituḥ pṛṣṭhadeśāniva⁷ |
putrāḥ⁸⁹ yathā pituraṅkam ārohanti¹¹ tadvata ||

yaṃ martyaiḥ puruspṛhaṃ vidadviśvasya dhāyase | pra svārdanaṃ pitūnāmastārtāti cidāyavai || 6 || yam | martyaiḥ | puru'spṛham | vidat | viśvasya | dhāyase | pra | svārdanam | pitūnām | asta'tātim | cit | āyavai || 6 ||
1. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pha- phalajanakatvaṃ na; na-phalajanakatvam ; bha-phalajanakatvena | 2. ga-ca-ta-na-bha-sa- svīkaromi | 3. kha-ga-ṇa-ta-bha-sa-asya | 4. ta-homāgniṣu | 5. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-svadājyamīmenam | 6. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pha-bha-adhvaryavaḥ | dṛṣṭāntaḥ | 7. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-la-sa- 'īm' nāsti | 8. kha-ga-ca- ṇa-na-bha- 'bahu' nāsti | 9. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-pṛṣṭhadeśe | 10. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-putraḥ | 11. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha- ārohati |
^yam agni ^puruspṛhaṃ bahubhiḥ spṛhaṇīyaṃ ^vidat vetti |
^martyaḥ yajamānaḥ ^viśvasya ^dhāyase sarvasya vyāpārārtha ^pitṝnām ajñānāṃ ^pra ^svādanena prakarṣeṇa svādvakartāram ^astatātim aste gṛhe nivāsakartāram ^āyave annātha ^yajamānāya vā |
^cit ^pūraṇaḥ ^||
sa hi ṣmā dhanvākṣitaṃ dātā na dātyā paśuḥ |
hiriśmaśruḥ śucidanbhuranibhṛṣṭataviṣiḥ ||
7 ||
saḥ | hi | sma | dhanva | ākṣitam | dātā | na | dāti | ā | paśuḥ | hiri'śmaśruḥ | śuci'dan | ṛbhuḥ | anibhṛṣṭa'taviṣiḥ || 7 ||
^sa ^hi ^sma ^khalvagniḥ ^dhanva nirudakapradeśam ^ākṣitaṃ tṛṇāṣṭhādibhirākṣitam ^ā ^dāti sarvataḥ khaṇḍayati dahatītyarthaḥ |
^dātā tṛṇādikhaṇḍayitā ^paśuḥ ^na paśuriva |
paśaryathā tṛṇajātaṃ krameṇa bhakṣayati tadvat |
^hiriśmaśruḥ hiraṇyaśmaśruḥ ^śucidan dīptadantaḥ ^ṛbhuḥ ^uru bhavana |
mahānityarthaḥ ^|
^anibhṛṣṭataviṣiḥ apīḍitabalaḥ ||
śuciḥ ṣmā yasmā atrivatsvadhitīva rīyate |
muṣūrasūta mātā krāṇā yadānaśe bhagam ||
8 ||
śuciḥ | sma | yasmai | atri'vat | pra | svadhitiḥ'iva | rīyate | su'sūḥ | asūta | mātā | krāṇā | yat | ānaśe | bhagam || 8 ||
ayamagniḥ ^śuciḥ ^ṣma dyotaḥ khalu |

smeti pūraṇaḥ |
^śuciyasmai ^prathamārthe caturthī |
^dyoto yo'trivat |
atti tṛṇamityatriḥ ^paśuḥ |
sa iva |

^svadhitiva paraśuriva chindan |
^susūḥ suprasavā ^mātā araṇiḥ ^asūt ^krāṇā kurvāṇaṃ jagadupakārakam ^|

yo'gniḥ ^bhagam annam ^ānaśe aśnute |

9 ||
ā | yaḥ | te | sarpiḥ'āsute | agne | śam | asti | dhāryase | ā | eṣu | dyumnam | uta | śravaḥ | ā | cittam | martyeṣu | dhāḥ || 9 ||
1. ta-spṛhaṇīyamārmam | 2. na-la-sa-vyāpyartham | 3. sa-mitrārtham | 4. na-la-sa-'cit pūraṇaḥ ' nāsti | 5. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-sma | 6. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pa-pha-ma-ṛbhurmahāna | 7. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pha-ma- atra; pa-annam | 8. ta-jagadupakāram |
he ᵛsarpirāsute ghṛtāsyānna ᵛagne ᵛyaḥ tvam ᵛā sarvato bhavasi tādṛśāya ᵛdhāyase sarvasya dhātre ᵛte tubhyaṃ ᵛśamasti sukhamasti |
stuteḥ sakāśāt |
tathā cet ᵛeṣu ᵛmartyeṣu stotṛṣvātreṣu ᵛśughnaṃ yaśaḥ ᵛā ᵛdhāḥ ādhehi |

iti | cit | manyum | adhirjaḥ | tvā'dāt | ā | paśum | dade | āt | agne | arpaṇataḥ | atriḥ | sāsahāt | dasyūna | iṣaḥ | sāsahāt | nṛn || 10 ||
ᵛiti ᵛcit itthaṃ kṛtaprakāreṇa ᵛmanyuṃ manasādhanaṃ stotram ᵛadhirjaḥ |
aṣṭam ᵛagneradhi- vyatiriṃktaḥ ᵛadhyavyaṃ ᵛvā janayitā ṛṣiḥ ᵛtvādātaṃ tvayā dātavyaṃ ᵛpaśum ᵛā ᵛdade ādatte |
ādadīta vā |

punaruktirādarārthā ||
25 ||
‘svāmagne’ iti sasacamaṣṭamaṃ sūktamātrasya ipsya ᵛārṣaṃ jāgatamāgneyam |
‘svāmagne matta jāgatam’ ityanukramaṇikā |
prātaranuvāke āgneye ṛtau jāgate chandasyāśvinaśastre cedaṃ sūktam |
tathā dha sūtraṃ—‘ janasya gopāsvāmama ṛtāyavaḥ ’ ( āśva. śrau. 4. 13 ) iti ||
tvāmagna ṛtāyavaḥ samīdhire pratnaṃ pratnāsa ūtaye sahaskṛta |
puruścandraṃ yajataṃ viśvadhāyasaṃ dmūnasaṃ gṛhapatiṃ vareṇyam ||
1 ||
tvām | agne | ṛta'yavaḥ | sam | īdhire | pratnam | pratnāsaḥ | ūtaye | sahaḥ'kṛta | puru'candram | yajatam | viśva'dhāyasam | dmūnasam | gṛha'patim | vareṇyam || 1 ||
he ᵛsahaskṛta balasya kartaḥ ᵛagne ᵛpratnaṃ purātanaṃ ᵛtvāṃ ᵛpratnāsaḥ purātanāḥ ᵛṛtāyavaḥ yajñakāmā ṛṣayaḥ ᵛūtaye svarakṣaṇāya ᵛsamīdhire samyagdīpitavantaḥ |
kīdṛśaṃ tvām |
ᵛpuruścandraṃ bahudhanam ani- śayenāhlādakaṃ vā ᵛyajataṃ yaṣṭavyaṃ ᵛviśvadhāyasaṃ bahujñaṃ ᵛdamūnasaṃ dānamanasaṃ ᵛgṛhapatiṃ yajamānagṛhasya pālakaṃ ᵛvareṇyaṃ varaṇīyam ||
1. pha-ghṛtāktānna |
2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pa-pha-bha-he agne iti |
3. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pha- na dhṛtam |
4. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pha-adhirjaḥ adhryaḥ; ta-na-la-sa-adhirjaḥ adhryam; bha-adhirjaḥ adhrya |
5. ta- adata |
6. kha-ga-va-ṇa-ta-pa-pha-bha-dveṣṭan |
7. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha- ‘ iṣasya ’ nāsti; sa-vasuśratasya |
tvāmagne atithiṃ pṛ॒thvyaṃ vi॒śaḥ śo॒ciṣkḙśaṃ gṛ॒hapa̭tiṃ ni॒ ṣedire |
bṛha॒tketuṃ purṷrūpaṃ dha॒nuspṛ̭taṃ su॒śarmāṇaṃ jara̭dviṣam ||
2 ||
tvām | agne | atithim | pṛ॒thvyam | vi॒śaḥ | śo॒ciḥ'kḙśam | gṛ॒ha'pa̭tim | ni | sedire | bṛhat'kḙtum | purṷ'rūpam | dha॒nuḥ'spṛ̭tam | su॒'śarmāṇam | jarat'vi̭ṣam ||2||
he agne tvāṃ viśaḥ yajamānāḥ gṛhapatiṃ gṛhasvāminaṃ ni ṣedire |
gārhapatyarūpeṇa sthāpitavanta ityarthaḥ |
kīdṛśaṃ tvām |
atithim atithisvarūpaṃ pūrvyaṃ purātanaṃ śociṣkeśaṃ dīptajālaṃ bṛhatketuṃ prabhūtaprajñānaṃ pururūpam āhavanīyādirūpeṇa bahurūpaṃ dhanaspṛtaṃ dhanānāṃ spartāraṃ suśarmāṇaṃ śobhanasukhaṃ svavasaṃ surakṣaṇaṃ jaradviṣaṃ jaratāṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ vyāpakaṃ jīrṇodakaṃ vā ||
agnīnāṃ parasparaṃ saṃsargaṃ vivice'gne kāciddiṣṭaḥ |
tatra 'svāmagne mānuṣīḥ' iti yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'svāmagne mānuṣīrīḻate viśo'ma āyāhi vītaye' (āśva. śrau. 3. 13 ) iti ||
tvāmagne mānuṣīrīḻate viśo॒ hotā̭vidaṃ vi॒viciṃ ra॒tnadhā̭tamam |
guhā॒ santaṃ su॒bhagaṃ vi॒śvada̭rśataṃ tu॒vidyṷmanaṃ su॒yaja̭ṃ ghṛ॒taśri̭yam ||
3 ||
tvām | agne | mānuṣīḥ | īḻate | viśaḥ | hotā̭'vidam | vi॒vici̭m | ra॒tna'dhā̭tamam | guhā | santam | su॒'bhagam | vi॒śva'da̭rśatam | tu॒vi'dyṷmanam | su॒'yaja̭m | ghṛ॒ta'śri̭yam || 3 ||
he agne subhaga śobhanadhana tvāṃ mānuṣīḥ viśaḥ mānuṣasaṃbandhinyaḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ īḻate stuvanti |
kīdṛśaṃ tvām |
hotāvidaṃ hotrāṇāṃ homānāṃ sahahotrāṇāṃ vā vettāraṃ viviciṃ vivecakaṃ sadamatoḥ ratnadhātamaṃ ramaṇīyānāṃ dhanānāṃ dātṛttamaṃ guhā guhāyāmaraṇyāṃ hṛdayeṣu vā santaṃ sarvadā vartamānaṃ viśvadarśataṃ sarvaiḥ draṣṭavyaṃ tuvidyumanaṃ prabhūtadhvaniṃ suyajaṃ suṣṭhu yaṣṭāraṃ ghṛtaśriyaṃ ghṛtaṃ śrayantam ||
tvāmagne dharṇasiṃ vi॒śvadhā̭ va॒yaṃ gī॒rbhirgṛ̭ṇanto॒ nama̭sopa sedima |
sa nau juṣasva sa॒midhā̭no a॒ṅgiro̭ de॒vo ma̭rtyasya॒ yaśa̭sā sudītibhiḥ ||
4 ||
tvām | agne | dharṇasim | vi॒śva'dhā̭ | va॒yam | gīḥ'bhiḥ | gṛ̭ṇantaḥ | nama̭sā | upa | sedima | saḥ | naḥ | ju॒ṣasva̭ | sa॒midha̭ānaḥ | a॒ṅgiraḥ | de॒vaḥ | ma॒rtyasya | yaśa̭sā | su॒dīti̭'bhiḥ || 4 ||
he agne dharṇasiṃ sarvasya dhārakaṃ tvāṃ vayam ātreyā vā viśvadhā bahuprakāreṇa gīrbhirgṛṇantaḥ stuvantaḥ namasā namaskāreṇa upa sedima upasannā bhavema |
saḥ tvaṃ naḥ asmān juṣasva sevasva
1. pa-prabhūtaprajñam | 2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pha-saṃsparśitāram; gha-pa-bha-saṃparkitāram | 3. kha-ga-ca-ṇa- pa-pha-bha-rakṣasām; na-sa-dakṣāṇām; la-rakṣaṇām | 4. ta-parasparasaṃsarge | 5. gha-na-la-sa-'hotrāṇāṃ' nāsti | 6. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pha-bha-hṛdaye | 7. ta-viśvaiḥ | 8. pa-'vā' nāsti |
dhanādibhiḥ |
ᐞsamidhānaḥ samyagdīpto dīpyamāno'smābhiᐞaṅgiraḥ sarvatra gantaraṅgirasaḥ putra vāᐞdevaḥ dīpyamānastvaṃ ᐞmarttasya yajamānasya ᐞyaśasā ᐞsudīptibhiḥ jvālābhiḥ saha jupasva ||
tvamagne puru rūpo viśeviśe vayo dadhāsi pratnathā puruṣṭu | puruṇyannā sahasā vi rājasi tviṣiḥ sā te tivipāṇasya nādhṛṣe || 5 || tvam | agne | puru'rūpaḥ | viśe'viśe | vayaḥ | dadhāsi | pratna'thā | puru'stuta | purūṇi | annā | sahasā | vi | rājasi | tviṣiḥ | sā | te | tivipāṇasya | na | ā'dhṛṣe || 5 ||
ha ᐞagne ᐞtvaṃ ᐞpururūpaḥ man ᐞviśeviśe sarvasmai ᐞyajamānāya ᐞpratnathā purāṇa iva ᐞvayaḥ annaṃ ᐞdadhāsi dhārayasi |
ha ᐞpuruṣṭuta bahubhiḥ stuta ᐞpurūṇi bahūni ᐞannā ajñāni haviṣpuroḍāśādīni ᐞsahasā balena ᐞvi ᐞrājasi īśvaro bhavasi svīkartum |
ᐞtivipāṇasya dāsya ᐞte tava ᐞsā prasiddhā ᐞtviṣiḥ dīptiḥ ᐞnādhṛṣe anyairadhṛṣyā bhavati ||
tvamagne samidhānaṃ yaviṣṭhya devā dūtaṃ cakrire havyavāhanam | urujrayasaṃ ghṛtayonimāhutaṃ tveṣaṃ cakṣurdhire codayanmati || 6 || tvām | agne | sam'idhānam | yaviṣṭhya | devāḥ | dūtam | cakrire | havya'vāhanam | uru'jrayasam | ghṛta'yonim | ā'hutam | tveṣam | cakṣuḥ | dadhire | codayat'mati || 6 ||
ha ᐞyaviṣṭhya yuvatama ᐞagne ᐞsamidhānaṃ samyagdhidhyamānaṃ ᐞtvāṃ ᐞhavyavāhanaṃ haviṣāṃ voḍhāraṃ ᐞdūtaṃ ᐞcakrire kṛtavantaḥ ᐞdevāḥ |
trayāṇāmagnīnāṃ madhye havyavāhanaḥ khalu pūrvaṃ devairvirvāhanāya dūtaḥ kṛtaḥ |
‘havyavāhano ᐞdevānām ’ ( tai. saṃ. 2. 5. 8. 6 ) iti hi śrutiḥ |
kiṃca ᐞurujrayasaṃ prabhūtavegaṃ ᐞghṛtayonim |
ghṛtaṃ ᐞyoniḥ kāraṇaṃ yasya tam |
ᐞāhutaṃ ᐞtvāṃ ᐞcodayanmati |
codayantī matiryasya taccodayanmati |
ᐞtveṣaṃ dīptaṃ ᐞcakṣuḥ ᐞutlakṣaṇaṃ ᐞsarvaprakāśakaṃ cakṣuḥsthānīyaṃ kṛtvā ᐞdadhire devā manuṣyāśca |
matyā hi cakṣuśrodhate ||
tvamagne pradiva āhutaṃ ghṛtaiḥ sumnāyaveḥ susamidhā samīdhire | sa vāvṛdhāna oṣadhībhirukṣito'bhi jyāṃsī pārthivā vi tiṣṭhase || 7 || tvām | agne | pra'divaḥ | ā'hutam | ghṛtaiḥ | sumna'yaveḥ | su'samidhā | sam | īdhire | saḥ | vavṛdhānaḥ | oṣadhībhiḥ | ukṣitaḥ | abhi | jyāṃsī | pārthivā | vi | tiṣṭhase || 7 ||
1. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-na-pa-pha-bha- ‘vā ’ nāsti | 2. na-la-sa-stutya | 3. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pha- devāḥ havyavāham ; gha-ta-na-pa-bha-la-sa-devāḥ havyavāhanam | 4. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-pha-bha-havyavāhaḥ | 5. ta-ghṛtameva | 6. ta-āhutaṃ sarvataḥ | 7. kha-pa-pha-bha- ‘cakṣurutlakṣaṇaṃ ’ nāsti |
he ^agne ^ghṛtaiḥ ^āhutaṃ ^tvāṃ ^pradivaḥ purātanāḥ ^sumnāyavaḥ sukhamicchanto yajamānāḥ ^supamidhā śobhanena idhmena ^samīdhire samyak dīpitavantaḥ |
^saḥ ^tvaṃ ^vāvṛdhānaḥ vardhamānaḥ ^oṣadhībhiḥ ^ukṣitaḥ siktaḥ ^jyāṃsi annāni ^pārthivā pārthivān vṛkṣān |
yadvā |
pārthiveti jrayoviśeṣaṇam |
pārthivāni carupuroḍāśādikāni ^abhi ^vi ^tiṣṭhase abhivyāpya vartase ||
26 ||
vedārthasya prakāśena tamo hārdaṃ nivārayan |
pumarthāṃścaturo deyāddvidyātīrthamaheśvaraḥ ||
iti śrīmadrājādhirājaparameśvaravaidikamārgapravartakaśrīvīrabukabhūpālasāmrājyadhuraṃdhareṇa sāyaṇācāryeṇa viracite mādhavīye vedārthaprakāśe ṛksaṃhitābhāṣye tṛtīyāṣṭake'ṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ ||
1. kha-ga-bha-prātanāḥ; gha-pa-la-purāṇāḥ; ca-pha
|| śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ ||
vāgīśādyāḥ¹ sumanasaḥ sarvārthānāmupakrame |
yaṃ natvā kṛtakṛtyāḥ syustaṃ namāmi gajānanam² ||
yasya³ niḥśvasitaṃ vedā yo vedebhyo'khilaṃ jagat |
nirmame tamahaṃ vande vidyātīrthamaheśvaram⁴ ||
pañcame maṇḍale prathame'nuvāke pūrvamaṣṭau sūktāni vyākhyātāni |

agnidevatā |

he 7agne 8tvā 9devaṃ dīpyamānaṃ 7haviṣmantaḥ homasādhanadravyarūmetāḥ 7martāsaḥ martyāḥ 7īḻate stuvanti | ahaṃ ca 7jātavedasam | jātamutpannaṃ carācaraṃ bhūtajātaṃ vettīti jātavedāḥ | athavā jātāni sthāvarajaṅgamātmakāni enaṃ viduriti jātavedāḥ | yadvā | veda iti dhananāma | jātaṃ sarvaṃ vedo dhanaṃ yasyāsau jātavedāḥ | evaṃvidhaṃ 10 tvā tvāṃ 7manye staumi | maniratra stutyarthe vartate dhātunāmekārtha- tvāt | 7saḥ tvaṃ 11 havyā havanasādhanāni 12 havīṃṣi 7ānuṣak nirantaratayā anantaratayā anuṣaktaṃ yathā tathā 7vakṣi vahasi || haviṣmantaḥ | haviḥśabdaḥ auṇādikahaspratyayāntaḥ | pratyayasvareṇāntodāttaḥ | ‘tadasyāsti’ iti matup | padasaṃjñāyā apavādātvena ‘tasau matvarthe’ iti bhasaṃjñāyāṃ rutvābhāvaḥ | matubjasoranu- dāttatvāt pratyayasvara eva śiṣyate | devam 12 | acapratyayāntatvāt devaśabdo'ntodāttaḥ | martāsaḥ 14 | ‘mṛṅ prāṇatyāge’ | ‘asihasimṛṅ’ ityādinā tan | guṇaḥ | marteḥ | jaserasuk | martāsaḥ | nirudāttādyudāttaḥ | īḻate 15 | ‘īḍa stutau’ | adāditvācchapāṃ luk | ‘ātmanepadeṣvanataḥ’ | ṭerevam |

yam agniṃ yajñāsaḥ sarve yajñāḥ saṃ caranti sahitā gacchanti | śruvasvaḥ yajamānasya prabhūtāṃ kīrtiṃ saṃpādayantaḥ vājāsaḥ havīṃṣi yamagniṃ saṃ caranti prāpnuvanti | evaṃviśiṣṭaḥ agniḥ dāsvataḥ haviṣprakṣepaṇādidānavataḥ vṛktabarhiṣaḥ | vṛktāni lūnāni bahūni darbhā yenāsau vṛktabarhiḥ | tasya kṣayasya | kṣiyanti nivasantyasmin svargasādhanaphalānīti kṣayaḥ | tasya yajamānasya yāgārthaṃ hotā devānāmāhvātā bhavati || dāsvataḥ | ' dā dāne ' | asunpratyayāntaḥ | matupaḥ ādyudāttatvāt vatvam | dhātusvareṇādyudātta eva | kṣayasya | ' kṣi nivāsagatyoḥ ' | ' kṣayo nivāse ' ityādyudāttatvam | vṛktabarhiṣaḥ | ' vṛjī varjane ' | varjanam atropacārāt chedanam | niṣṭhā | pratyayasvareṇāntodāttaḥ | bahuvrīhitvāt pūrvapadaprakṛtisvaratvam | yajñāsaḥ | yajñaśabdaḥ ' yajayāca ' ityādinā naḍpratyayāntaḥ antodāttaḥ | caranti | ' cara gatibhakṣaṇayoḥ ' | adupadeśalasārvadhātukānudāttatvam | ' yadvṛttātkriyām ' iti nighātābhāvaḥ | vājāsaḥ | vajerdhañ | vājaḥ | ' karṣāvataḥ ' ityantodāttatvam | chāndasatvāt

⁷uta ⁷sma api ca ⁷mānuṣīṇāṃ manojātānāṃ ⁷viśām | viśanti praviśanti garbhāśayamiti viśaḥ prajāḥ | tāsāṃ ⁷dhartāram āhārādipākadvāreṇa śarīradhātūnāṃ poṣakaṃ ⁷svadhvaraṃ śobhanayajñaṃ ⁷yam ⁷agnim ⁷araṇī dve araṇī ⁷navaṃ nūtanaṃ ⁷śiśuṃ ⁷yathā apatyamiva ⁷janiṣṭa ajanipātām | araṇī prajānāṃ popaṇārthaṃ caitamagnim ⁷asūjatāmityarthaḥ || śiśum | ‘śo tanūkaraṇe’ | ‘ādecaḥ’ ityādinā ātvam | ‘śaḥ kin sancca’ ityupratyayaḥ | sanjbhāvaśca | dvirbhāvo'bhyāsasya hrasvaśca | ‘sanyataḥ’ itītvam | ‘bhāto dhātoḥ’ ityākāralopaḥ | nintvādādyudāttaḥ | yathā | ‘yatheti pādānta’ iti sarvānudāttaḥ | janiṣṭa | ‘janī prādurbhāve’ | luṅ | chāndastvād‌vacanavyatyayaḥ | araṇī | ‘ṛ gatau’ | ‘atisṛbhṛñ- dhmyasyavitṛbhyo'niḥ’ iti anipratyayaḥ | ādyudāttaḥ | ābhyārmāti gacchatyagnirityaraṇī | kathamapādānārthe auṇādiko'niḥ | ‘tābhyāmanyatronādayaḥ’ (pā. sū. 3. 4. 75) iti niyamāt bhīmāditvāt siddham | ⁷mānuṣīṇām | manojātāvayavato vukca iti anapratyayaḥ pugāgamaśca | ‘dityadi- nityam’ ityādyudāttatvam | viśām | ‘viśa praveśane’ | kartari kip | ‘sāvekācaḥ’ iti vibhakteḥ udāttatvam | svadhvaram | hiṃsārthasya dhvarateḥ pacādyac | na vidyate dhvaro yāgahiṃsā yasminnityadhvaraḥ | bahuvrīhisarupavādatvena ‘nansubhyām’ ityantodāttaḥ ||
uta sma durgabhīyase putro na dārvāṇām |
puru yo dagdhāsi vanāyai paśurna yarvase ||

^adha ^sma api ca ^dhūmine: dhūmavata: ^yasya agne: ^arcaya: arcīṣi ^samyakśobhanaṃ ^saṃyanti sarvata: prāpnuvanti ^trita: tripu sthāneṣu tato vyāpta: trīṇisthānāni vā atītya ^divi antarikṣe ^yat yadā ^īm ayamāramānam ^upa ^dhamati upavardhayati^3 tadā jvālā: sarvato nirgacchantītyartha: |
kathamiva |
^dhamātā'iva^4 karmāro yathā dhmātrādibhiragniṃ saṃvardhayati tadvatsvātmānaṃ vardhayatītyartha: |
kiṃca ^dhmātari ^yathā |

he agne sahasvaḥ balavan naraḥ haviṣāṃ netā tvam asmākaṃ taṃ rayiṃ loke prasiddhaṃ pathādi- lakṣaṇaṃ dhanam abhi ā bhara ābhimukhyenāhara | pūrvoktaḥ saḥ agniḥ kṣepayat | asmadīyān śatrūn kṣepayatu | saḥ agniḥ poṣayat asmān poṣayatu | vājasya annasya sātaye lābhāya bhuvat bhavatu | uta api ca he agne pṛtsu pṛtanāsu yuddheṣu naḥ asmākaṃ vṛdhe samṛddhaye gṛdhi bhava | saṃgrāme śatruvijayārthaṃ sa tvaṃ samṛddhiṃ kurvityarthaḥ || sahasvaḥ | 'paha marṣaṇe ' | asun | sahasyanena śatrūniti saho balam | matup | 'mādupadhāyāḥ ' iti vatvam | 'matuvaso ruḥ saṃbuddhau chandasi ' iti rutvam | 'āmantritasya ca ' iti nighātaḥ | kṣepayat | 'kṣipa preraṇe ' | ṇijantāt leṭ | itaśca lopaḥ | 'leṭo'ḍāṭau ' ityaḍāgamaḥ | śap guṇāyādeśau pararūpatvaṃ ca | poṣayat | 'puṣa puṣṭau ' | bhuvat | 'bhū sattāyām ' | 'bhūsuvostiṅi ' iti guṇapratiṣedhaḥ | uvaṅ | edhi | 'asa bhuvi ' | dhvasore- dvābhyāsalopaśca ' iti evam | tasyāsiddhatvāt 'hujhalbhyo hedhiḥ ' iti dhirādeśaḥ | nighātaḥ | pṛtsu | 'padādiṣu ' ityādinā pṛtanāśabdasya pṛdādeśaḥ | sāvekācatvāt antodāttatvam || || 1 ||
'agna ojiṣṭham ' iti saptacaṃ daśamaṃ sūktam |
ṛṣyādi viniyogaśca pūrvavat |
caturthīsaptamyau paṅktī |
'agna ojiṣṭhamanyācaturthī ca ' ityanukrāntam ||
1. pa-pha-ba-ahaṃ turyāma tareyam | 2. ra-antodāttatve; śa-antodāttaḥ | 3. śa-udāttatvam | 4. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-śa-netāraḥ | 5. kha-ca-ṇa-sānekāca iti | 6. pa-pha-ba-ātreyo gaya ṛṣiḥ | caturthīsaptamyau paṅktī | śiṣṭā anuṣṭubhaḥ | agnidevatā |
a॒m oji̭ṣṭhaṃ bhara॒ dyumna̭ma॒smabhya̭ṃ dhri॒go | pra naṷ rā॒yā parī̭ṇasā ra॒tsi vā॒jāya॒ panthā̭m || 1 || a॒me | oji̭ṣṭham | ā | bha॒ra | dyumna̭m | a॒smabhya̭m | a॒dhi॒go | i॒tyadhi̭go | pra | na̭ḥ | rā॒yā | parī̭ṇasā | ra॒tsi | vā॒jāya̭ | panthā̭m || 1 ||
he agne ojiṣṭhaṃ balavattamaṃ dyumnaṃ dyotate kaṭakamukuṭādirūpeṇa sarvatra prakāśate iti dyumnaṃ dhanam |
asmabhyam ā bhara āhara |

˅vṛṇānāḥ saṃbhajamānāḥ santaḥ ˅kavikratum krāntayajñam ˅agnim ˅vṛṇate saṃbhajante ||
sādhuyā |
‘supāṃ suluk ’ ityādinā vibhakteryādeśaḥ ||
1. ca-˅nimittāt | 2. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-agnihotrārtham | 3. kha-va-bha-ṇa-na-bha-ra- vāsavataḥ | 4. kha-va-jha-ṇa-bha-ra-śa-vin | 5. ta-bāhulakāt |

ṛgveda:
tubhyedaṃmagne madhumattamaṃ vacastubhyaṃ manīṣā iyamastu śaṃ hṛde |
tvāṃ gira: sindhumivānanīrmahīrā pṛṇanti śavasā vardhayanti ca ||
5 ||
tubhya | idam | agne | madhumattamam | vaca: | tubhyam | manīṣā | iyam | astu | śam | hṛde | tvām | gira: | sindhum'iva | avanī: | mahī: | ā | pṛṇanti | śavasā | vardhayanti | ca ||5||
he agne tubhya tubhyaṃ tvadarthaṃ madhumattamam atyantaṃ madhuram hṛdaṃ vaca: kriyate |
hṛde hṛdi śaṃ sukhaṃ kurvantī iyaṃ manīṣā stuti: tubhyam astu |
gira: stutirūpā vāca: tvām ā pṛṇanti pūrayanti |
śavasā balena vardhayanti ca |
kathamiba |
mahī: mahya: avanī: avantya: natha: sindhumiva samudramiva ||
tvāṃmagne aṅgiraso guhā hitamanvavindañchiśriyāṇaṃ vanevane |
sa jāyase madhyamāna: saho mahatvāmāhu: sahasaputramaṅgira: ||
6 ||
tvām | agne | aṅgirasa: | guhā | hitam | anu | avindan | śiśriyāṇam | vane'vane | sa: | jāyase | madhyamāna: | saha: | mahat | tvām | āhu: | sahasa: | putram | aṅgira: || 6 ||
he agne aṅgirasa: ṛṣaya: guhā guhāyāṃ hitaṃ nihitaṃ nigūḍhaṃ vanevane vṛkṣavṛkṣe śiśriyāṇam āśritaṃ tvām anvavindan alabhanta | mahata mahatā saha: sahasā balena yukta: sa: tvaṃ madhyamāna: jāyase | he aṅgira: aṅgirasāṃ prakṛtibhūta tvāṃ sahasa: putram āhu: || śiśriyāṇam | ' śriñ sevāyām ' | liṭa: kānac | śapa: bhlu: | ' śrau ' iti dvivacanam | iyaḍādeśa: | cittvādantodātta: || || 3 ||
' prāgnaye ṛtate ' iti ṣaḍṛcaṃ dvādaśaṃ sūktaṃ sutabharasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamāgneyam |
' prāgnaye ' ityanu- krāntam |
prātaranuvāke āmeye ṛtau traiṣṭubhe chandamyāśvinaśastre cedaṃ sūktaṃ viniyuktam |
sutritaṃ ca- ' prāgnaye bṛhate pra ve'me kavaye ' ( āśva. śrau. 4. 13 ) iti ||
prāgnayai bṛhate yajñiyāya ṛtasya vṛṣṇe asurāya manmaṃ |
ghṛtaṃ na yajña āsye supūrtaṃ giraṃ bhare vṛṣabhāya pratīcīm ||
1 ||
prā | agnaye | bṛhate | yajñiyāya | ṛtasya | vṛṣṇe | asurāya | manma | ghṛtam | na | yajñe | āsye | su'pūtam | giram | bhare | vṛṣabhāya | pratīcīm || 1 ||
1. na-iti prātaranuvākyāyeti ca |
ᵛbṛhate sāmarthyātiśayena mahate ᵛyajñiyāya yajñārhāya ᵛṛtasya udakasya ᵛvṛṣṇe varṣitre |
ṛtamimyudakanāmasu pāṭhāt |
ᵛasurāya balavate ||
aserauṇādika uranpratyayaḥ ||
ᵛvṛṣabhāya kāmānāṃ varṣitre ᵛagnaye ᵛmanma jñānasādhanaṃ ᵛpratīcīm abhimukhīṃ ᵛgiraṃ stutirūpāṃ vācaṃ ᵛpra ᵛbhare praṇayāmi |
kathamiva |
ᵛsupūtaṃ samyaksaṃskṛtam ᵛāsye prahutaṃ ᵛyajñe ᵛghṛtaṃ ᵛna ghṛtamiva |
āsye prahutaṃ ghṛtaṃ yathā prītikaraṃ bhavati tathā prītikarīṃ stutimityarthaḥ ||
ṛtaṃ cikitva ṛtamiccikiddhṛtasya dhārā anṷ tṛndhi pūrvīḥ |
nāhaṃ yātuṃ saha̭sā na dvayena̭ ṛtaṃ sa̭ṃpāmyuruṣasya vṛṣṇaḥ ||
2 ||
ṛtam | cikitvaḥ | ṛtam | it | cikiddhi | ṛtasya | dhārā | anṷ | tṛndhi | pūrvīḥ | na | aham | yātum | saha̭sā | na | dvayena̭ | ṛtam | sa̭pāmi | aruṣasya | vṛṣṇaḥ || 2 ||
ᵛṛtaṃ stotraṃ he ᵛcikitvaḥ jānannagne tvaṃ ᵛṛtaṃ mayā kriyamāṇamidaṃ stotraṃ ᵛcikiddhi jānīhi ||
‘kita jñāne’ |
juhotyādiḥ |

ᵛit iti pūraṇaḥ |
ṛtamiti padasya parasmin‌ mantrate vidyamāne'pi chāndasatvātparāṅgavadbhāvābhāvaḥ ||
kiṃca ᵛpūrvīḥ bahvīḥ ᵛṛtasya udakasya ᵛdhārāḥ sasyapoṣaṇārthamutpādayitum ᵛanu ᵛtṛndhi meghānanuvidhaya |
ᵛsahasā balena yuktaḥ ᵛahaṃ ᵛyātuṃ karmaṇā nāśakarīṃ hiṃsāṃ ᵛna ᵛsapāmi na spṛśāmi na karomīti yāvat |
ᵛdvayena ca satyānṛtābhyāṃ yatkiṃcidvaidikaṃ kṛtyaṃ ᵛna sapāmi ||
‘papa samavāye’ iti dhātuḥ ||
kiṃtu ᵛaruṣasya ārocamānasya ᵛvṛṣṇaḥ kāmānāṃ varṣitustava ᵛṛtaṃ stotrameva ᵛsapāmi ||
karyā no̭ agne ṛtaya̭nnṛtana̭ bhuvo॒ narvedā ucatha̭sya na॒vyaḥ |
vedā̭ me de॒va ṛta॒pā ṛtṷ̄nāṃ nāhaṃ pa॒tiṃ sa̭nitu॒rasya̭ rā॒yaḥ ||
3 ||
karyā | naḥ | agne | ṛtayan | ṛtena | bhuvaḥ | narvedāḥ | ucathasya | navyaḥ | veda | me | deva | ṛtapāḥ | ṛtūnām | na | aham | patim | sanituḥ | asya | rāyaḥ ||3||
he ᵛagne ᵛṛtadhan sarvaprāṇināṃ jīvanahetukamudakaṃ kurvan ᵛkayā kenāpi ᵛṛtena satyena kriyamāṇena karmaṇā ᵛnaḥ ᵛucathasya asmadīyasya stotrasya ᵛnarvedāḥ jñātā ᵛbhuvaḥ bhaveḥ |
ᵛnavyaḥ stutyaḥ ᵛṛtūnāṃ ᵛṛtapāḥ |
dvitīyaḥ ᵛṛtuśabdo'nuvādakaḥ ᵛdevaḥ dyotamāno'si ᵛme māṃ ᵛveda vettu |
ᵛsanituḥ bhajamānasya ᵛasya mama ᵛrāyaḥ paścādilakṣaṇasya dhanasya ᵛpatiṃ svāminaṃ tamagnim ᵛahaṃ ᵛna jānāmi ||

‘arcantastvā’ iti ṣaṭca trayodaśa sūktaṃ sutabharasyārṣaṃ gāyatramāgneyam |
anukrāntaṃ ca— ‘arcanto gāyatraṃ tu’ iti |

sūtritaṃ ca—‘arcantasveti sūkte’ (āśva. śrau. 4. 13) iti |
āhavanīyopasthāne ‘tvamagne saprathā asi’ ityekā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘saṃ naḥ sṛja sumatayā vājavatyā tvamagne saprathā asīti ca’ (āśva. śrau. 3. 10) iti |
aśvamedhe pauṣṇau ‘tvamagne saprathāḥ’ iti prathājyabhāgānuvākyā |
‘tvamagne saprathā asi soma yāste mayobhuva iti saddhantau’ (āśva. śrau. 10. 6) iti ||
arcantastvā hvāmahe'rcantaḥ samidhīmahi | agne arcanta ūtaye || 1 || arcantaḥ | tvā | hvāmahe | arcantaḥ | sam | idhīmahi | agne | arcantaḥ | ūtaye || 1 ||
ha agre tvā tvām arcantaḥ pūjayanto vayaṃ hvāmahe āhvayāmaḥ |
arcantaḥ stuvanto vayam ūtaye rakṣaṇāya tarpaṇāya vā tvāṃ samidhīmahi samidbhiḥ saṃdīpayāmaḥ |
arcanta iti nṛtārtha padam ādarātiśayārtham ||
agneḥ stomaṃ manāmahe sidhramadya divispṛśaḥ | devasya draviṇasyavaḥ || 2 || agneḥ | stomam | manāmahe | sidhrama | adya | divispṛśaḥ | devasya | draviṇasyavaḥ || 2 ||

agni̭rja॒ṣata॒ no॒ giro̭ hotā॒ yo mānṷṣeṣvā | sa ya̭kṣa॒daivya̭ṃ jana̭m || 3 || agniḥ | ja॒ṣata॒ | naḥ | gira̭ḥ | hotā̭ | yaḥ | mānṷṣeṣvā | ā | saḥ | ya॒kṣat | daivya̭m | jana̭m ||3||
ha hotā devānāmāhdātā yaḥ agniḥ mānuṣeṣvā vasati saḥ agniḥ naḥ asmākaṃ giraḥ stutīḥ juṣata sevatām |
so'gniḥ daivyaṃ janaṃ devasambandhanaṃ janaṃ yakṣat yajatu ||
tvama̭gne॒ supra̭thā asi॒ juṣṭo̭ hotā॒ varḙṇyaḥ | tvayā̭ ya॒jñaṃ vi ta̭nvate || 4 || tvam | agnḙ | su'pra̭thāḥ | asi॒ | juṣṭa̭ḥ | hotā̭ | varḙṇyaḥ | tvayā̭ | ya॒jñam | vi | ta॒nva॒te ||4||

he stotaḥ amartyam amaraṇadharmāṇam agniṃ stomena stotreṇa bodhaya |
svābhilapitārthasiddhaye prabodhaya |
yo'gniḥ samidhānaḥ stotraiḥ samyak prakāśamānaḥ saḥ naḥ havyā asmadīyāni havyāni havīṃṣi deveṣu dadhat nidadhātu ||

dvitīye'nuvāke aṣṭādaśa1 sūktāni |
tatra ‘pra vedhase’ iti pañcacaṃ prathamaṃ sūktamāṅgirasasya dharuṇa- syārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhamāgneyam |
atrānukramaṇikā—‘pra vedhase pañcāṅgiraso dharuṇaḥ’ iti |

1 ||
pra | vedha̭se | kavayḙ | ve॒dyāya̭ | gi॒ram | bharḙ | ya॒śasḙ | pū॒rvyā̭ya | ghṛ॒ta'prasa̭ttaḥ | a॒sura̭: | su॒śeva̭: | rā॒yaḥ | dha॒rtā | dha॒ruṇa̭ḥ | vasva̭: | a॒gniḥ || 1 ||
yaḥ ᵥagniḥ ᵥghṛtaprasattaḥ havīrūpeṇa ghṛtena prasannaḥ ᵥasuraḥ balavān ᵥsuśevaḥ |
śobhanaṃ śevaṃ sukhaṃ yasyāsau suśevaḥ |
śevamiti sukhanāmasu pāṭhāt |
ᵥrāyaḥ dhanasya ᵥdhartā poṣakaḥ ᵥdharuṇaḥ haviṣāṃ dhārakaḥ ᵥvasvaḥ vāsakaḥ agniḥ bhavati ᵥvedhase vidhātre ᵥkavaye krāntadṛśe ᵥvedyāya stutyāya ||
vidiryaṃ maninā samānārtho maniṣa stutikarmā ||
ᵥyaśase yaśasvine ᵥpūrvyāya mukhyāya tasmā agnaye ᵥgiraṃ stutiṃ ᵥpra ᵥbhare praṇayāni ||

di॒vo dha॒rman dha॒ruṇa̭ sedu॒ṣe nṛ॒ṇātḙrajā॒tā~ a॒bhi ye na̭nakṣuḥ ||
2 ||
ṛ॒tena̭ | ṛ॒tam | dha॒ruṇa̭m | dhā॒ra॒ya॒nta | ya॒jñasya̭ | śā॒ke | pa॒ra॒me | vi'o̭man | di॒vaḥ | dha॒rman | dha॒ruṇa̭ | se॒du॒ṣaḥ | nṝn | jā॒taiḥ | a॒jā॒tān | a॒bhi | ye | na॒nakṣuḥ || 2 ||
ᵥye yajamānāḥ ᵥdivaḥ dyulokasya ᵥdharuṇe dhārake ᵥdharman yajñe ᵥseduṣaḥ āsīnān ᵥnṝn netṝn ᵥajātān devān ᵥjātaiḥ manuṣyairṛtvigbhiḥ ᵥabhi ᵥnanakṣuḥ abhiprāmuvanti te yajamānāḥ ᵥyajñasya ᵥdharuṇa̭ dhārakam ᵥṛtaṃ satyarūpamagniṃ ᵥśāke karmaṇi nimitte ᵥparame utkṛṣte ᵥvyoman sthāne uttaravedyām ᵥṛtena stotreṇa ᵥdhārayanta dhārayanti ||
a॒ṃhoyṷvasta॒nva̭sta॒nva̭te vi va॒yo ma̭ha॒duṣṭa̭raṃ pu॒rvyā̭ya |
sa ma̭va॒to na̭va॒jāta̭ḥ tu॒ryā॒ti॒siha॒ṃ na ku॒drama̭bhi॒taḥ pari̭ dhuḥ ||
3 ||
a॒ṃhaḥ'yṷvaḥ | ta॒nva̭: | ta॒nva̭te | vi | va॒yaḥ | ma॒hat | du॒sta॒ram | pu॒rvyā̭ya | saḥ | su॒'vra̭taḥ | na॒va॒'jāta̭ḥ | tu॒ryā̭t | si॒ham | na | ku॒dram | a॒bhi॒taḥ | pari̭ | dhuḥ ||3||
1. ga-ja-śa-daśa |
2. jha-ta-ra- itīti |
ye¹ yajamānā: ᵛpūjyāya mukhyāyāsmā agnaye ᵛmahan adhikaṃ ᵛduṣṭaṃ rākṣasairuprāpaṃ ᵛvaya: havīrūpamannaṃ prayacchanti te yajamānā: ᵛtanva: mvāstanu: ᵛaṃhoyuva: aṃhasā viyojikā: ᵛvi ᵛtanvate vistārayanti |
ᵛnavajātaḥ ᵛsa: agni: ᵛsaṃvataḥ saṃgatān śatrūn ᵛtuturyān taratu |
kiṃca ᵛabhita: sarvato vartamānā: śatravo māṃ ᵛpari varjayitvā ᵛsthu: tiṣṭheyu: |
kathamiva |
mṛgā: ᵛkutraṃ ᵛsiṃhaṃ ᵛna siṃhamiva ||
māteva yaddharase praprathāno janaijanaṃ dhāryase cakṣase ca |
varyovayo jarase yaddhāna: pari tmanā viṣurūpo jigāsi ||
4 ||
mātā'iva | yat | bharase | praprathāna: | janam'janam | dhāryase | cakṣase | ca | varya:'vaya: | jarase | yat | dadhāna: | pari | tmanā | viṣu'rūpa: | jigāsi || 4 ||
ᵛpraprathāna: sarvatra prathamāna: ᵛyat yastvaṃ ᵛmāteva janarnāva ᵛjanajanaṃ sarva janaṃ ᵛbharase bibharṣi |
kiṃca ᵛdhāyase dhāraṇāya ᵛcakṣase darśanāya ᵛca sarvai: prārthyase |
ᵛyat yadā ᵛdadhāna: dhāryamāṇo bhavasi tadā ᵛvayovaya: sarvamannaṃ ᵛjarase jarayasi |
api ca ᵛvipurūpa: nānārūpa: san ᵛmanā ātmanā ᵛpari ᵛjigāmi sarvabhūtāni parigacchasi ||
vājo nu te śavasaspātvaṃtamuruṃ dodhaṃ dhruṇaṃ deva rāya: |
padaṃ na tāyuguhā dadhāno maho rāye citayannatrimaspa: ||
5 ||
vāja: | nu | te | śavasa: | pātu | antam | urum | dodham | dhruṇam | deva | rāya: | padam | na | tāyu: | guhā | dadhāna: | maha: | rāye | citayan | atrima: | asparhayasya: ||5||
he ᵛdeva dyotamānāgne ᵛuruṃ pṛthuṃ ᵛdodhaṃ kāmānāṃ dāghāraṃ ᵛrāya: dhanasya ᵛdhruṇaṃ dhārakaṃ ᵛvāja: havilakṣaṇamannaṃ ᵛte tvadīyasya ᵛśavasa: balasya ᵛantaṃ samāṃsi saṃpūti ᵛnu adya ᵛpātu rakṣatu |
ᵛtāyu:³ ᵛna taskaro yathā ᵛguhā guhāyāṃ ᵛpadaṃ dravyaṃ ᵛdadhāna: dhārayan rakṣati tadvat ᵛmaha: mahate ᵛrāye dhanāya dhanalābhāthaṃ ᵛcitayan sanmārgaṃ prakāśayan ᵛatrimaspa: |
atrimaṣṭimapramāṇaya: |
ᵛyad⁴ tamāpadyo'pārāya:⁴ ||
‘sṛ prīṇanadhāraṇayo:’ iti dhātu: ||
7 ||
‘brahadvaya:’ iti pañcacaṃ dvitīyaṃ sūktātreyasya purorūpam |
antyā paṅkti: |
śiṣṭā anuṣṭubha: |
agnidevatā |
tathā cānukāntaṃ—‘bṛhatpūra:⁵ paṅktyantaṃ hi’ iti |
prātaranuvāke āgneye ṛtāvānu-ṣṭubhe chandasyāśvinaśastre cedamādīni daśa sūktāni caturthanavamavarjitāni |
āditastriṣu sūkteṣvanyā-
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-bha-ra-śa- he |
2. ta-dyotamānarūpāgne |
3. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-śa-tāyustaskara: |
tāyuriti stenanāma |
padamiva ātmīyaṃ mārgamiva guhā guhāyāṃ dadhāna: ātmana: śatrūṇāṃ prakāśaṃ nidadhāna: |
4. ga-gha-ja- ‘yadvā...apārāya:’ nāsti |
5. jha-na-ra-bṛhatpura: |
mudraret |
tathā ca sūtritaṃ—'bṛhadraya iti daśānāṃ caturthanavame udvareduttamamuttamāṃ cāditasyāṇām ' ( āśva. śrau. 4. 13 ) iti' ||
bṛhadrayo hi bhānave'rcā devāyāgnaye | yaṃ mitraṃ na praśastibhirmatīso dadhire puraḥ || 1 || bṛhat | vayaḥ | hi | bhānave | arca | devāya | agnaye | yam | mitram | na | praśastibhiḥ | matīsaḥ | dadhire | puraḥ || 1 ||
yajñe 'bhānave dīptimate agnaye 'bṛhadrayaḥ havīrūpamannaṃ dīyate 'hi |
atastvamapi 'devāya dyotamānāya 'agnaye vayaḥ 'arca prayaccha |
'matīsaḥ manuṣyāḥ 'yam agniṃ 'mitraṃ 'na sakhāyamiva 'praśastibhiḥ prakṛṣṭābhiḥ stutibhiḥ 'puraḥ 'dadhire puraskurvanti |
sa hi dyubhijarnānāṃ hotā dakṣasya bāhvoḥ | vi havyamagnirānuṣagbhago na vāramṛṇvati || 2 || saḥ | hi | dyubhiḥ | janānām | hotā | dakṣasya | bāhvoḥ | vi | havyam | agniḥ | ānuṣak | bhagaḥ | na | vāram | ṛṇvati || 2 ||
yaḥ 'agniḥ 'havyaṃ haviḥ 'ānuṣak deveṣvanuṣaktaṃ karoti 'bāhvoḥ bhujayoḥ 'dakṣasya balasya 'dyubhiḥ dyutibhiryuktaḥ 'saḥ agniḥ 'janānāṃ yajamānānāṃ 'hotā devānāmāhvātā bhavati |
'bhago 'na sūrya iva 'vāraṃ varaṇīyaṃ dhanaṃ 'vi 'ṛṇvati viśeṣeṇa prayacchati manuṣyeṣu ||
asya stomairmaghonaḥ suruye vṛddhaśaucipaḥ | viśvā yasminttuviṣvāṇi samaryai śuṣmamādadhuḥ || 3 || asya | stomaiḥ | maghonaḥ | sadhaye | vṛddha'śaucipaḥ | viśvā | yasmin | tuvi'svani | sam | arye | śuṣmam | ādadhuḥ || 3 ||
'maghonaḥ maghavato dhanavataḥ 'vṛddhaśaucipaḥ |
vṛddhāni pravṛddhāni' śocīṃṣi tejāṃsi yasyāsau vṛddhaśociḥ |
tasyaivavidhasyāgneḥ 'stome stotre 'sadhaye ca syāma' |
'tuviṣvāṇi bahuśabde 'arye svāmini 'yasmin agnau 'viśvā ||
'supāṃ suluk ' iti dvādeśaḥ ||
viśve sarve ṛtvijaḥ 'śuṣmaṃ balaṃ havirbhiḥ stotraiśca 'sam 'ādadhuḥ samyagādadhati ||
1. na-iti prātaranuvākanyāyeneti ca | 2. pa-pha-ba-haviḥ | hi pūraṇaḥ | 3. gha-śa-'pravṛddhāni ' nāsti | 4. ta-syāma | vayamiti śeṣaḥ |
adhā hya̭ma e॒ṣāṃ su॒vīrya̭sya maṃhanā | tami॒dyaha̭ṃ na roda॒sī pari̭ śra॒vo ba̭bhūvatuḥ || 4 || adha | hi | a॒gne | e॒ṣām | su॒vīrya̭sya | maṃhanā | tam | it | ya॒ham | na | roda॒sī iti̭ | pari̭ | śra॒vaḥ | ba॒bhūvatṷḥ || 4 ||
he agne adha atha eṣām asmākaṃ yajamānānāṃ suvīryasya sarvaiḥ spṛhaṇīyasya balasya maṃhanā maṃhanāyai dānāya bhava ||
mahatirdānakarmā ||
rodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau yahaṃ na mahāntaṃ sūryamiva śravaḥ sarvaiḥ śravaṇīyam tamin tamaprameva pari babhūvatuḥ parigṛhīta: ||
nū na e॒hi vā॒rya̭ma॒gne gṛ̭ṇāna ā bha̭ra | ye va॒yaṃ ye ca̭ sū॒raya̭ḥ sva॒sti dhā॒rmahe॒ sacā̭taidhi pṛ॒tsu no̭ vṛ॒dhe || 5 || nu | naḥ | ā | i॒hi | vā॒rya̭m | a॒gne | gṛ॒ṇānaḥ | ā | bha॒ra | ye | va॒yam | ye | ca̭ | sū॒raya̭ḥ | sva॒sti | dhā॒rmahe | sacā̭ | u॒ta | e॒dhi | pṛ॒tsu | naḥ | vṛ॒dhe || 5 ||
he agne gṛṇānaḥ stūyamānaḥ san nu kṣipram ehi |
yajñaṃ pratyāgaccha |
āgatya ca naḥ asmabhyaṃ vāryaṃ varaṇīyaṃ dhanam ā bhara saṃpādaya |
ye vayaṃ yajamānāḥ ye ca sūrayaḥ stotāraḥ sacā havirbhiḥ sahitā ubhe ye vayaṃ svasti stotraṃ dhāmahe kurmaḥ |
uta api ca pṛtsu pṛtanāsu naḥ asmākaṃ vṛdhe samṛddhaye edhi bhava ||
8 ||
‘ā yajñeḥ’ iti pañcacaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sūktamātreyasya purorudhamāmeyam |
anayā paṅktiḥ |
śiṣṭāḥ paṅktyantaparibhāṣayānuṣṭubhaḥ |
‘ā yajñeḥ’ ityanukramaṇikā |
anyāvarjaṃ prātaranuvākāśvinaśastrayoḥ ‘bṛhadvayaḥ’ ityādidaśasūktamadhye viniyoga uktaḥ ||
ā ya॒jñairde॒va ma̭rtya॒ itthā̭ ta॒vyā̭sa॒mūta̭ye | a॒gniṃ kṛ̭te su॒dhvarḙ pū॒rurī̭ḻīta॒ artḙve || 1 || ā | ya॒jñaiḥ | de॒va | ma॒rtyaḥ | i॒tthā | ta॒vyā̭sam | ū॒tayḙ | a॒gnim | kṛ॒te | su॒'adhva̭re | pū॒ruḥ | ī̭ḻīta | a॒rva̭se || 1 ||
he deva martyaḥ manuṣya ṛtvik itthā itthaṃ tavyāṃsaṃ svatojobhiḥ pravṛddham agnim ūtaye tarpaṇāyā yajñaiḥ stotraiḥ ā hvayati |
kiṃca puruḥ manuṣyaḥ stotā kṛte svadhvare śobhane ṛtau tam agnim avase rakṣaṇāya īḻīta stauti ||
1. pa-pha-ba-adha hi |
2. kha-ca-ma-ṇa-na-ma-ra-śa-śrayaṇīyam |
asya hi svaryaśastara āsā vidharmanmanyase |
taṃ nākaṃ citraśociṣaṃ mandraṃ paro manīṣayā ||
2 ||
asya | hi | svaryaśaḥ'taraḥ | āsā | vi'dharman | manyase | tam | nākam | citra'śociṣam | mandram | paraḥ | manīṣayā || 2 ||
he vidharman | viśiṣṭo dharmo yasyāsau vidharmā stotā | tasya saṃbodhanam | he stotaḥ | svaryaśastaraḥ | suyaśasāṃ madhye'tiśayena suyaśastvaṃ | manīṣayā prakṛṣṭabuddhyā | sādhanena | taṃ prasiddham | asya amuṃ hi agnim | āsā āsyena vācā | manyase stauṣi | kiṃviśiṣṭamagnim | nākaṃ sukham | akaṃ duḥkham | na vidyate'kaṃ yasya saḥ | taṃ | citraśociṣaṃ citratejasaṃ | mandraṃ stutyaṃ | paraḥ parastāt sthitam ||
asya vāsā u arciṣā ya āryukta tujā girā |
divo na yasya retasā bṛhacchocantyarcayaḥ ||
3 ||
asya | vai | asau | ū~ iti | arciṣā | yaḥ | ayukta | tujā | girā | divaḥ | na | yasya | retasā | bṛhat | śocanti | arcayaḥ || 3 ||
yaḥ | agniḥ | tujā jagadrakṣaṇasamarthena balena | girā stutyā | ayukta saṃbaddho bhavati | divo na dyotamānasyādityasyeva | yasya agneḥ | retasā prabhayā kṛtsnaṃ jagadbhāsaṃ yasyāgneḥ | bṛhat bṛhantaḥ | arcayaḥ | dīptayaḥ | śocanti prakāśante | asya vai mvagneḥ | arciṣā prabhayā | asau ādityaḥ | arciṣmān bhavati | u iti pūraṇaḥ ||
asya kratvā viccetaso dasmasya vasu ratha ā |
adhā viśvāmu havyo'gnirvikṣu pra śasyate ||
4 ||
asya | kratvā | vi'cetasaḥ | dasmasya | vasu | rathe | ā | adha | viśvāmu | havyaḥ | agniḥ | vikṣu | pra | śasyate || 4 ||
vicetasaḥ | sumataya ṛtvijaḥ | dasmasya darśanīyasya | asya agneḥ | kratvā karmaṇā yajñādinā | vasu vasūni dhanāni | rathe ā dadhāti | havyaḥ yajñārthamāhvayaḥ | saḥ | agniḥ | adha atha upastyanantarameva | viśvāsu sarvāsu | vikṣu prajāsu | pra | śasyate stūyate ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-prakṛṣṭayā | 2. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-śa-abhavat | 3. ga-gha-ja-asya dasmasya | 4. kha-ga-dha-ca-ja-jha-ṇa-na-bha-śa- 'asya' nāsti |
nū na iddhi vāryamāsā saṃcanata sūryaḥ |
ūrjā napādbhiṣṭaye pāhi śagdhi svastaye utaidhi pṛtsu nau vṛdhe ||
5 ||
na | naḥ | it | hi | vāryam | āsā | saṃcanata | sūryaḥ | ūrjā | napāt | abhiṣṭaye | pāhi | śagdhi | svastaye | uta | edhi | pṛtsu | naḥ | vṛdhe || 5 ||
he agne naḥ asmabhyaṃ vāryaṃ varaṇīyaṃ dhanaṃ nu kṣipraṃ prayaccha |
iddhi iti pūrṇau |
yataḥ sūryaḥ mtoṭārastvattaḥ sakāśāt āmā āsyena stotreṇa saṃcanata dhanaṃ labhante |
kiṃca he ūrjo napāt annasya na pātayitaḥ balasya putra vā abhiṣṭaye abhīṣṭate mahyamannaṃ prayaccheti śeṣaḥ |
pāhi |
asmānāpadbhyāṃ rakṣa |
mante kṣemāya paścādillakṣaṇasya dhanasya kṣemārthaṃ śagdhi tvāṃ yāce ||
vyatyayena madhyamaḥ ||
śakno bhavati vā |
he agne uta api ca pṛtsu pṛtanāsu no vṛdhe matsṛṣṭaye edhi bhava ||
5 ||
‘prātaragniḥ’ iti pañcarcaṃ caturthaṃ sūktam |
atreyamanukramaṇikā—‘prātarmuktavāhā dvitaḥ’ iti |
muktavāhā iti viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭa ātreyo dvita ṛṣiḥ |
‘paṅkyantaṃ hi’ iti hiśabdādasyāpi sūktasya pañcamī paṅktiḥ |
paṅkyantaparibhāṣayā śiṣṭā anuṣṭubhaḥ |
ukto viniyogaḥ prātaranuvākāśvinaśastrayoḥ ||
prātaragniḥ purupriyo viśaḥ stavetātithiḥ |
viśvāni yo amartyo havyā mateṣu raṇayati ||
1 ||
prātaḥ | agniḥ | puru'priyaḥ | viśaḥ | staveta | atithiḥ | viśvāni | yaḥ | amartyaḥ | havyā | mateṣu | raṇayati || 1 ||
purupriyaḥ bahupriyaḥ viśaḥ yajamāne dhanasya niveśakaḥ |
atithiḥ |
yajamānānāṃ gṛhānprati tithivyatetyatithiḥ |
tathāha yāskaḥ—‘atithirabhyatito gṛhānbhavatyabhyeti tithiṣu parukulānīti vā paragṛhāṇīti vā’ (niru. 4. 5) iti |
evaṃvidhaḥ agniḥ prātaḥ stavena stūyate |
amartyaḥ amaraṇadharmā yaḥ agniḥ mateṣu manuṣyeṣu yajamāneṣu sthitāni viśvāni sarvāṇi havyā havyāni havīṃṣi raṇayati kāmayate ||
dvitārya muktavāhase svasya dakṣasya maṃhanā |
induṃ sa dhatta ānuṣakstotā citte amartya ||
2 ||
dvitārya | mukta'vāhase | svasya | dakṣasya | maṃhanā | indum | saḥ | dhatte | ānuṣak | stotā | cit | te | amartya || 2 ||
1. ma-dha-ja-yancha |
2. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-ā yajñaiḥ prātarmuktavāhā |
he agne ᵥdvitāya dvitaputrāya ᵥsṛktavāhase|
sṛktaṃ śuddhaṃ havirdevebhyo vahati prāpayatīti sṛktavāhaḥ|
tasmai ᵥsvasya ātmīyasya ᵥdakṣasya balasya dhanasya vā ᵥmahanā mahanāyai dānāya bhava|
he ᵥamartya niyāme yataḥ kāraṇāt ᵥte tubhyaṃ ᵥsaḥ sṛktavāhāḥ ᵥānupak sarvadā'nupaktam ᵥinduṃ somaṃ ᵥdhatte dhārayati ᵥstotā ᵥcit stotā ca bhavati ||
taṃ vo dīrghāyuśociṣaṃ girā huve maghonām |
ariṣṭe yeṣāṃ ratho vyaśvadāvannīyate ||
3 ||
tam | vaḥ | dīrghāyu'śociṣam | girā | huve | maghonām | ariṣṭe | yeṣām | rathaḥ | vi | aśva'dāvan | īyate || 3 ||
he agne ᵥdīrghāyuśociṣaṃ dīrghagamanadīptiṃ ᵥtaṃ ᵥvaḥ |
pūjāyāṃ bahuvacanam |
tvāṃ ᵥmaghonāṃ dhanināṃ kṛte ᵥgirā stotreṇa ᵥhuve āhvayāmi |
he ᵥaśvadāvan aśvānāṃ dātaḥ ᵥyeṣāṃ dhanināṃ ᵥrathaḥ yuddheṣu ᵥariṣṭaḥ śatrubhirhiṃsitaḥ ᵥvi ᵥīyate vigatacchat ||
citrā vā yeṣu dīdhitirāsabhukthā pānti ye |
stīrṇaṃ barhiḥ svarnnare śravāṃsi dadhire pari ||
4 ||
citrā | vā | yeṣu | dīdhitiḥ | āsan | ukthā | pānti | ye | stīrṇam | barhiḥ | svaḥ'nare | śravāṃsi | dadhire | pari || 4 ||
ᵥyeṣu ṛtvikṣu ᵥcitrā nānāvidhā ᵥdīdhitiḥ yajñaviṣayā kriyā bhavati |
ᵥye ᵥāmanu āsye ᵥukthā ukthāni stotrāṇi ᵥpānti rakṣanti tairṛtvigbhiḥ ᵥstīrṇaṃ stīrṇasya ᵥbarhiḥ barhiṣaḥ ᵥpari upari ᵥśravāṃsi annāni havīṃṣi ᵥsvarnnare |
svaḥ svargaṃ naraṃ yajamānaṃ nayatīti svarnnaro yajñaḥ |
tasmin ᵥdadhire nidhrīyante ||
ye mai pañcāśataṃ duduraśvānāṃ sadhastuti |
dyumadagne mahi śravo bṛhatkṛdhi maghonāṃ nudasmṛta nṛṇām ||
5 ||
ye | me | pañcā'śatam | daduḥ | aśvānām | sadhastuti | dyumat | agne | mahi | śravaḥ | bṛhat | kṛdhi | maghonām | nudasvat | amṛta | nṛṇām ||5||

agne viśvebhirā gahi devebhirvyadāṃtaye | hotāraṃ tvā vṛṇīmahe || 4 || agne | viśvebhiḥ | ā | gahi | devebhiḥ | havya'dāṃtaye | hotāram | tvā | vṛṇīmahe || 4 ||
he agne viśvebhiḥ sarvaiḥ devebhiḥ devaiḥ saha sva havyadāṃtaye haviṣāṃ dātre yajamānāya tadartham ā gahi yajñaṃ pratyāgaccha |
yataḥ hotāraṃ devānāmāhvātāraṃ tvā tvāṃ vṛṇīmahe prārthayāmahe ||
yajamānāya sunvate agne suvīryaṃ vaha | deverā satsi barhiṣi || 5 || yajamānāya | sunvate | ā | agne | su'vīryam | vaha | devaiḥ | ā | satsi | barhiṣi || 5 ||

ᐡyaḥ bhikṣaṇo'rthī ᐢsūrye prerakaya dhanānāṃ dātre vāsmai ᐣaśvamedhāya rājaparyaye ᐤme mahyaṃ dehi ᐥiti ᐦpravocati pravavīti |
yo'śvamedhaṃ dhanāni bhikṣate ityarthaḥ |
ᐧṛcā agneḥ stotreṇa saha ᐨyate ātmanaḥ samīpaṃ gacchate tasmā arthine'śvamedhaḥ ᐩsaniṃ dhanaṃ ᐪdadat dadāti |

1 ||
sam'iddhaḥ | agniḥ | divi | śociḥ | aśret | pratyaṅ | uṣasam | urvi‍yā | vi | bhāti | eti | prācī | viśva'vārā | namaḥ'bhiḥ | devān | īḻānā | haviṣā | ghṛtācī || 1 ||
ᵥsamiddhaḥ samyagdīptaḥ ᵥagniḥ ᵥdivi dyotamāne'ntarikṣe ᵥśociḥ tejaḥ ᵥaśret śrayati |
tathā ᵥuṣasaṃ ᵥpratyaṅ upasamabhimukhaḥ san ᵥurviyā uru vistīrṇaṃ ᵥvi ᵥbhāti viśeṣeṇa ājate |
ᵥnamobhiḥ stotraiḥ ᵥdevān indrādīn ᵥīlānā stuvatī ᵥhaviṣā puroḍāśādilakṣaṇena yuktā ᵥghṛtācī ghṛtācyā sucā sahitā ᵥviśvavārā sarvamapi pāparūpaṃ śatruṃ vārayiṣyetannāmikā ᵥprācī prāṅmukhī satī ᵥeti |
evaṃbhūtarmagni pratigacchati ||
sumidhyamāno amṛtasya rājasi haviṣkṛṇvantaṃ sacase svastaye |
viśvaṃ sa dhatte draviṇaṃ yaminvasyātidhyamagne ni ca dhatta itpuraḥ ||
2 ||
su'idhyamānaḥ | amṛtasya | rājasi | haviḥ | kṛṇvantam | sacase | svastaye | viśvam | saḥ | dhatte | draviṇam | yam | invasi | atidhyam | agne | ni | ca | dhatte | it | puraḥ || 2 ||
he agne ᵥsamidhyamānaḥ samyagidhyamānastvam ᵥamṛtasya udakasya ᵥrājami īśiṣe |
tathā ᵥhaviṣkṛṇvantaṃ puroḍāśādihavikartāraṃ yajamānaṃ ᵥsvastaye avināśāya ᵥsacase sevase |
kiṃca ᵥyaṃ yajamānam ᵥinvami gacchāmi ᵥsaḥ yajamānaḥ ᵥviśvaṃ samastaṃ ᵥdraviṇaṃ paścādilakṣaṇaṃ dhanaṃ ᵥdhatte dhārayati |
api ca he ᵥagne ᵥatidhyam atithi rūpasya tava yogyaṃ haviḥ ᵥpuraḥ ᵥit tava purastādeva ᵥni ᵥdhatte ᵥca sthāpayati ca ||
pavitreṣṭyāṃ sviṣṭakṛtaḥ 'agne śardha' iti yājyā |
sūyate hi—'juṣṭo damanā agne śardha mahate saubhāgeti saṃyājye' ( āśva. śrau. 2. 12 ) iti |
mākamedhepu marudgaṇaḥ krīḍibhyaḥ puroḍāśaṃ sasakapālamityatrāpyeva sviṣṭakṛto yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'juṣṭo damanā agne śardha mahate saubhagayeti saṃyājye' ( āśva. śrau. 2. 18 ) iti ||
agne śardha mahate saubhāgāya tavaṃ dyumnānyuttamāni santu |
saṃ jāspatyaṃ suyamamā kṛṇuṣva śatrūyatāmabhi tiṣṭhā mahāṃsi ||
3 ||
agne | śardha | mahate | saubhāgāya | tava | dyumnāni | uttamāni | santu | sam | jāḥpatyam | su'yamam | ā | kṛṇuṣva | śatrūyatām | abhi | tiṣṭha | mahāṃsi || 3 ||

1. pa-pha-ba-samyagiddhasya |
^uta api ca he ^brahmāṇaḥ bṛhanto he ^marutaḥ yūyam ^indraḥ ca ^susutasya^ suṣṭhubhiṣutasya ^me madīyasya ^asya ^somasya imaṃ somam ||
dvitīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī ||
^peyāḥ pibata ||
pibaterāśīrliṅ madhyamabahuvacanasyaikavacanam ||
^taddhi tatkhalu ^hadhyaṃ yuṣmābhiḥ pītaṃ somātmakaṃ haviḥ ^manuṣye manuṣyāya yajamānāya ^gāḥ dhenvṛṣṭilakṣaṇānyudakāni vā ^avindan vedayati |
yajamānaṃ gā lambhayatītyarthaḥ |

ādrodasī vitaraṃ vi ṣkebhāyatsaṃvivyānaśrvidviyasai mṛgaṃ keḥ |

1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-ma-iti suktam |
he indra tvaṃ yat yadā trī trayāṇāṃ śatā śatasaṃkhyākānāṃ mahiṣāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ mā: māṃsāni agha: bhakṣitavānasi ||
ghaseratevāṃ rūpam ||
yadā ca maghavā dhanavāṃstvaṃ somyā somamayāni trī trīṇi sarāṃsi pātrāṇi apā: pītavānasi |
yat yadendra: ahiṃ vṛtraṃ jaghāna hatavān tadā viśve sarve devā: bhara somapānādinā pūrṇam indrāya indraṃ yuddhārtham ahanta āhvayanta |
tatra dṛṣṭānta: |
kāraṃ na svāmina: karmakaramiva tadvat ||
uśanamastome 'uśanā yarasahasyai:' iti marutvatīya sūktamukhyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'uśanasa- stomena garagīrṇamivātmānaṃ manyamāno yajetāśanā yarasahasyairayātam' (āśva. śrau. 9. 5) iti ||
uśanā yatsa̭hasyai॒rayātaṃ gṛ॒hamindra̭ jūjuvānebhi̭raśvaiḥ |
va॒nvāno atra̭ sa॒ratha̭ṃ yayātha ku॒tsena̭ de॒vairava̭norha śuṣṇam ||
stomā̭sastvā॒ gauri̭vīte॒ravardha̭nna॒dhayo॒ vaida̭thināya॒ piprṷm |
ā tvā॒mṛji॒śvā sa॒ruyāya॒ cakre॒ paca̭npu॒ktīrapi̭ba॒ḥ soma̭masya ||
11 ||
stomā̭saḥ | tvā | gauri̭'vīteḥ | a॒vardha̭n | a॒ra॒dhaya̭ḥ | vaida̭thināya | piprṷm | ā | tvā॒m | ṛ॒ji॒śvā | sa॒ru॒yāya̭ | cakrḙ | paca̭n | pu॒ktīḥ | a॒pi॒baḥ | soma̭m | a॒sya || 11 ||
he indra gaurivīteḥ etannāmakasya mantradraṣṭurmedha sambandhini stomāsaḥ stotrāṇi tvā tvām avadharna vardhayantu |
vaidathināya vidathinaḥ putrāya ṛjiśvā ṛjiśvāmne piprum etannāmaka masuram aradhayaḥ vaśamanayaḥ |
he indra ṛjiśvā nāma kaścidapi saruyāya tava sakhitvāya puktīḥ pakvyāni puroḍāśādīni pacan pākaṃ kurvan tvām ā cakre abhimukhāṃcakāra |
kiṃca tvam asya ṛjiśvanaḥ sambandhinaṃ somam apibaḥ apāḥ ||
daśapeye'hani marutvatīya śastre 'navagvāmaḥ' iti sūktamuktvā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'navagvāmaḥ sutasomāsa indraṃ sakhā ha yatra sakhibhirnavagvābhiriti nividdhānayorāśa' (āśva. śrau. 9. 3) iti ||
navagvā̭saḥ sutaso̭māma indra॒ṃ daśa̭gvāso॒ abhya̭rcantya॒rkaiḥ |
ga॒vyaṃ cidūrva̭mapi॒dhāna̭vanta॒ṃ taṃ ci॒nnara̭ḥ śaśa̭mānā a॒pa vra̭n ||
12 ||
nava̭'gvāsaḥ | suta̭'somāsaḥ | indra̭m | daśa̭'gvāsaḥ | a॒bhi | a॒rca॒nti | a॒rkaiḥ | ga॒vyam | ci॒t | ū॒rvam | a॒pi॒dhāna̭'vantam | tam | ci॒t | nara̭ḥ | śaśa̭mānāḥ | a॒pa | vra॒n || 12 ||
navagvāsaḥ satrāgamanutiṣṭhanto ye navabhirmāsaiḥ samāpya gatāste navagvāḥ |
daśabhirmāsaiḥ samāpya ye gatāste daśagvāḥ |
ete ubhayavidhā aṅgirasaḥ sutasomāsaḥ abhiputasomāḥ santaḥ indram arkaiḥ arcanyaiḥ stotraiḥ abhyarcanti abhiṣṭuvanti |
tathā śaśamānāḥ stuvantaḥ naraḥ netāro'ṅgirasaḥ adhidhānavantaṃ balenāsureṇācchāditavantaṃ taṃ cit bahuṣu pradeśeṣu prasiddhamapi gavyaṃ cit gosambandhinamapi ūrvaṃ samūham apa vran apāvṛṇvan ||
parikrīnāmnyekāhe marutvatīyanikevalayoḥ sūktamuktīya 'katho nu te' ityādye dve ṛcau |
sūtritaṃ ca—'katho nu te pari carāṇi vidvāniti dve' (āśva. śrau. 9. 5) iti ||
1. ta-sambandhinaḥ | 2. ga-gha-ja-jha-ta-na-bha-ra-śa-tvā tvām | 3. pa-pha-ba-daśagvāsaḥ darśirmāsaiḥ | 4. kha-ca-ṇa-bahupradeśeṣu |
katho nu te pariṃ carāṇi vidvāntrīryā maghavantyā cakardha | yā co nu navyā kṛṇavaḥ śaviṣṭha pradu tā te vidatheṣu bravāma || 13 || katho iti | nu | te | pariṃ | carāṇi | vidvān | vīryā | maghavan | yā | cakardha | yā | co iti | nu | navyā | kṛṇavaḥ | śaviṣṭha | pra | it | ū~ iti | tā | te | vidatheṣu | bravāma || 13 ||
he maghavan dhanavannindra tvaṃ yā yāni vīryā vīryāṇi cakardha cakrupe ahaṃ tāni vīryāṇi vidvān jānan te tubhyaṃ katho nu kathaṃ nu pari carāṇi paricaryā karavāṇi |
he śaviṣṭha balavattmendra tvaṃ yā co yāni caiva navyā nabyāni nūtanāni vīryāṇi nu kṣipraṃ kṛṇavaḥ kuryāḥ te tvadīyāni tā tāni vīryāṇi vidatheṣu yajñeṣu preḍu bravāma praiva vadāma ||
etā viśvā cakravā~ indra bhūyaṃparīto januṣā vīryeṇa | yā citnu vajrinkṛṇavau dadhuṣvātra te vṛtā tarviṣpā asti tasyāḥ || 14 || etā | viśvā | cakra'vān | indra | bhūyaḥ | apariritaḥ | januṣā | vīryeṇa | yā | cit | nu | vajrin | kṛṇavaḥ | dadhuṣvāt | na | te | vṛtā | tarviṣpāḥ | asti | tasyāḥ || 14 ||
he indra apariritaḥ śatrubhiraparigatastvaṃ januṣā jananasiddhenātmīyena vīryeṇa balena bhūri bhūrīṇi bahūni etā etāni pratyakṣeṇopalabhyamānāni viśvā viśvāni vyāsāni bhuvanajātāni cakravān kṛtavānasi |
he vajrin vajravannindra dadhuṣvāt sapatnān dharṣayasva nu kṣipraṃ yā cit yāni ca viśvāni kṛṇavaḥ kuryāḥ te tvadīyasya tasyāḥ tarviṣpāḥ tasya balasya vṛtā nivārayitā na ko'pi asti ||
indra brahma kriyamāṇā jupasva yā tai śaviṣṭha navyā arkarma | vatraiva bhadrā sukṛtā vasūyū rathaṃ na dhīraḥ svapā atakṣam || 15 || indra | brahma | kriyamāṇā | jupasva | yā | te | śaviṣṭha | navyāḥ | arkam | vatra'iva | bhadrā | sukṛtā | vasu'yuḥ | ratham | na | dhīraḥ | svapāḥ | atakṣam || 15 ||

yatra sthāne viddho mriyate tanmarmetyucyate |
^asya vṛtrasya ^kratubhiḥ prajñānairhe indra tvaṃ ^vidadidat ajñāsīreva |
^yat yadā he ^sukṣatra subalendra tvaṃ ^madasya mādakasya somasya ^prabhṛtā prabhṛtau saṃbharaṇe sati ^yuyutsantaṃ yoddhumicchantam ^īm enaṃ vṛtraṃ ^harmye hārake ^tamasi ^dhāḥ nyadadhāḥ |
vṛtra indrasya bhayāt tamasi prāviśādityarthaḥ ||
1. kha-ca-ṇa-pravṛddhasya |
2. kha-jha-ra-pratidvandvarahitaḥ; ga-gha-ja-pa-ka-ba-pratibandharahitaḥ |
3. kha- jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-' śuṣṇaḥ ' nāsti |
4. ga-gha-ja-śa-' vṛtrasya ' nāsti |
5. kha-na-pa-ta-na-bha-ra- śa-śuṣṇamasuram |
tyaṃ cittithā katpayaṃ śaryānamasūrye tamasi vāvṛdhānam |
taṃ cinmandāno vṛṣabhaḥ sutasyocairindro apagurya jaghāna ||
6 ||
tyam | cit | itthā | katpayam | śaryānam | asūrye | tamasi | vāvṛdhānam | tam | cit | mandānaḥ | vṛṣabhaḥ | sutasya | uccaiḥ | indraḥ | apa'gurya | jaghāna || 6 ||
v bhutasya abhiputena somena v mandānaḥ modamānaḥ v vṛṣabhaḥ kāmānāṃ varṣitā v indraḥ v uccaiḥ ūrdhvam v apagurya vajramudyamya v tyaṃ cit tameva vṛtraṃ v jaghāna avadhīt |
kīdṛśam |
v itthā amugrāntarikṣaloke v katpayam |
kan sukhakaraṃ payo yasya tam |
v śaryānam a'su śayanaṃ kurvantam v asūrye sūryarahite v tamasi andhakāre v vavṛdhānaṃ vardhamānam |
v taṃ v cit iti punarvacanaṃ pūrṇārtham ||
32 ||
udyadindro mahate dāna
( tai. saṃ. 2. 5. 2. 2 ) iti hi taittirīyakam |
tadanantaraṃmantraḥ 'duryoṇe saṃgrāme 'mahatā prabhutena 'vadhena vajreṇa 'apādaṃ pādarahitam amatram' amātraṃ parimāṇarahitaṃ 'mṛdhavācaṃ hiṃsitavāgindriyam |
jṛmbhābhibhūtamimyarthaḥ |
'vṛtraṃ 'ni 'avṛṇak nitarāmahiṃsīt |
'tasmājjaabhyamānādadhīpomau nirakrāmatāṃ prāṇāpānau vā enaṃ tadajahitām' ( tai. saṃ. 2. 5. 2. 4 ) iti hi taittirīyakam ||
ko asya śuṣmaṃ tarviṣāṃ varāta eko dhanā bharate apratītaḥ |
ime cidasya jryaso nu devī indrasyaujaso bhiyasā jihāte ||
9 ||
kaḥ | asya | śuṣmam | tarviṣīm | varāte | ekaḥ | dhanā | bharate | apratiitaḥ | ime iti | cit | asya | jryasaḥ | nu | devī iti | indrasya | ojasaḥ | bhiyasā | jihāte iti || 9 ||
'asya indrasya saṃbandhanī 'śuṣmaṃ śopaṇī 'tarviṣīṃ balaṃ 'kaḥ 'varāte nivārayeta ||
śuṣmaśabdasya strīliṅgaviśeṣaṇatve'pi niyataliṅgatvāt puṃliṅgatvameva ||
'apratītaḥ kenāpyapratigata indraḥ 'ekaḥ asahāya eva 'dhanā dhanāni śatrūṇāṃ vasūni 'bharate bibharti harate vā |
'devī devyau dyotamāne 'ime 'cin evaṃ 'dyāvāpṛthivyau 'jryasaḥ vegavataḥ 'asya 'indrasya saṃbandhanaḥ 'ojasaḥ balādubhūtena 'bhiyasā bhayena 'nu kṣipraṃ 'jihāte gacchataḥ |
calata ityarthaḥ ||
nyasmai devī svadhitirjihītu indrāya gātuṃśutīva yeme |
saṃ yadojo yuvate viśvamābhiranu svadhānaṃ kṣitayo namanta ||
10 ||
ni | asmai | devī | svadhitiḥ | jihīte | indrāya | gātuḥ | uśatī'iva | yeme | sam | yat | ojaḥ | yuvate | viśvam | ābhiḥ | anu | svadhām | kṣitayaḥ | namanta || 10 ||
'svadhitiḥ svadhṛtiḥ svena dhṛtā 'devī dyotamānā dyauḥ 'asmai indrāya 'ni 'jihīte nīcatvena yāti |
'gātuḥ bhūmiḥ 'uśatīva kāmayamānā yoṣidiva 'indrāya 'yeme ātmānaṃ niyacchati |
yatheṣṭamātmānaṃ viniyoktuṃ yogyā bhavatītyarthaḥ |
'yat yadendraḥ 'viśvaṃ samastam 'ojaḥ balam 'ābhiḥ prajābhiḥ 'saṃ 'yuvate saṃyojayati |
indraḥ svakīyaṃ balaṃ sarvāsu prajāsu nihitavānityarthaḥ |
'anu ānukūlyena vartamānāḥ 'kṣitayaḥ manuṣyāḥ |
kṣitaya iti manuṣyānāmaitat |
'svadhām balavate indrāya 'namanta saṃnamanti prahvībhavanti ||
1. ga-gha-ja-ta-pa-pha-ba-atrama |
2 sva-ca-jha-ṇa-bha-ra-jambhābhibhūta |
3. jha-ra-tadajahitām; ta-tadajahitām |
4. kha-na-ṇa-bha-śoṣaṇam; na-śorṣiṇīm; bha-śoṣaṇam |
5. ga-gha-ja-ta-pa-pha-ba-ra-ime; śa-ete eva |
6. ma-ca-ṇa-niyoktum; jha-na-bha-ra-viyoktum |
7. sa-ca-jha-ṇa-bha-ra-svadhāme svadhājena |
8. na-pustakaṃ'sthānantarameva pāṭho dṛśyate- ekatrikanāmanyekāhe nidhenatyaśastre ekaṃ nu tveti sūktamukhyā |
sūtritaṃ ca-ekasya cinme vibhvastvo na ekaṃ nu tvā satapati pāśvajanyamiti |
ekaṃ nu tvā satpatiṃ pāścajanyaṃ jñātaṃ śṛṇomi yaśasaṃ janeṣu | taṃ me jagṛbhre āśaso narviṣṭhaṃ doṣā vastorhavamānāsa indram || 11 || ekam | nu | tvā | satpatim | pāścajanyam | jñātam | śṛṇomi | yaśasam | janeṣu | tam | me | jagṛbhre | ā'śasaḥ | narviṣṭham | doṣā | vastoḥ | havamānāsaḥ | indram || 11 ||
he indra tvā tvām ekaṃ nu mukhyameva janeṣu sarveṣu manuṣyeṣu madhye'haṃ mantradraṣṭā śṛṇomi ṛṣimukhyebhyaḥ |
kīdṛśam |
satpatiṃ satāṃ pālakaṃ pāścajanyaṃ pañcajanebhyo manuṣyebhyo hitaṃ jñātam utpannaṃ yaśasaṃ yaśoyuktam |
doṣā kṣapāyāṃ vastoḥ vāsare ca havamānāsaḥ stuvantaḥ āśasaḥ kāmanānāṃ śaṃsamānāḥ me madīyāḥ prajāḥ narviṣṭham atiśayena stutyaṃ tam indraṃ jagṛbhre gṛhntu svīkurvantu ||
evā hi tvāmṛtuthā yātayantaṃ maghā viprebhyo dadataṃ śṛṇomi | kiṃ te brahmaṇo gṛhate sakhāyo ye tvāyā nidadhuḥ kāmamindra || 12 || eva | hi | tvām | ṛtuthā | yātayantam | maghā | viprebhyaḥ | dadatam | śṛṇomi | kim | te | brahmaṇaḥ | gṛhate | sakhāyaḥ | ye | tvā'yā | nidadhuḥ | kāmam | indra || 12 ||
eva evamuktaprakāreṇa hi ṛtuthā kālekāle yātayantaṃ jantūn prerayantaṃ he indra tvāṃ viprebhyaḥ stotṛbhyaḥ maghā maghāni dhanāni dadataṃ yacchantaṃ śṛṇomi niśāmayāmi |
etanmayeva pratibhāti |
he indra tvāyā tvayi ye stotāraḥ kāmaṃ svakīyābhilāṣaṃ nidadhuḥ nyakṣipan brahmaṇaḥ nṛhantaḥ te tvadīyāḥ sakhāyaḥ stotāraḥ kiṃ gṛhate tvattaḥ kiṃ gṛhṇate |
ṛṣiḥ svakīyā bhilāpaprāptivilambanādevamuktvānetyarthaḥ ||

1 śa- ‘nu’ nāsti |
2. kha-ca-ṇa-bha-prajānāḥ ; jha-ra-prajanāḥ ; na-janāḥ |

tatrātreḥ pañcame maṇḍale tṛtīye'nuvāke dvādaśa sūktāni | tatra ‘mahi maha’ iti daśarcaṃ prathamaṃ sūktam | prajāpatiputraḥ saṃvaraṇaṛṣiḥ | triṣṭup chandaḥ | indro devatā | tathā cānukrāntaṃ— ‘mahi daśa prājāpatyaḥ saṃvaraṇaḥ’ | viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||

1 ||
mahi̭ | ma॒he | tava̭se | dī॒dhye | nṛn | i॒ndrāya̭ | i॒thā | tava̭se | a॒tarvyā̭n | yaḥ | a॒smai̭ | su॒mati̭m | vā॒rjasā̭tau | su॒taḥ | janḙ | sa॒ma

3 ||
na | te | te | indra | abhi | asmat | ṛṣva | ayuktāsaḥ | abrahmatā | yat | asa̭n |

brahma paribṛḍhaṃ karma |

bhago na bhaga iva havyaśrāruḥ |

1 ||

6 ||
papṛkṣeṇyam | indra | tve iti | hi | ojaḥ | nṛmṇāni | ca | nūtamānaḥ | amartaḥ | saḥ | naḥ | ernīm | vasavānaḥ | rayim | dāḥ | pra | aryaḥ | stuṣe | tuvi'maghasya | dānam ||6||

eva | naḥ | indra | ūti'bhiḥ | ava | pāhi | gṛṇataḥ | śūra | kārūn | uta | tvacam | dardataḥ | vāja'sātau | piprīhi | madhvaḥ | su'sutasya | cāroḥ || 7 ||

dvitīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī |

utkalakṣaṇena somapānena prīṇaya ||

8 ||
uta | tye | mā | pauru'kutsyasya | sūreḥ | trasadasyoḥ | hiraṇinaḥ | rarāṇāḥ | vahantu | mā | daśa | śvetāsaḥ | asya | gairi'kṣitasya | kratu'bhiḥ | nu | sastre || 8 ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-gantā; ga-gha-ja-saṃgato; ra-śa-saṃgatā |
2. ga-gha-ja-pa-pha-ba-ava pāhi |
3. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-bha-ra-sūkta |

caturthyarthe dvitīyā |
mahyamityarthaḥ |
ᵥraraṇāḥ ||

dattā ityarthaḥ |
ᵥpaurukutsyasya purukutsāpatyasya ᵥsureḥ prerakasya ᵥhiraṇinaḥ hiraṇya- vataḥ ᵥasya ᵥtrasadasyoḥ etannāmakasya rājarṣeḥ ᵥgairikṣitasya girikṣitagotrotpannasya ᵥiyetāsaḥ śubha- varṇāḥ ᵥdaśa aśvāḥ ᵥmā māṃ ᵥvahantu prāpayantu |
ahaṃ ca ᵥkratubhiḥ karmabhi rathaniyojanādibhiryajñā- dibhirvā ᵥnu kṣipraṃ ᵥsaśre gaccheyam ||

9 ||
uta | tye | mā | māruta'aśvasya | śoṇāḥ | kratvā'maghāsaḥ | vidathasya | rātau | sahasrā | me | cyavatānaḥ | dadānaḥ | ānuk | aryaḥ | vapuṣe | na | ārcat || 9 ||
ut api ca ᵥye te'śvā vakṣyamāṇāḥ ᵥmārutāśvasya |
marutsadṛśavegāśvāmmarutāśvaḥ |
tadapa- tyasya ᵥvidathasya etannāmakasya ᵥrājñaḥ ᵥśoṇāḥ śoṇavarṇāḥ ᵥkratvāmaghāsaḥ kratunā karmaṇā śīghragama- nādilakṣaṇena mahanīyā aśvāḥ ᵥrātau dāne viniyuktā māṃ vahantīti śeṣaḥ |
kiṃca ᵥmahnā mahnāni aparimitāni dhanāni ᵥaryaḥ pūjyasya ᵥme caturthyarthe ṣaṣṭhī |
pūjyāya mahyaṃ ᵥcyavatānaḥ cyāvayan ᵥdadānaḥ prayacchan saḥ ᵥānuk ābharaṇaṃ ca ᵥvapuṣe svaśarīrālaṃkārāya ᵥārcat prāyacchat |
ᵥna iti cārthe ||

10 ||
uta | tye | mā | dhvanyasya | juṣṭāḥ | lakṣmaṇyasya | su'rucaḥ | yatānāḥ | mahnā | rāyaḥ | su'varaṇasya | ṛṣeḥ | vrajam | na | gāvaḥ | pra'yatāḥ | arpi | gmana || 10 ||
ut api ca ᵥye te vakṣyamāṇāḥ rāyaḥ ᵥdhvanyasya dhvanyanāmakasya ᵥlakṣmaṇyasya lakṣmaṇa- putrasya rājñaḥ saṃbandhino'śvāḥ ᵥjuṣṭāḥ māṃ prāptāḥ ᵥsurucaḥ śobhanadīptayaḥ ᵥyatānāḥ vaha- nāya yatamānāḥ |
ᵥrāyaḥ ᵥmahnā mahattvena yuktā rāyaḥ ᵥprayatāḥ dattāḥ satyaḥ ᵥsaṃvaraṇasya ᵥṛṣeḥ ᵥapi ᵥgmana apigatāḥ prāptāḥ |

1. ta-'vidathasyaitannāmakasya' nāsti | 2. ga-gha-ja-ta-pa-pha-ba-śa-aśvāḥ vidathasya vedanasādhanasya | 3. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-'rātāṃ' nāsti | 4. ga-gha-ja-pa-pha-ba-ābhūṣe | 5. ga-gha-ja-mā | 6. kha-ga-gha-ca-ja-ṇa-pa-pha-ba-śa-vahamānā; ra-vahamānāya | 7. ga-gha-ja-pa-pha-ba-rāyo dhanāni |

1 ||
ajāta'śatrum | ajarā | svaḥ'vatī | anu | svadhā | amitā | dusmam | īyate | sunotan | pacata | brahma'vāhase | puru'stutāya | pra'taram | dadhātana || 1 ||
vajātaśatrum |
anuṣaṅgāḥ śatayitāro yasya tam |

tan: śubhā śobhanīyā alaṃ- kārādibhi: ^yasya tam |
tādṛśaṃ svapoṣakam ayajvānam ^apa ^ūhati |
aparo'paśabda: pūraṇa: |
^madhavā dhanavān ^ya: ca ^kavāsava: kutsitapuruṣasahāyastamapohatīti ||
yasyāvadhītpitaraṃ yasya mātaraṃ yasya śakro ārtaraṃ nārta īṣate |
vetīdvasya paryatā yataṃ kuro na kilbiṣādīṣate vasva ākara: |
4 |
yasya | avadhīt | pitaram | yasya | mātaram | yasya | śakra: | bhrātaram | na | ārta: | īṣate | veti | it | ū~ iti | asya | pra'ryatā | yatam'kara: | na | kilbiṣāt | īṣate | vasva: | | ākara: || 4 ||
^śakra: śakko'ya ^yasya ayajvana: ^pitaraṃ ^avadhīt hanvān |
^yasya ca ^mātaram avadhīt |
^yasya ca ^bhrātaraṃ ^ata: asmādayajvana: sakāśān ^na ^īṣate |
na bibheti na ^gacchati ^vā |
kiṃtu ^asya ^prayatā pradattāni ^havīṃṣi ^vetīti kāmayata eva |
ayajvānaṃ śikṣayitvā niyojaya- tīyartha: |
^yaṃtakara: ^yamanakartā ^vasva: vasuna: dhanasya ^ākara: ābhimukhyakartā ya: ^kilbiṣāt pitrādivadhayuktāt ^na ^īṣate na calati na bibheti vā |
indrasya amnotṇāṃ ^hani: ‘indro yatīn sālāvṛkebhya: prāyacchat antarikṣe paulomān pṛthivyāṃ kālakāñjānanmukhyānyatīn sālāvṛkebhya: prāyaccham’ ityādi śrutiṣu prasiddhā ||
na paśvabhirdaśabhirvṛṣṭyārabhaṃ nāsunvatā sacate puṣyatā cana |
jināti vedamayā hanti vā dhunirā devayuṃ bhajāti gormati vraje |
5 |
na | paśva'bhi: | daśa'bhi: | vṛṣṭi | ā'rabham | na | asunvatā | sacate | puṣyatā | cana | jināti | vā | it | amuyā | hanti | vā | dhuni: | ā | deva'yum | bhajāti | go'mati | vraje || 5 ||
1. kha-ca-jña-ṇa-na-bha-alaṃkaraṇādibhi: |
ga-gha-ja-alaṃkārā: |
2. kha-ca-jña-ṇa-ta-na-ma-ra-yajvānam |
3. kha-ca-ṇa-bha-āporhati; ga-gha-ja-pa-pha-ba-apo apohati |
4. kha-ca-jña-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-‘dhanavān’ nāsti |
5. a-ta-ra-yajvana: |
6. kha-jha-necchati |
7. ga-gha-ja-śa-dattāni |
8. ta-yataṃkara: yamanakara: |
9. kha-ca-jña-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-pitrādidhanayuktāt; ga-gha-ja-pa-pha-ba-pitrādivat; śa-pitrādinā vadhayuktāt |
10. kha-ca-jña-ṇa-ta-na-bha-stotṛṇām |
ayamindraḥ pañcabhirdevaśabhiḥ vā sahāyaiḥ ārabham ārambhaṃ śatruhananāyālambanaṃ na vaṣṭi sāhāyyaṃ nāpekṣata ityarthaḥ |
yadvā |
amumindraṃ pañcabhirdaśabhirvā yajñādanyairupāyaiḥ ārabham ārabhyamuyogaṃ kartuṃ na vaṣṭi na kāmayate |
asunvatā abhipavamakurvatā saha puṣyatā cana bandhādīnapoṣayatāpi na sacate na saṃgacchate |
asunvantam abhipavamakurvāṇam ayaṣṭāram apumāntaṃ ca na prāpnotītyarthaḥ |
na kevalamaprāptimātraṃ kiṃtu jināti vā |
vāśabdaścārthe |
jināti ca |
bādhate |
ita iti pūraṇaḥ |
hanti vā amuyā amumayaṣṭāraṃ dhuniḥ kampakaḥ śṛṇutām |
devayuṃ devamindraṃ kāmayamānaṃ yajamānaṃ gomati vraje ā bhajati yojayatītyarthaḥ ||
3 ||
vitvakṣaṇaḥ samṛtau cakramāsajo'sunvato virṣuṇaḥ sunvato vṛdhaḥ |
indro viśvasya damitā vibhīṣaṇo yathāvaśaṃ nayati dāsmārthaḥ ||
6 ||
vitvakṣaṇaḥ | sam'ṛtau | cakram'āsajaḥ | asunvataḥ | virṣuṇaḥ | sunvataḥ | vṛdhaḥ |
indraḥ | viśvasya | damitā | vibhīṣaṇaḥ | yathā'vaśam | nayati | dāsam | āryaḥ || 6 ||
samṛtau saṃgrāme vitvakṣaṇaḥ viśeṣeṇa tankartā śatrūṇāṃ tadarthaṃ cakramāsajaḥ rathacakrasyā- saṃrjayatā asunvataḥ ayajamānasya virṣuṇaḥ parāṅmukhaḥ sunvataḥ yajamānasya vṛdhaḥ vardhayitā indraḥ viśvasya damitā śikṣayitā vibhīṣaṇaḥ bhayajanakaḥ āryaḥ svāmī yathāvaśaṃ yathecchaṃ dāsaṃ dāmakarmāṇaṃ janaṃ nayati svavaśam ||
samīṃ paṇerajati bhojanaṃ muṣe vi dāśuṣe bhajati sūnaraṃ vasu |
durge cana dhriyate viśva ā puru jano yo asya tarvipīmacukudhat ||
7 ||
sam | īm | paṇeḥ | ajati | bhojanam | muṣe | vi | dāśuṣe | bhajati | sūnaram | vasu |
duḥ'ge | cana | dhriyate | viśvaḥ | ā | puru | janaḥ | yaḥ | asya | tarvipīm | acukudhat ||7||
īm ayamindraḥ paṇeḥ vaṇija iva lubdhakasyādātuḥ bhojanaṃ dhanamannaṃ vā muṣe corayituṃ sam ajati samyak gacchati |
gatvā cādāya dāśuṣe havirdātre yajamānāya vi bhajati sūnaraṃ śobhanamanuṣyaṃ vasu dhanam |
ayaṣṭenamākṛṣya yaṣṭe prayacchatītyarthaḥ |
na kevalaṃ vaṇigdhana- pradānamātraṃ kiṃtu puru puruṇi durge cana duḥkhena gantavye apyapi viśvaḥ sarvo'pi dātṛjanaḥ ā dhriyate āsthāpyate |
yaḥ janaḥ asya indrasya tarvipīṃ balam acukudhat vihitakaraṇādinā tasya paṇerajatīti pūrvaśeṣaḥ |
yadvā |
yo'sya tarvipīmacukudhat sa sarvo'pi jano durge canādhriyata indreṇa ||
canetyekapadamadhyetṛsaṃpradāyaprāptam ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-bha-ra-janaṃ ' nāsti | 2. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-bha-ra-vaṇijāmava | 3. ma-ca-ṇa- vaṇikapradāna | 4. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-bha-cana duḥkhena; ra-ca duḥkhena na | 5. jha-na-bha-ra-janaḥ ' nāsti |
saṃ yajanau sudhanau viśvaśardhasāvedindrau maghavā goṣu śubhriṣu |
yujaṃ hyanyamakṛta pravepanyudīṃ gavyaṃ sṛjate satvabhirdhuniḥ ||
8 ||
saṃ | yat | janau | sudhanau | viśva'śardhasau | avait | indraḥ | magha'vā | goṣu | śubhriṣu | yujam | hi | anyam | akṛta | pra'vepanī | uta | īm | gavyam | sṛjate | satvabhiḥ | dhuniḥ ||8||
vmam vaven maṃjānāti vyat yadā vjanau parasparapratidvandvinau vsudhanau śobhanadhanau vviśvaśardhasau vyāsabalau bahutsāhau vā vmaghavā dhanavān vindraḥ vgoṣu vśubhriṣu śobhanāsu goṣu nimittabhūtāsu vanyaṃ yantāraṃ vyujaṃ sahāyam vakṛta kṛtavān vpravepanī śatrūṇāṃ pravepanavān |
vīm asmai vgavyaṃ gosamūham vuta vsṛjate vsatvabhiḥ marudbhiḥ saha vdhuniḥ meghānāṃ kampayitā |
indro yajamānam ayajamānaṃ vā yaṃ jānāti gonimittaṃ jñātvā yajvane gosamūhaṃ prayacchatītyarthaḥ ||
sahasrāmāgniveśi gṛṇīṣe śatrimaya upamāṃ ketumaryaḥ |
tasmā āpaḥ saṃyateḥ pīpayanta tasminkṣatramamavaceṣamastu ||
9 ||
sahasra'sām | āgniveśim | gṛṇīṣe | śatrima | agne | upa'mām | ketum | aryeḥ | tasmai | āpaḥ | sam'yataiḥ | pīpayanta | tasmin | kṣatram | ama'vat | tveṣam | astu ||9||
agniveśisutaṃ śatrimaṃsyātdhanapradam |
āśāsta ṛṣiranyarcā prajāpatisutaḥ sudhīḥ ||
varthaḥ : ahaṃ vsahasramām aparimitadhanapradam vāgniveśim agniveśisutaṃ vśatrima etannāmakaṃ rājarṣi vgṛṇīṣe gṛṇe staumi |
he vagne añjanādiguṇaviśiṣṭendra |
kīdṛśaṃ tam |
vupamām upamānabhūtaṃ vketuṃ prajñāpakaṃ prakhyātamityarthaḥ |
vtasmā vāpaḥ vsaṃyataḥ samyak gacchantyaḥ vpīpayanta pyāyantām |
vtasmin rājñi vkṣatraṃ dhanam vamavat balasahitaṃ vtveṣaṃ dīptam vastu iti ||
4 ||
‘ yaste sādhiṣṭaḥ ’ ityetacca tṛtīyaṃ sūktam |
aṅgirogotraḥ prabhavasurnāma ṛṣiḥ |
anyā paṅktiḥ |
paṅktyantaparibhāṣayā śiṣṭā anuṣṭubhaḥ |
indro devatā |
‘ yaste'ṣṭau prabhavasurāṅgirasaḥ paṅktyantam ’ ityanukrāntam |
sūktaviniyogo laiṅgikaḥ |
ābhilavikeṣvatheṣu tṛtīyamavane ādha- stvocchāvākasya vaikalpiko'nurūpaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ yaste sābhiṣṭo'vase purāṃ bhindyuvā kaviḥ ’ ( ādha. śrau. 7. 8 ) iti |
dvitīyatṛtīyayoḥ svarasāpnoścācau tṛcāvanurūpau |
‘ yaste sābhiṣṭo'vasa iti ṣaḍanuṣṭubhaḥ ’ ( ādha. śrau. 8. 5 ) iti hi sūtritam ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-nonimittam | 2. ga-gha-ja-pa-pha-ba-arthaḥ svāmī | 3. ga-gha-ja-pa-pha- ba-puṣyantu | 4. ga-gha-ja-pa-pha-ba-balamavat gṛhayuktam |
yastḙ sādhi॒ṣṭho'va̭sa indra kratu॒mā bhḙra |
a॒smabhya̭ṃ carṣaṇī॒saha̭ṃ sā॒smin vā॒jeṣṷ du॒stara̭m ||
1 ||
yaḥ | te | sādhiṣṭhaḥ | avase | indra | kratum | ā | bhara | asmabhyam | carṣaṇī'saham | sāsmin | vājeṣu | dustaram || 1 ||
he indra te tava yaḥ sādhiṣṭhaḥ atiśayena sādhakaḥ kratuḥ ukthalakṣaṇaṃ karma prajñā vāsti taṃ kratuṃ naḥ avase asmadrakṣaṇāya asmabhyam ā bhara āhara |
kīdṛśaṃ tam |
carṣaṇīsahaṃ sarveṣāṃ manuṣyāṇāmabhibhavitāraṃ sāsmi saṃbhrātaṃ śuddhaṃ vājeṣu saṃgrāmeṣu dustaram anyairanabhibhāvyam ||
yadi̭ndra te cata̭sro yacchū॒ra sa̭nti ti॒sraḥ |
yadvā॒ pañca̭ kṣitī॒nāmava̭statsu॒ na ā bhḙra ||
2 ||
yat | indra | te | catasraḥ | yat | śūra | santi | tisraḥ | yat | vā | pañca | kṣitīnām | avaḥ | tat | su | naḥ | ā | bhara || 2 ||
he indra te tava yat yā utayaḥ catamaḥ caturṣu varṇeṣu saṃśritāḥ santi yat yāśca śūra tisraḥ tripu lokeṣu vartamānāḥ yadvā |
vāśabdaścārthe |
yat yāśca pañca kṣitīnāṃ pañcajana- sambandhinya utayaḥ santi tat sarvam avaḥ rakṣaṇaṃ su suṣṭhu naḥ asmabhyam ā bhara āhareti ||
ā tḙvo varḙṇyaṃ vṛ॒ṣanta̭masya hū॒mahe |
vṛ॒ṣajṷ̄tirhi ja॒jñiṣe॒ ābhṷ̄bhi॒rindra̭ tu॒rvaṇi̭ḥ ||
3 ||
ā | te | avaḥ | vareṇyam | vṛṣantasya | hūmahe | vṛṣajūtiḥ | hi | jajñiṣe | ābhūbhiḥ | indra | turvaṇiḥ || 3 ||
he indra vṛṣantasya phalanāṃ varṣakatasya te tava avaḥ rakṣaṇaṃ gamanaṃ vā vareṇyaṃ varaṇīyam ā hūmahe āhvayāmaḥ |
he indra vṛṣajūtiḥ varṣaṇagamanaḥ turvaṇiḥ tṛṇavanihiṃsako vā tvam ābhūbhiḥ sarvato bhavadbhiḥsaimarhadbhiḥ saha avaḥ hi jajñiṣe utpādayesi khalu |
te'va āhvayāma iti |
yadvā |
vṛṣagamanaḥ san yasmādyajñāmāgatya phalaṃ jajñiṣe tasmātte'vo hūmaha iti ||
1. kha-jha-catasṛṣu; ga-gha-ja-pa-pha-ba-rakṣāḥ catasraḥ catasṛṣu; ta-ra-catasraḥ catasṛṣu |
2. kha-ca-jha- ṇa-na-bha-ra-'yat' nāsti |
3. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-'yadvā' nāsti |
4. kha-va-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra- abho rakṣaṇaṃ tatsarvam |
5. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-yāhi |

vṛṣā hyasi rādhase jajñiṣe vṛṣṇi te śarvaḥ | svakṣatraṃ te dhṛpanmanaḥ satrāhāmindra pauryam || 4 || vṛṣā | hi | asi | rādhase | jajñiṣe | vṛṣṇi | te | śarvaḥ | sva'kṣatram | te | dhṛpat | manaḥ | satrā'ham | indra | pauryam || 4 ||
ha indra rādhase yajamānānāṃ samṛddhaye vṛṣā hyasi phalasya varṣako'si khalu |
yadvā |
vṛṣā svadhami |
rādhase dhanārthaṃ jajñiṣe utpadyase |
te ścavaḥ balaṃ vṛṣṇi varṣitṛ |
te manaḥ svakṣatraṃ svāyattabalaṃ dhṛpat varṣakaṃ virodhinam |
he indra te pauryaṃ puṃstvaṃ satrā'haṃ saṃghahantu ||
tvaṃ tamindra martyamamitrayanta madrivaḥ | sarvathā śatakrato ni yāhi śavasaspate || 5 || tvam | tam | indra | martyam | amitrayantam | adrivaḥ | sarva'rathā | śatakrato iti śata'krato | ni | yāhi | śavasaḥ | pate || 5 ||
he indra tvaṃ tam amitrayantaṃ śatru
he ᵥindra ᵥasmākaṃ ᵥrathaṃ rahanasvabhāvaṃ vā putram ᵥava rakṣa |
kīdṛśaṃ ratham |
ᵥduṣṭaraṃ duḥkhena taraṇīyam ᵥājisu saṃgrāmeṣu ᵥpuroyāvānaṃ purato miśrayitāraṃ ᵥmayāvānam, anucaraiḥ saha gantāraṃ ᵥdhanedhane sarveṣu dhaneṣu ᵥvājayantaṃ saṃgrāmaṃ dhanaṃ vecchantam ||
asmākaṃmindrehiṃ no rathamavā puraṃdhyā | vayaṃ śaviṣṭha vāryaṃ divi śravau dadhīmahi divi stomaṃ manāmahe || 8 || asmākam | indra | ā | ihi | naḥ | ratham | ava | puram'dhyā |
vayam | śaviṣṭha | vāryam | divi | śravaḥ | dadhīmahi | divi | stomam | manāmahe || 8 ||
he ᵥindra ᵥasmākam asmadīya eva san ᵥehi āgaccha |
āgamya ca ᵥpuraṃdhyā śobhanabuddhayā ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥratham ᵥava rakṣa |
ᵥvayaṃ ca he ᵥśaviṣṭha atiśayena balavan ᵥvadanugrahāna ᵥvāryaṃ varaṇīyaṃ ᵥśravaḥ annaṃ kīrtiṃ vā ᵥdivi dyotamāne ᵥyavi ᵥdadhīmahi sthāpayāmaḥ |
tathā ᵥdivi ᵥyavi ᵥstomaṃ stotraṃ ᵥmanāmahe karavāmahe ityarthaḥ ||
6 ||
‘sa ā gamat’ iti ṣaṭkaṃ caturthaṃ sūktam | āṅgirasaḥ prabhuvamṛṣiḥ | tṛtīyā jagatī śiṣṭā anuṣṭup triṣṭubhaḥ | indro devatā | anukāntaṃ ca—‘sa ā gamat ṣaṭ tṛtīyā jagatī’ iti || viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||
sa ā gamadindro yo vasūnāṃ ciketadhātuṃ dāmano rayīṇām | dhanvacaro na vaṃsagastṛpāṇaścakamānaḥ pibatu dugdhamaṃśum || 1 || saḥ | ā | ᵥgamat | indraḥ | yaḥ | ᵥvasūnām | ᵥciketat | ᵥdātum | ᵥdāmanaḥ | ᵥrayīṇām | ᵥdhanva'caraḥ | na | ᵥvaṃsagaḥ | ᵥtṛpāṇaḥ | ᵥcakamānaḥ | ᵥpibatu | ᵥdugdham ᵥaṃśum || 1 ||
ᵥsaḥ ᵥindraḥ asmadyajñaṃ prati ᵥā ᵥgamat āgacchatu |
ᵥyaḥ devaḥ ᵥvasūnāṃ dhanānām ||
karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī ||
vasūni dhanāni ᵥciketat jānāti pradātum |
sa kīdṛśaḥ |
ᵥdāmanaḥ dātā dānamanā vā ᵥrayīṇāṃ dhanānām |
gamane dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
ᵥdhanvacaraṃ ᵥna dhanuṣā saha saṃcaran dhānuṣka iva ᵥvaṃsagaḥ vananīyagamanaḥ ᵥtṛpāṇaḥ atyantaṃ tṛpitaḥ ᵥcakamānaḥ kāmayamānaśca san ᵥdugdham abhipūtam ᵥaṃśuṃ somaṃ ᵥpibatu ||
ā te hanū harivaḥ śūra śipre ruhatsomo na parvatasya pṛṣṭhe | anu tvā rājanbharvato na hinvan gīrbhirmadema puruhūta viśvai || 2 || ā | te | hanū iti | ᵥharivaḥ | ᵥśūra | śipre iti | ᵥruhat | somaḥ | na | ᵥparvatasya | ᵥpṛṣṭhe | ᵥanu | ᵥtvā | ᵥrājan | ᵥarvataḥ | na | ᵥhinvan | ᵥgīḥ'bhiḥ | ᵥmadema | ᵥpuru'hūta | ᵥviśvai || 2 ||
he indra he vharivaḥ harimyāṃ tadvan vśūra vte tava vhan hanasādhane |
yadyapyayaṃ śiprāvacanaḥ tathāpi punaḥ śiprābhidhānāt gauṇo'vagantavyaḥ |
utkarūpe saṃhate vā vśipre vā vruhat ārohatu prāpnotu vsomaḥ asmadat |
vparvatasya vpṛṣṭhe vna rahat nārohati |
tatra na tiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ |
yadvā |
naśabdaḥ purastātprayukto'pyathopamārthaḥ |
parvatasya pṛṣṭhe uparipradeśe somo na |
tatra yathā visrambheṇa prasarati tadvat |
he vrājan rājamānendra vtvā svām vanu tvayā saha ||
'tṛtīyārthe' ityanayoḥ karma- pravacanīyatvam ||
vviśve vayam varvato vna aśvāniva tṛṇādibhiḥ vgīrbhiḥ stutibhiḥ vhvinvan hvinvantaḥ tvāṃ prīṇayantaḥ santaḥ vmadema hṛṣyema |
he vpuruhūta bahuprakāraṃ bahubhirvāhūtendra ||
cakraṃ na vṛttaṃ puruhūta vepate manau bhiyā me amateridadivaḥ |
rathādadhi tvā jaritā sadāvṛdha kuvitnu stoṣnamaghavanpuruvasuḥ ||
3 ||
cakram | na | vṛttam | puru'huta | vepate | manaḥ | bhiyā | me | amateḥ | ita | adrivaḥ | rathāt | adhi | tvā | jaritā | sadā'vṛdha | kuvit | nu | stoṣṭa | maghavan | puru'vasuḥ ||3||
he vpuruhūta bahubhirāhūta he vadrivaḥ vajravannindra vme vmanaḥ vamaterin dāridyādastoturvā sakāśāt vbhiyā bhītyā vvepate kampate |
vvṛttaṃ vcakraṃ vna bhūmau vartamānaṃ cakrabhiva |
yasmādevaṃ tasmān vrathādadhi upari sthitaṃ vtvā tvāṃ he vsadāvṛdha sarvadā vardhamāna he vmaghavan indra vjaritā stotā vpuruvasuḥ ahampi vkuvit bahu vnu kṣipraṃ vstoṣṭan stoṣyati stūyāt ||
atra kuvicchandayoge'pi ākhyātasya bahugrahaṇātnighātaḥ ||
eṣa grāveva jaritā taṃ indraryarti vācaṃ bṛhadāśuṣāṇaḥ |
pra sūnyena maghavanyaṃsi rāyaḥ pra dakṣiṇaddarivo mā vi vainaḥ ||
4 ||
eṣaḥ | grāvā'iva | jaritā | te | indra | iyarti | vācam | bṛhat | āśuṣāṇaḥ | pra | sūnyena | maghavan | yaṃsi | rāyaḥ | pra | dakṣiṇat | hari'vaḥ | mā | vi | venaḥ || 4 ||
veṣaḥ vjaritā garitā stotā vgrāveva abhivāimeva |
sa yathā rasaṃ janayati tadvat he vindra vte tubhyaṃ vvācaṃ stutim viyarti prerayatītyarthaḥ |
vbṛhata mahat prabhūtaṃ vphalam vāśuṣāṇaḥ āśu saṃbhaktā san he vmaghavan vsadhyena hastena vrāyaḥ dhanāni vpra vyaṃsi prayacchāmi |
vdakṣiṇat dakṣiṇenāpi vpra yaṃsiti śeṣaḥ |
vmā vvi vvenaḥ vigatakāmaṃ mā kārṣīḥ ||
1 kha-ca jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra--prabhūtabalam |
vṛṣā tvā vṛṣaṇaṃ vardhatu dyaurvṛṣā vṛṣabhyāṃ vahase haribhyām |
sa no vṛṣā vṛṣarathaḥ suśiprā vṛṣakrato vṛṣā vājinbharai dhāḥ ||
5 ||
vṛṣā | tvā | vṛṣaṇam | vardhatu | dyauḥ | vṛṣā | vṛṣa'bhyām | vahase | hari'bhyām | saḥ | naḥ | vṛṣā | vṛṣa'rathaḥ | su'śiprā | vṛṣakrato iti vṛṣa'krato | vṛṣā | vājin | bharai | dhāḥ ||5||
he indra tvā tvām vṛṣā varṣitā dyauḥ dyotayitrī stutiḥ ||
dyotadīvyatervā stutyarthasyedaṃ rūpam ||
vṛṣaṇaṃ kāmānāṃ varṣakaṃ tvāṃ vardhatu vardhayatu |
vṛṣā tvaṃ vṛṣamyāṃ sektṛbhyāṃ haribhyāṃ vahase yajñaṃ pratyuhyase |
he suśiprā śobhanahano vṛṣakrato varṣakarman he vajrin vajrayudhendra saḥ tvaṃ vṛṣā vṛṣarathaḥ ca san naḥ asmān bhare saṃgrāme bharaṇāti yajñe vā dhāḥ dhāraya ||
yo rohitau vājinau vājinīvāntribhiḥ śataiḥ sacamānādiṣṭa |
yūne samasmai kṣitayo namantāṃ śrutarathāya maruto duvoyā ||
6 ||
yaḥ | rohitau | vājinau | vājinī'vān | tri'bhiḥ | śataiḥ | sacamānaḥ | ādiṣṭa | yūne | sam | asmai | kṣitayaḥ | namantām | śruta'rathāya | marutaḥ | duvaḥ'yā || 6 ||
iyaṃ śrutarathasya rājño dānastutiḥ |
yaḥ rājā vājinīvān annavān śrutarathaḥ rohitau rohitavarṇau vājinau vejanavantāvaśvau tribhiḥ śataiḥ gavāṃ dhanānāṃ vā uktasaṃkhyābhiḥ sacamānau saṃgacchamānau adiṣṭa ||
dadāteḥ diśatervā luḍīdaṃ rūpam ||
yūne sarvatra miśrayitre niyatarunāya vā asmai śrutarathāya kṣitayaḥ sarvāḥ prajāḥ duvoyā tṛtīyaikavacanasya yājādeśaḥ ||
paricaryayā saṃ namantāṃ praṇatā bhavantu |
sevantāmityarthaḥ |
he marutaḥ iti indrasahāyabhūtānāṃ marutāṃ saṃbodhanam ||
7 ||
‘saṃ bhānunā’ iti pañcarcaṃ pañcamaṃ sūktamaṃtrāpya traiṣṭubhamaindram |
‘saṃ bhānunā pañcātriḥ’ ityanukramaṇikā |
viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||
saṃ bhānunā yatate sūryasyājuhāno ghṛtapṛṣṭhaḥ svaśvāḥ |
tasmā amṛdhrā uṣaso vyucchānya indrāya sunavāmetyāha ||
1 ||
sam | bhānunā | yatate | sūryasya | ā'juhānaḥ | ghṛta'pṛṣṭhaḥ | su'aśvāḥ | tasmai | amṛdhrāḥ | uṣasaḥ | vi | ucchān | yaḥ | indrāya | sunavāma | iti | āha || 1 ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-vṛṣa |
2. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-śa-dāteḥ |
ᵥsūryasya ᵥbhānunā tejasā saha ᵥājuhvānaḥ sarvatra hūyamānaḥ ||
karmaṇi kartṛpratyayaḥ ||
ᵥghṛtapṛṣṭhaḥ pradīptajvālo ghṛtayuktapṛṣṭhapradeśo vā ᵥsvapnāḥ svapno'gniḥ ᵥsaṃ ᵥyatate samyak prayatnaṃ karoti |
udayakāle hyagnayaḥ prajvālyante |
tasmāt kāraṇāt ᵥyaḥ ᵥyajamānaḥ ᵥindrāya ᵥsunavāmetyāha brūte'dhvaryuṃ prati ᵥtasmai yajamānāya ᵥamṛdhāḥ ahiṃsitāḥ ᵥupasaḥ ᵥvyucchān vyucchantu |
udayakāle'gniḥ prajvālitastasmāt somayāgaṃ kurma iti yo brūte tasya sudināni bhavantītyarthaḥ ||
samiddhāgnirvatastīrṇābarhiryuktāgrāvā sutasomo jarāte |
grāvāṇo yasyeṣmiṃ vadantyadadhvaryurhaviṣāva sindhum ||
2 ||
'sa 'rājā 'na 'vyathate vyathito na bhavati |
'yasmin' rājani tadīye yajñe 'indraḥ 'tīvraṃ madajanakaṃ 'somaṃ 'pibati 'gosakhāyam |
vikāre prakṛtiśabdaḥ |
kṣīrādimiśraṇavantam |
sa rājā 'satvanaiḥ satvabhiranucaraiḥ 'ā sarvataḥ 'ajati gacchati |
'hanti ca 'vṛtraṃ pāpaṃ vairiṇaṃ vā |
'kṣeti gacchati 'kṣitāḥ prajāḥ |
athavā kṣitīrnivāsānnavasate |
'subhagaḥ śobhanamukhaḥ 'nāma nāmakaṃ dhanamindrasya stotraṃ vā 'puṣyan poṣayan |
puṣyātkṣemai abhi yoge bhavātyubhe vṛtau saṃyatī saṃ jayāti |
priyaḥ sūryai priyo agnā bhavāti ya indrāya sutasomo dadāśat |
5 |
puṣyāt | kṣemaṃ | abhi | yoge | bhavāti | ubhe iti | vṛtau | saṃyatī iti sañyatī | sam | jayāti | priyaḥ | sūryai | priyaḥ | agnā | bhavāti | yaḥ | indrāya | sutasoma
