ṛgveda:
(a. 4. a. 2. va. 9)
he ˅indra ˅śaviṣṭha atiśayena balavan ˅yat ˅iṣam annaṃ ˅śravāyyaṃ śravaṇīyam ˅īm sarvataḥ ˅dadhiṣe dhārayasi dadāsi vā ||
iṣaśabdo napuṃsakaliṅgo'pyasti ||
haṃ ˅hiraṇyavarṇa indra tadannaṃ ˅paprathe prathate |
katham |
˅dīrghaśruttamam atidūraśravaṇīyatamam |
sarvatra kāryamityarthaḥ |
˅duṣṭaram anabhi- bhāvyamulakṣaṇaṃ prathate ||
śuṣmaso ye teṃ adrivo mehanā ketusārpaḥ |
ubhā devāvamiṣṭaye divaśca gmaśca rājathaḥ ||
3 ||
śuṣmasaḥ | ye | te | adrivaḥ | mehanā | ketu'sārpaḥ | ubhā | devau | abhiṣṭaye | divaḥ | ca | gmaḥ | ca | rājathaḥ || 3 ||
he ˅adrivaḥ vajravanninrda ˅te tvadīyāḥ ˅ye ˅śuṣmāsaḥ balabhūtā marutaḥ santi |
kīdṛśāste |
˅mehanā mahanīyāḥ ˅ketusāpaḥ prajñāpakaṃ karmāntarikṣaṃ vā spṛśantaḥ |
˅ubhā? ubhau ˅devau tvaṃ ca marutsamūhaśca ˅ityubhau yuvāṃ ˅divaśca dyulokāt ˅gmaśca bhūmeḥ sakāśāt ˅abhiṣṭaye abhito gamanāya ˅rājathaḥ īśāthe ||
utā nau asya kasya cidakṣasya tavaṃ vṛtrahan |
asmabhyaṃ nṛmṇamā bharāsmabhyaṃ nṛmaṇasyase ||
4 ||
uta | iti | naḥ | asya | kasya | cit | dakṣasya | tava | vṛtra'han | asmabhyam | nṛmṇam | ā | bhara | asmabhyam | nṛ'manasyase || 4 ||
˅uto api ca he ˅vṛtrahan indra ˅naḥ asmabhyaṃ ˅kasya ˅cit anirdeśyasya ˅dakṣasya pravṛddhasya dāturvā ˅asya ˅tava svabhūtebhyaḥ ˅asmabhyam |
punaḥ śrutirādarāthī |
˅nṛmṇaṃ nṛmaṇaṃ dhanam ˅ā ˅bhara āhara |
he indra yatastvam ˅asmabhyaṃ ˅nṛmaṇasyase dhanamicchasi ||
nū taṃ ābhirabhiṣṭibhiristva śarm'chatakrato |
indra syāma sugopāḥ śūra syāma sugopāḥ ||
5 ||
nu | te | ābhiḥ | abhiṣṭi'bhiḥ | tava | śarman | śatakrato iti śata'krato | indra | syāma | su'gopāḥ | śūra | syāma | su'gopāḥ || 5 ||
1. sva-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra- 'ubhā' nāsti |
2. śa-marudgaśca |
he ᵛśatakrato indra ᵛte tava ᵛābhiḥ ᵛabhiṣṭibhiḥ abhigamanaiḥ ᵛnu śīghraṃ samṛddhā bhavemeti śeṣaḥ |
he ᵛindra ᵛtava ᵛśarman śarmaṇi sukhe mati ᵛsugopāḥ ᵛsyāma bhavema |
he ᵛśūra tava śarmaṇi ᵛsugopāḥ śobhanarakṣaṇāḥ ᵛsyāma bhavema |
punaruktirādarārthā ||
9||
‘yadindra citra’ iti pañcarcaṃ saptamaṃ sūktamatrerārṣamandram |
anyā paṅktiḥ śiṣṭāḥ ṣaḍvyanta- paribhāṣayānuṣṭubhaḥ |
‘yadindra paṅktyantam’ ityanukramaṇikā |
ābhilavikeṣvatheṣu tṛtīyasavane ādya- stṛco'cchāvākasya vaikalpikaḥ stotriyaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘yadindra citra mehanā yate sādhiṣṭhā'vase’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 8 ) iti ||
yadindra citra mehanāsti tvādātadrivaḥ |
rādhastanno vidadvasa ubhayāhastayā bhara ||
1 ||
yat | indra | citra | mehanā | asti | tvādātam | adrivaḥ | rādhaḥ | tat | naḥ | vidadvaso iti vidadvaso | ubhayāhasti | ā | bhara || 1 ||
he ᵛcitra cāyanīya he ᵛadrivaḥ vajravan ᵛindra ᵛyata ᵛmehanā mahanāya ᵛtvādāta tvayā dātavyaṃ ᵛrādhaḥ dhanam ᵛasti ᵛnan dhanaṃ he ᵛvidadvaso labdhadhanendra ᵛnaḥ asmabhyam ᵛubhayā- hasti ubhābhyāṃ hastābhyām ᵛā ᵛbhara āhara |
atra ‘yadindra citra cāyanīya mahanāya dhanamasti’ (niru.4.4 ) ityādiniruktaṃ draṣṭavyam ||
yanmanyase vareṇyamindra dyukṣaṃ tadā bhara |
vidyāma tasya te vayamarkūpārasya dāvane ||
2 ||
yat | manyase | vareṇyam | indra | dyukṣam | tat | ā | bhara | vidyāma | tasya | te | vayam | akūpārasya | dāvane || 2 ||
he ᵛindra ᵛyat ᵛdyukṣam annaṃ ᵛvareṇyaṃ varaṇīyaṃ ᵛmanyase ᵛtana ṇukṣam ᵛā ᵛbhara asmabhyam |
ᵛte tava saṃbandhin ᵛvayaṃ ᵛtasya tādṛśasyākkalakṣaṇasya ᵛakūpārasya |
akutsitaḥ pāro'nto yasya tādṛśasya annasya ᵛdāvane dāne ᵛvidyāma syāma ||
vājapeye atiriktauthe ‘yatte disu’ ityepā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘yatte disu parādhya svāmicchava- saspate’ (āśva. śrau. 9. 9 ) iti ||
1. ra-he indra he | 2. ta-śa-‘indra’ nāsti | 3. ga-gha-ca-ja-ṇa-asmabhyamābhara; śa-asmabhyamābhara āhara | 4. ga-gha-ja-pa-pha-ba-dyumnasya | 5. ta-atiriktaukthye | ṛ. 2-105
yattai ditsu parādhyaṃ mano asti śrutaṃ bṛhat | tena dṛḻahā cidadriva ā vāje darṣi sātaye || 3 || yat | te | ditsu | pra'rādhyam | manaḥ | asti | śrutam | bṛhat | tena | dṛḻahā | cit | adri'vaḥ | ā | vājam | darṣi | sātaye || 3 ||
he indra ॰te tava ॰yat ॰ditsu dānecchu ॰parādhyaṃ prakarṣeṇa stutyaṃ ॰śrutaṃ viśrutaṃ ॰bṛhan mahat ॰manaḥ ॰asti ॰tena manasā he ॰adrivaḥ vajravannindra ॰dṛḻahā ॰cit dṛḍhamapi ॰vājam annam ॰ā ॰darṣi āśrayasi ॰sātaye asmadbhajanāya lābhāya vā ||
maṃheṣṭhaṃ vo maghonāṃ rājānaṃ carṣaṇīnām | indramupa praśastaye pūrvīrbhirrujuṣe giraḥ || 4 || maṃheṣṭham | vaḥ | maghonām | rājānam | carṣaṇīnām | indram | upa | pra'śastaye | pūrvībhiḥ | jujuṣe | giraḥ || 4 ||
॰maghonāṃ havirlakṣaṇadhanavatāṃ ॰vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ ॰maṃhiṣṭham atiśayena pūjyaṃ ॰carṣaṇīnāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ ॰rājānam ॰indram ॰upa upetya ॰praśastaye praśaṃsituṃ ॰pūrvībhiḥ purātanībhirvāgbhiḥ ॰giraḥ stotāraḥ ॰jujuṣe sevante ||
asmā itkāvyaṃ varca ukthamandrāya śaṃsyam | tasmā u brahmavāhase giro vardhantyatrayo giraḥ śubhbhantyatrayaḥ || 5 || asmai | it | kāvyam | varcaḥ | uktham | indrāya | śaṃsyam | tasmai | ū~ iti | brahma'vāhase | giraḥ | vardhanti | atrayaḥ | giraḥ | śubhbhanti | atrayaḥ ||5||
॰asmā ॰it asmā eva ॰indrāya ॰kāvyaṃ kaveḥ stotuḥ saṃbandha |
yadvā |
‘ku śabde’ |
śabdanīyaṃ ॰vacaḥ vāgrūpam ॰ukthaṃ śastraṃ ॰śaṃsyaṃ śaṃsanīyam |
yasmādevaṃ tasmāt ॰tasmā ॰u tasmā ॰indrāyaiva ॰brahmavāhase parivṛḍhasya stotrasya vāhakāya ॰giraḥ stutīḥ ॰atrayaḥ:’ atrigotrāḥ ॰vardhanti vardhayanti |
॰atrayaḥ ॰giraḥ ॰śubhbhanti dīpayanti |
punarantarādarthā ||
10 ||
‘ā yāhyadribhiḥ ’ iti navacaṣṭamaṃ sūktam |
atreyamanukramaṇikā—‘ā yāhi nava ॰yuṣṇi- gācanyānuṣṭupmadhye ca sā saurī tadādītihāsaḥ stutikarmatvādadridaivataiva ’ iti |
kṛtsnasya ‘ ṛṣi-
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-śa-’ nāsti |
2. ta-kṛtsnasūktasya |
śrāmyasmāt ' (anu. 12.2) iti paribhāṣayā atrairṛpiḥ |
ādyāstisra uṣṇihaḥ pañcamīnavamyāvanuṣṭubhau śiṣṭāścatasriṣṭubhaḥ |
atreḥ stūyamānatvāt ṣaṣṭyādyāḥ atridevatākāḥ adhyāryeyāśca pañcamī sūryadevatyā śiṣṭā aindrīḥ |
daśarātre tṛtīye'hani praugaśastre ādyastṛca aindraḥ pañcamaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—' ā yāhyadribhiḥ sutaṃ sajūrviśebhirdevebhiḥ ' (āśva. śrau. 7. 10) iti |
mahāvrate'pyauṣṇihacārśātāvayaṃ tṛcaḥ ||
ā yāhyadribhiḥ sutaṃ somapate piba |
vṛṣabhindra vṛṣabhivṛtrahantama ||
1 ||
ā | yāhi | adribhiḥ | sutam | somapam | soma'pata | piba | vṛṣan | indra | vṛṣabhiḥ | vṛtrahantama || 1 ||
he indra ā yāhi asmadyajñamāgaccha |
āgatya ca ha somapate somasya svāminnindra adribhiḥ prāvabhiḥ sutaṃ abhiputaṃ somaṃ piba |
he vṛpan phalasya varṣayitaḥ vṛtrahantama ati śayena śatrūṇāṃ hantuma vṛṣabhiḥ varṣakairmarudbhiḥ sahā yāhi piba ca ||
vṛṣā grāvā vṛṣā mado vṛṣā somo ayaṃ sutaḥ |
vṛṣabhindra vṛṣabhivṛtrahantama ||
2 ||
vṛṣā | grāvā | vṛṣā | madaḥ | vṛṣā | somaḥ | ayam | sutaḥ | vṛṣan | indra | vṛṣabhiḥ | vṛtrahantama || 2 ||
grāvā abhipavasādhanaḥ pāṣāṇaḥ vṛṣā varṣakaḥ somasarasya phalasya vā |
madaḥ somapānena janitaḥ vṛṣā varṣakaḥ |
ayaṃ sutaḥ abhiputaḥ somaḥ vṛṣā |
taṃ somaṃ vṛṣabhiḥ saha piba ||
vṛṣā tvā vṛṣaṇaṃ huve vajriñścitrābhirūtibhiḥ |
vṛṣabhindra vṛṣabhivṛtrahantama ||
3 ||
vṛṣā | tvā | vṛṣaṇam | huve | vajrin | citrābhiḥ | ūtibhiḥ | vṛṣan | indra | vṛṣabhiḥ | vṛtrahantama || 3 ||
he vajrin vajravan indra vṛṣā somasya sektāhaṃ vṛṣaṇaṃ tvāṃ huve āhvayāmi |
kimartham |
citrābhiḥ cāyanīyābhiḥ ūtibhiḥ rakṣāmirnimittabhūtābhiḥ |
śiṣṭaṃ gatam ||
' ṛjīṣī vajrī ' ityeṣā brāhmaṇācchaṃsinaḥ śastrājyā |
' ṛjīṣā vajrī vṛṣabhasturāpāḻiti yājyā ' (āśva. śrau. 5. 16) iti hi sūtritam ||
1. ga-gha-ja-pa-pha-ba-he vṛtrahantama vṛṣabhindra tam |
2. kha-ga-gha-ca-ja-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-' vajravan ' nāsti |
3. ga-gha-ja-pa-pha-ba-tvā tvām |
4. ra-śa-śastre yājyā |
ṛjīṣī vṛtrī vṛṣabhasturāṣāṭ śuṣmī rājā vṛtrahā somapāvā | yuktvā harībhyāmupa yāsadarvāṅmādhyaṃdine sarvane matsadindraḥ || 4 || ṛjīṣī | vṛtrī | vṛṣabhaḥ | turāṣāṭ | śuṣmī | rājā | vṛtrahā | soma'pāvā | yuktvā | harī'bhyām | upa | yāsat | arvāṅ | mādhyaṃdine | sarvane | matsat | indraḥ || 4 || ᵥṛjīṣī | savanadvaye'bhiṣutasya gatasārasya ᵥsomasya ᵥtṛtīyasavane āpyāyābhiṣutaḥ ᵥyo'sti sa ṛjīpaḥ somaḥ | ᵥyo'syārttā ᵥṛjīṣī | ᵥvṛtrī vajravān ᵥvṛṣabhaḥ varṣitā ᵥturāṣāṭ turāṇāṃ tvaramāṇānāṃ śatrūṇāṃ soḍhā ᵥśuṣmī balavān ᵥrājā īśvaraḥ sarvasya ᵥvṛtrahā vṛtrasya hantā ᵥsomapāvā somarasasya pātā evaṃ mahānubhāva indraḥ ᵥharibhyāṃ ᵥyuktvā rathaṃ yojayitvā ᵥupa ᵥyāmata upa gacchatu | ᵥarvāṅ asmadabhimukhamāgacchatu | āgatya ca ᵥmādhyaṃdine ᵥsavane ᵥmatsat mādyatu somena ᵥindraḥ ||
yattvā sūrya svarbhānustamasāvidhyadāmuraḥ | akṣetravidyathā mugdhā bhuvanānyadīdhayuḥ || 5 || yat | tvā | sūrya | svaḥ'bhānuḥ | tamasā | avidhyat | āsuraḥ | akṣetra'vit | yathā | mugdhaḥ | bhuvanāni | adīdhayuḥ || 5 || idamādicaturbhirmantraiḥ atrīṇāṃ karma kīrtyate | ha ᵥsūrya preraka deva ᵥhavā tvāṃ ᵥyata yadā ᵥāsuraḥ asurasya prāṇāpahartuḥ sūryā nā putraḥ ᵥsvarbhānuḥ svarbhānusaṃjñakaḥ ᵥtamasā māyānirmitena ᵥavidhyat āvṛṇodityarthaḥ | tadā ᵥbhuvanāni sarvāṇi ᵥadīdhayuḥ dṛśyante | ᵥyathā tatratyaḥ martyo janaḥ ᵥakṣetravit svasthānamajānan ᵥmugdhaḥ mūḍho bhavati tathā || || 13 ||
svarbhānoradha yindra māyā avo divo vartamānā avāhan | gūḻahaṃ sūrya tamasāpavratena turīyeṇa brahmaṇāvindadatriḥ || 6 || svaḥ'bhānoḥ | adha | yat | indra | māyāḥ | avaḥ | divaḥ | vartamānāḥ | ava'ahan | gūḻaham | sūryam | tamasā | apa'vratena | turīyeṇa | brahmaṇā | avindat | atriḥ || 6 || ᵥadha atha jaganmohyānantaraṃ ᵥsvarbhānoḥ asurasya ᵥyat yāḥ ᵥmāyāḥ santi | kīdṛśyāḥ | ᵥdivaḥ dyutamānādādityāt ᵥavaḥ avastāt ᵥvartamānāḥ | tadupaparitirodhānāsāmarthyāditi bhāvaḥ |
1. kha-ca-ṇa-gatarasasya somasya, jha-bha-gatasya somasya; ta-gatasārasya somasya rasasya |
2. na-āpyāyamāno'bhiṣutaḥ |
3. ga-gha-ja-śa-āgatya ca nāsti |
4. ga-gha-ja-pa-pha-ba-na dṛśyante |

he antarātman tvaṃ ᵥprathamaṃ mukhyaṃ prakṛṣṭamamirartha: |

ye aśpadā uta vā santi godā ye vakṣadā: subhagāsteṣu rāya: ||

tave | ūti'bhi: | sacamānā: | ariṣṭā: | bṛhaspate | maghava'vāna: | su'vīrā: | ye | aśva'dā: | uta | vā | santi | go'dā: | ye | vakṣa'dā: | subhagā: | teṣu | rāya: || 8 ||
he bṛhaspate ᵥtavottibhi: rakṣābhi: ᵥsacamānā: saṃgacchamānā: ᵥariṣṭā: ahiṃsitā: ᵥmaghavāna: dhanavantaḥ ᵥsuvīrā: śobhanaputrāśca bhavanti |

vyaḥ yajamānaḥ vrakṣasaḥ balino rākṣasān vohate prāpayati vdevavītau|

٧su suṣṭhu ٧pra ٧bhare prakarṣeṇa saṃpādayāmi ٧mahe mahate ٧suśaraṇāya śobhanarakṣakāyandrāya parjanyāya vā sukhāyā vā |

‘ā dhenava:’ iti sasadaścamekādaśa sūktamatraipayaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ vaiśvadevam |
atrānukramaṇikā— ‘ā dhenavaścyūnaitayorupānayaikapadā’ iti |
viniyogo laiṅgika: |
aponaptrīye ekadhanāsu havirdhānaṃ prati nīyamānāsvādhānuvacanīyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ā dhenava: payasā tūṇyarthā: samanyā yantyupa yantyanyā:’ ( āśva. śrau. 5. 1 ) iti ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-āhayatītyartha: |
2. kha-ca-ṇa- netrī |
3. na-vayamatraya: |
ā dhenavaḥ paryasā tūṇyarthā amardhantīrupa no yantu madhvā | maho rāye bṛhatīḥ sapta vipro mayobhuvau jaritā johavīti || 1 || ā | dhenavaḥ | paryasā | tūṇi'arthāḥ | amardhantīḥ | upa | naḥ | yantu | madhvā | mahaḥ | rāye | bṛhatīḥ | sapta | vipraḥ | mayaḥ'bhuvau | jaritā | johavīti || 1 ||
dhenavaḥ prīṇayitryo nadyaḥ madhvā madhureṇa payasā rasena sahitāḥ tūṇyarthāḥ tvaramāṇagamanāḥ |
‘adhortaiḥ’ ( niha. 1. 14 ) iti yāskaḥ |
amardhantīḥ ahiṃsantyaḥ satyaḥ naḥ asmān upa bhā yantu upāgacchantu |
tadarthaṃ mahaḥ mahate rāye dhanāya bṛhatīḥ mahatīḥ sapta sarpaṇa- svabhāvāḥ saptasaṃkhyākāḥ ‘imaṃ me gaṅge’ ( ṛ. saṃ. 10. 75. 5 ) iti mantroktā gaṅgādyā vā |
tatra hi prādhānyena saptoktāḥ |
mayobhuvaḥ sukhasya bhāvayitrīḥ vipraḥ viśeṣeṇa prīṇayitā jaritā stotā johavīti āhvayati ||
ā suṣṭutī namasā vartyadhyai dyāvā vājāṃya pṛthivī amṛdhre | pitā mātā madhuvacāḥ suhastā bharebhare no yaśasāvaviṣṭhām || 2 || ā | su'stutī | namasā | vartyadhyai | dyāvā | vājāya | pṛthivī iti | amṛdhre iti | pitā | mātā | madhu'vacāḥ | su'hastā | bhare'bhare | naḥ | yaśasau | aviṣṭhām || 2 ||
ahaṃ suṣṭutī suṣṭutyā śobhanastotreṇa namasā haviṣā ca amṛdhre hiṃsārahite dyāvā pṛthivī dyāvāpṛthivyau ā vartyadhyai āvartayitumicchāmi vājāya annādyam |
‘asaṃ vai vājaḥ’ ( tai. brā. 1.3.6.6 ) iti hi śrutiḥ |
pitā pālayitrī dyauḥ mātā nirmātrī pṛthivī madhuvacāḥ priya- vacanā suhastā abhimatadānena śobhanahastā |
ete ubhayaviśeṣaṇe |
yaśasī yaśoyuktau dyāvāpṛthivyau bharebhare sarveṣu saṃgrāmeṣu yajñeṣu vā naḥ asmān aviṣṭhāṃ rakṣatām |
adhvaryavaścakṛvāṃso madhūni pra vāyave bharata cāru śukram | hoteva naḥ prathamaḥ pādyasya deva madhvā rarimā te madāya || 3 || adhvaryavaḥ | cakṛ'vāṃsaḥ | madhūni | pra | vāyave | bharata | cāru | śukram | hotā'iva | naḥ | prathamaḥ | pāhi | asya | deva | madhvaḥ | rarimā | te | madāya || 3 ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-bha-saptasaṃkhyākā vā; ta-ra-saptasaṃkhyā vā |
2. kha-jha-bha-ājuhvayati |
3. kha- ca-ṇa-’amṛdhre...pṛthivī’ nāsti; jha-na-ma-ra-dyāvā pṛthivī |
4. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-’abhimatadānena śobhanahastā’ nāsti; ga-gha-ja-śobhanahastā |
he ᵛadhvaryava: adhvaraṃ yuñjānā: ᵛmadhūni madhurasāni somājyādīni ᵛcakṛvāṃsa: kurvāṇā yūyaṃ ᵛvāyave prathamaṃ ᵛpra ᵛbharata prakaryeṇa saṃpādayata ᵛcāru caraṇīyaṃ ᵛśukraṃ dīsaṃ somam|
he vāyo tvaṃ ca ᵛhoteva hotā yathā prathamaṃ pibati tadvat ᵛna: asmadartham ᵛasya amuṃ somaṃ ᵛprathama: itaradevebhya: pūrvaṃ ᵛpāhi piba|
he ᵛdeva vāyo ᵛmadhva: madhuraṃ somarasaṃ ᵛte ᵛmadāya ᵛrarima dadāma|
vāyo: pūrva- pānamaskṛprapannitam||
daśa kṣipoṃ yuñjate bāhū adriṃ somasya yā śamitārā suhastā|
madhvo rasaṃ sugabhastirgiriṣṭhāṃ carniśradaduduhe śukraṃśu: ||
4 ||
daśa| kṣipa:| yuñjate| bāhū iti| adrim| somasya| yā| śamitārā| suhastā| madhva:| rasam| sugabhasti:| giri'sthām| carniśradat| duduhe| śukram| aṃśu: || 4 ||
atrābhiṣavasya pratipādyamānatvāt somo devatā|
yadvā aindrī|
ᵛadrim abhiṣava- prāvāṇaṃ ᵛdaśa ᵛkṣipa: daśasaṃkhyākā: kṣepyo'ṅgulaya: ᵛyuñjate gṛhnti|
yadvā|
parasparaṃ yuktā bhavanti|
ᵛbāhū adhvaryusaṃbandhinau adriṃ yuñjate|
ᵛyā yau ᵛsuhastā śobhanahananau ᵛsomasya śami- tārau abhiśotārau tau ᵛbāhū yuñjate|
ᵛmadhva: madhurasya somasya ᵛrasaṃ ᵛgiriṣṭhāṃ girisrthāyinaṃ giri- vadyanatapradeśasthitaṃ vā ᵛsugabhasti: śobhanāṅguliradhvaryu: ᵛcarniśradat āhlādayan||
‘cadi āhlādane’ ityasmād‌luki chāndasaṃ rūpam|
ᵛduduhe dogdhi|
sa ca ᵛaṃśu: ᵛśukraṃ nirmalaṃ rasaṃ ᵛduduhe dugdhe|
yadaiaikaṃ vākyam|
aṃśu: vyāsa: ᵛsugabhasti: suhasto'dhvaryu: madhvo rasaṃ duduhe dogdhi||
asāvi te jujuṣāṇāya soma: kratve dakṣāya bṛhate madāya|
harī rathe sudhurā yoge arvāgindraṃ priyā kṛṇuhi hūyamāna: ||
5 ||
asāvi| te| jujuṣāṇāya| soma:| kratvai| dakṣāya| bṛhate| madāya| harī iti| rathe| sudhurā| yoge| arvāk| indram| priyā| kṛṇuhi| hūyamāna: || 5 ||
he indra ᵛte tubhyaṃ ᵛjujuṣāṇāya sevamānāya ᵛsoma: ᵛasāvi abhiṣuta:|
kimartham|
ᵛkratve kratave yatravadhādikarmaṇe ᵛdakṣāya balāya ᵛbṛhate ᵛmadāya ca|

5. kha-ga-gha-va-ja-ṇa-na-bha-satatagamanāīm |
‘agnau prāstāhutiḥ samyagādityamupatiṣṭhate |
ādityājjāyate vṛṣṭirvṛṣṭerannaṃ tataḥ prajāḥ’ (manu. 3. 76) iti |
ariṣṭagātuḥ ahiṃsitagamanaḥ |

tadevāha |
saḥ sa khalu hotā homaniṣpādakaḥ sahòbhiḥ balasya bhartā barhiḥ anu prasannārṇaḥ prakarṣeṇa saran vṛpā varṣakaḥ phalasya śiśuḥ sa iva rakṣaṇīyo'prauḍho vā yuvā sarvatra miśrayitā ajaraḥ jarārahitaḥ viśruhā viśuhāṇāmoṣadhīnāṃ madhye hitaḥ nihitaḥ sthāpitaḥ evaṃbhūtaḥ sacate||
pra vaṃ ete suyujo yārmiṣye nīcīrmuṣmai yumyaṃ ṛtāvṛdhaḥ |
suyantubhiḥ sarvaśāsairbhīśubhiḥ krivirnāmāni pravaṇe muṣāyati ||
4 ||
pra | vaḥ | ete | suyujaḥ | yārman | iṣyai | nīcīḥ | amuṣmai | yumyaḥ | ṛta'vṛdhaḥ | suyantubhiḥ | sarva'śāsaiḥ | abhīśubhiḥ | kriviḥ | nāmāni | pravaṇe | muṣāyati || 4 ||
eṣā saurī vā |
ete raśmayaḥ suyujaḥ suṣṭhu parasparaṃ saṃyuktāḥ yāmana yajñagamane iṣyai eṣaṇāya nīcīḥ nīcaṃ gacchantīti śeṣaḥ |
amuṣmai yajamānāya yumyaḥ yamyā gamanāhaḥ |
athavā yamo niyamitādityaḥ |
tasya saṃbandhanaḥ |
ṛtāvṛdhaḥ yajñasya vardhayitāraśca |
ayamādityaḥ suya- ntubhiḥ sugamanaiḥ sarvaśāsaiḥ sarvasya śāsakaiḥ abhīśubhiḥ raśmibhiḥ kriviḥ kartā nāmāni nāma- kānyudakāni pravaṇe nimne bhūpradeśe mupāyati muṣṇāti ādatte ityarthaḥ |
athavāmināvādyasyāpre- rete'śvā iṣye yajñagamanāya yāmani rathe suyujaḥ pragacchanti |
śiṣṭaṃ samānam |
kriviḥ krivi- sahaśo'gniḥ pravaṇe nāmāni havīṃyuktalakṣaṇāmirjālāmimuṣāyati ādatte ||
maṃjarbhurāṇastarabhiḥ sutegṛbhaṃ vayākinaṃ cittagarbhāsu susvaruḥ |
dhāravākevṛjugātha śobhase vardhasva patnīrabhi jīvo adhvare ||
5 ||
su'jarbhurāṇaḥ | taru'bhiḥ | sute'gṛbham | vayākinam | citta'garbhāsu | su'svaruḥ | dhāra'vākeṣu | ṛju'gātha | śobhase | vardhasva | patnīḥ | abhi | jīvaḥ | adhvare || 5 ||
ha ṛjugātha śobhanastutika agne tarubhiḥ taruvikārairmehaiḥ sutegṛbham abhisutena rasena gṛhītaṃ vayākinam |
kutsitā vayāḥ śākhā vayākā latāḥ |
tadvantaṃ somaṃ cittagarbhāsu citta- grāhiṇīṣu stutiṣu stutibhiḥ saha saṃjarbhurāṇaḥ gṛbhnan gṛhan susvaruḥ śobhanagamanaḥ śobhana- stutiko vā tvaṃ dhāravākeṣu |
dhārayanti vākān stotrāṇīti dhāravākā ṛtvigyajamānāḥ |
teṣu madhye śobhase |
kiṃca adhvare jīvaḥ sarvasya jīvayitā patnīḥ pālayitrīropadhīrjvalā vā abhi abhimukhaṃ vardhasva abhivardhaya ||

^tam |

ᵥsadāpṛṇaḥ sarvadā dānaśīla etannāmā | ᵥyajataḥ yaṣṭā ca | ᵥbāhuvṛktaḥ bāhubhyāṃ vṛktadabhraśca ᵥśrutavit śrutasya vettā ca | ᵥtaryaḥ ca | eteṣāṃ pañcānāṃ parasparapekṣayā pratyekamekavacanam | saḥ saḥ ᵥṛṣiḥ | ᵥdvipaḥ śatrūn | ᵥvi ᵥvadhīt hiṃsyāt | ᵥvaḥ yuṣmākaṃ | ᵥsacā sahitaḥ | yuṣmābhiḥ sahita ityarthaḥ | ᵥsaḥ ᵥṛṣiḥ | ᵥvarā śreṣṭhau | ᵥubhā ubhau ihalokaparalokaviṣayau kāmau | ᵥpratyeti abhigacchati | ᵥbhāti ᵥca | ᵥyat yasmāt | ᵥīm enaṃ | ᵥgaṇaṃ devasaṃghaṃ | ᵥsuprayāvābhiḥ suṣṭhu prakarṣeṇa miśrayadbhiḥ stotraiḥ | ᵥbhajate tasmādeva bhavatīti ||

‘prayuñjatī prāṇinaḥ prastuteṣu karmasu ‘divaḥ dyulokān ‘eti āgacchati ‘bruvāṇā stūyamānā ||
karmaṇi kartṛpratyayaḥ ||
‘mahī mahatī ‘mātā prakāśasya devānāṃ vā nirmātrī ‘duhituḥ bhūmyāḥ ‘bodhayantī bodhaṃ kurvāṇā |
tadupajīvyatvāt duhitṛtvam’ upacaryate |
yadvā duhiturdiva iti sambandhaḥ |
uṣasāṃ divo duhitṛtvaṃ ‘vyucchā duhitardivaḥ’ (ṛ. saṃ. 1.48.1) ityādiṣu prasiddham |
anyatra ca ‘divo duhitā bhuvanasya patnī’ (ṛ. saṃ. 7.75.4) ityupasaḥ ‘duhitṛtvam’ ānātam |
ata idamadhigamyate |
kadācidyadaupaso nāyana upāśva diva iti |
‘prajāpatitvai svāṃ duhitaramadhyadhyāyadiva- mityanya āhurapamamityanye’ (ai. brā. 2. 33) iti hi śrūyate |
liṅgaṃ ca dṛśyate kāryakāraṇabhāvadhayatyaye ‘aditerdakṣo ajāyata dakṣādaditiḥ4’ (ṛ. saṃ 10. 72. 4) iti ||
kiṃca ‘āvirvāsantī paricarantī ‘yuvatiḥ miśrayantī sarvatra niyatarṇī vā ‘manīṣā stutimatī ‘pitṛbhyaḥ ||
tṛtīyārthe pañcamī ||
pitṛbhiḥ pālakaṃdaivaḥ saha ‘sadane yāgagṛhe ‘ā sarvataḥ ‘johuvānā āhūyamānā |
yadvā |
pitṛbhyaḥ karmaṇāṃ pālakebhyaḥ yajamānebhya iṣṭaprāpaṇe pālakebhyo vā tepāmarthāya johuvānā ||
ajirāsastadapa īyamānā ātasthivāṃso amṛtasya nābhim |
anantāsa uravo viśvataḥ sīṃ pari dyāvāpṛthivī yanti panthāḥ ||
2 ||
ajirāsaḥ | tat'apa | īyamānāḥ | ātasthivāṃsaḥ | amṛtasya | nābhim | anantāsaḥ | uravaḥ | viśvataḥ | sīm | pari | dyāvāpṛthivī iti | yanti | panthāḥ || 2 ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-taduhitṛtvam |
2. kha-ca-ṇa-iti ca uṣasaḥ; jha-ta-na-bha-ra-coṣasaḥ |
3. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-duhitṛtvam; śa-divo duhitṛtvam |
4 kha-ca-ṇa-bha-dakṣādaditiḥ pāra |
^ajirāsaḥ gamanaśīlāḥ |
ajiḥ iracpratyayāntaḥ ‘ajiraśiśara’ (u. sū. 1. 53) ityādinā nipātitaḥ ||
^tadapaḥ tadeva prakāśanarūpaṃ karma yasya tattādṛśamahaḥ ^īyamānāḥ gacchantaḥ ^amṛtasya sūryasya ^nābhi saṃnāhakaṃ maṇḍalam amṛtasyodakasya vā nābhimantarikṣam ^ātasthivāṃsaḥ ātiṣṭhantaḥ ^anantāṃsaḥ aparimitāḥ ^uravaḥ dyāvāḥ ^panthāḥ patanaśīlā raśmayaḥ ^dyāvāpṛthivī ^pari divaṃ ca pṛthivīṃ ^vāntarikṣaṃ ca ^viśvataḥ paritaḥ ^yanti gacchanti |
^sīm iti pūraṇaḥ ||
ukṣā samudro aruṣaḥ suparṇaḥ pūrvasya yoniṃ piturā viveśa |
madhye divo nihitaḥ pṛśniśmā vi cakrame rajasaspatyantau ||
3 ||
ukṣā | samudraḥ | aruṣaḥ | suparṇaḥ | pūrvasya | yonim | pituḥ | ā | viveśa | madhye | divaḥ | niḍahitaḥ | pṛśniḥ | aśmā | vi | cakrame | rajasaḥ | pāti | antau || 3 ||
sauryo'yaṃ tracaḥ |
^ukṣā udakasya kāmānāṃ vā sektā ^samudraḥ samundanaḥ |
yadvā |
saṃmodante asmin devā iti samudraḥ |
^arupaḥ ārocamānaḥ ^suparṇaḥ sugamanaḥ ^pūrvamya |
avayavalakṣaṇeyam |
pūrvadigavayavasya ^pituḥ pālakabhyāntarikṣasya ^yonim utpādakamavayavam ^ā viveśa āviṣṭhavān |
paścāt ^divaḥ ^madhye ^nihitaḥ ^pṛśniḥ |
prāśruta enaṃ varṇa iti pṛśnirādityaḥ |
^aśmā sarvatra vyāptaḥ |
luptopamā vā |
asmasadṛśaḥ ^rajasaḥ antarikṣasya ^antau pūrvāparabhāgau ^vi ^cakrame vikramate |
^pāti ca jagat |
jagadrakṣārthaṃ kamate ityarthaḥ ||
catvāra ī bibhrati kṣemayanto daśa garbhaṃ carase dhāpayante |
tridhātavaḥ paramā asya gāvo divaścaranti pari sadho antān ||
4 ||
catvāraḥ | īm | bibhrati | kṣema'yantaḥ | daśa | garbham | carase | dhāpayante | tri'dhātavaḥ | paramāḥ | asya | gāvaḥ | divaḥ | caranti | pari | sadhaḥ | antān || 4 ||
^catvāraḥ ṛtvijaḥ ^īm enamādityaṃ ^kṣemayantaḥ kṣemātmana icchantaḥ ^bibhrati dhārayanti havirbhiḥ stutibhiśca |
kiṃca ^daśa diśaḥ ^garbhaṃ garbhamadhye utpannamenaṃ ^carase caraṇāya udayāstamaya- vyavahārāya ^dhāpayante gamayanti |
^tridhātavaḥ triprakārāḥ śītoṣṇavarṣabhedena trividhāḥ ^asya ādi- tyasya ^paramāḥ utkṛṣṭāḥ ^gāvaḥ raśmayaḥ ^divaḥ antarikṣasya ^antān pari paritaḥ ^sadhaḥ udayā- nantarameva ^caranti gacchanti ||
1. sva-ca-jha-ṇa-na-ma- ‘ pṛthivīm ’ nāsti |
2. a-na-ra-tasmin |
3. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-ma- ‘ havirbhiḥ stutibhiśca ’ nāsti |
4. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-ma-ra- ‘ caranti ’ nāsti |
idaṃ vapurnivacanaṃ janāścaranti yanmartyastasthurārpaḥ | dve yadī vibhṛto māturanye iheha jāte yamyā sabandhū || 5 || idam | vapuḥ | ni'vacanam | janāsaḥ | caranti | yat | naryaḥ | tasthuḥ | āpaḥ | dve iti | yat | īm | vibhṛtaḥ | mātuḥ | anye iti | iha'iha | jāte iti | yamyā | sabandhū iti sa'bandhū || 5 ||
^janāmaḥ he janā ṛtvijaḥ ^idaṃ purataḥ dṛśyamānaṃ ^vapuḥ śarīraṃ maṇḍalaṃ ^nivacanaṃ stutyaṃ bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
yanmaṇḍalamupajīvatha ^nadyaḥ ^caranti pravahanti |
^āpaḥ ca ^yat maṇḍalamadhitasthuḥ |
maṇḍalādhīnatvāttāsām |
^īm etadullakṣaṇaṃ yanmaṇḍalaṃ ^dve ahorātre ^mātuḥ nirmāturmātṛsthānīyādanta- rikṣāt ^anye ^iheha ^jāte iha ceha ca sūrye jāte niṣpanne ^yamyā yamye niyamanīye yugmabhūte vā ^sabandhū samānabale samānabandhane ubhayorapyeka eva sūryo bandhako'kṣo yayontārayo ^vibhṛtaḥ dhārayataḥ ||
vi tanvate dhiyau asmā apāṃsi vastrā putrāya mātarau vayanti | upaprakṣe vṛṣaṇo modamānā divaspathā vadhvo yantyaccha || 6 || vi | tanvate | dhiyaḥ | asmai | apāṃsi | vastrā | putrāya | mātaraḥ | vayanti | upa'prakṣe | vṛṣaṇaḥ | modamānāḥ | divaḥ | pathā | vadhvaḥ | yanti | accha || 6 ||
^asmai sūryāya ^dhiyaḥ stutīḥ ^apāṃsi karmāṇi ca ^vi ^tanvate vistārayanti yajamānāḥ |
^putrāya putrasthānīyāya ^mātaraḥ nirmādhya upaso diśo vā ^vastrā vastrāṇi ^vayanti niṣpādayanti |
vastrasadṛśāni tejāṃsi nibiḍāni saṃpādayantītyarthaḥ |
^vṛṣaṇaḥ sektuḥ sūryasya ^upaprakṣe upaparcane saṃparke sati ^modamānāḥ hṛṣyantaḥ ^vadhvaḥ vadhūsthānīyā raśmayaḥ ^divaspathā sūryasambandhinā mārgeṇa ^accha asmadabhimukhaṃ ^yanti gacchanti prasaranti ||
tadastu mitrāvaruṇā tadagne śaṃ yo'smabhyamidamastu śastam | aśīmahi gādhamuta pratiṣṭhāṃ namo dive bṛhate sādanāya || 7 || tat | astu | mitrāvaruṇā | tat | agne | śam | yoḥ | asmabhyam | idam | astu | śastam | aśīmahi | gādham | uta | prati'sthām | namaḥ | dive | bṛhate | sādanāya || 7 ||
atra liṅgasiddhā devatāḥ |
he ^mitrāvaruṇā mitrāvaruṇau ^tata ^idaṃ sūktaṃ ^śastaṃ stutam ^astu bhavatu |
he ^agne ^tat sūktaṃ śastam ^astu |
^asmabhyaṃ ^śaṃ sukhāya ^yoḥ duḥkhānamiśraṇāyedaṃ 1. kha-jha-na-ma-ra-śa- ‘tat’ nāsti |
śastamastu |
vayaṃ ca aśīmahi prāguyāma gādhaṃ sthitima |
uta api ca pratiṣṭhāṃ sthitiravicchitsi cāśīmahi |
namaḥ namaskāromi dive dyotamānāya sūryāya bṛhate mahate sādanāya āśrayāya viśvasya ||
1 ||
‘kadu priyāya’ itipañcarcaṃ caturthaṃ sūktam |
pratibhānurnāmātreya ṛṣiḥ |
jagatī chandaḥ |
viśve devā devatā |
atrānukramaṇikā—‘kadu pañca pratibhānurjāgatam’ iti |
ābhiṣṭuvike pañcame'hani vaiśvadeva- śastre idaṃ sūktaṃ nividdhānam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘kadu priyāyeti vaiśvadevam’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 7) iti ||
kadu priyāya dhāmne manāmahe svakṣattrāya svaryaśase mahe vayam |
āmenyasya rajaso yadabhra ā~ apo vṛṇānā vitanoti māyinī ||
1 ||
kat | oṃ iti | priyāya | dhāmne | manāmahe | sva'kṣattrāya | sva'ryaśase | mahe | vayam | ā'menyasya | rajasaḥ | yat | abhre | ā | apaḥ | vṛṇānā | vi'tanoti | māyinī || 1 ||
atra vaidyutāgnisaṃstavaḥ |
kadu kadā |
u iti pūraṇaḥ |
priyāya sarveṣāṃ priyabhūtāya |
vṛṣṭyārā havirādiprayojakatvāt sarvapriyatvamasya prasiddham |
tādṛśāya dhāmne tejase vaidyutāya |
uttaravākye abhre apaḥ vitanoti iti vakṣyamāṇatvādivaṃ labhyate |
kīdṛśāya |
svakṣattrāya svabhūtabalāya kiṃca svayaśase svabhutājñāya |
sarvamapyatra hāsya svam |
mahe mahate pūjyāya vayaṃ kadā manāmahe stavāma |
yat |
uttaratra strīliṅganirdeśāt yā iti vipariṇamayitavyam |
yā āpreyī śaktiḥ āmenyasya sama- ntānmātavyasya rajasaḥ antarikṣasya |
aparimitamapi nabha iyaditi sarvamīyate |
saṃbandhin abhre meghe bhā adhi meghasyopari apaḥ udakāni vṛṣṭilakṣaṇāni vitanoti vistārayati vṛṇānā ācchādayantī sevyamānā vā māyinī |
māyeti prajñānam |
prajñāvatī satī |
yat yasya sambandhinī mādhyamikī vāgiti vā yojyam ||
tā aṃnata vayunaṃ vīravakṣaṇaṃ samānyā vṛtayā viśvamā rajaḥ |
apo apācīraṃparā arpejate pra pūrvābhistarate devayurjanaḥ ||
2 ||
tāḥ | ata | vayunam | vīra'vakṣaṇam | samānyā | vṛtayā | viśvam | ā | rajaḥ | apo iti | apācīḥ | arparāḥ | arpa | ījate | pra | pūrvābhiḥ | tirate | deva'yuḥ | janaḥ ||2||
iyamupasthā | tāḥ upasaḥ ata vitanvanti | kim | vayunaṃ prajñānam | kīdṛśam | vīravakṣaṇam | vīraiḥ svibhirvahanīyam | yadvā | vakṣaṇāḥ kāryavoḍhāro yena preryante tattādṛśam | kiṃca samānyā ekarūpayā vṛtayā āvārakayā dīdhyā viśvam ā sarvamapi rajaḥ jagat ata | evaṃ sati devayuḥ devakāmaḥ janaḥ apācīḥ apāñcanāḥ pratinivṛttamukhīḥ aparāḥ anyā āgāmīnīḥḥ
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-stutim |
2. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-kīṭaśāya balāya |
uṣasaḥ ˅apejate apacālayati | aparo'paśabdaḥ pūraṇaḥ | ˅pūrvābhiḥ tābhiḥ ˅pra ˅tirate | prapūrvasminnirvardhanāthaḥ | vardhayati svamarṇāṣāṃ vardhate vā svayam ||
ā grāvabhirahandyaibhirktubhirviriṣṭaṃ vajramā jigharti māyini |
śataṃ vā yasya pracaransve dame saṃvartayanto vi ca vartayanhāṃ ||
3 ||
ā | grāvabhiḥ | ahandyaibhiḥ | aknubhiḥ | variṣṭam | vajram | ā | jigharti | māyini |
śatam | vā | yasya | pra'caran | sve | dame | sam'vartayantaḥ | vi | ca | vartayan | arhā || 3 ||
atra pūrvārdhe indra ucyate uttarārdhe indrātmā sūryaḥ |
˅ā hūyata iti śeṣaḥ |
kaiḥ |
˅ahandyaibhiḥ ahani saṃpāditaiḥ ˅aknubhiḥ rātribhiḥ |
tatra niṣpannairityarthaḥ |
tathāvidhaiḥ ˅grāvabhiḥ |
sādhye sādhanaśabdaḥ |
grāvamādhyairadhiparvairnimittabhūtaiḥ |
āgatyātra saumīṃ san ˅māyini vṛtre nimitte sati ˅variṣṭam uttaraṃ ˅vajram ˅ā sarvataḥ ˅jigharti dīpayati |
˅vā kiṃca |
vāśabdaścārthe |
˅yasya indrātma- kasyādityasya ˅śataṃ śatasaṃkhyākā raśmayaḥ ˅sve ˅dame svakīye gṛhaṃ ākāśe ˅pracaran pracaranti |
kiṃ kurvantaḥ |
˅saṃvartayantaḥ prāṇināṃ nāśayantaḥ |
yadvā |
ahā ahāni divasān saṃvartayantaḥ samyak pravartayantaḥ ˅vi ˅ca ˅vartayan ahā ahāni nivartayanti gatānyeva punaḥ prāpayanti ca |
tapana- parispandādhīnatvāt prāṇināṃmāyuḥkṣayasyāhorātrādivyavahārasya ||
tāmasya rītiṃ parrśoriva pratyanīkamaruyaṃ bhuje asya varṣasaḥ |
sacā yadi pitumantamiva kṣayaṃ ratnaṃ dadhāti bharahūtaye viśe ||
4 ||
tām | asya | rītim | parrśaḥ'iva | prati | anīkam | ahyam | bhuje | asya | varṣasaḥ |
sacā | yadi | pitumantam'iva | kṣayam | ratnam | dadhāti | bhara'hūtaye | viśe || 4 ||
iyamāpreyī |
˅tāṃ prasiddhāṃ ˅asya agneḥ ˅rītiṃ svabhāvaṃ gatiṃ vā ˅parrśoriva ˅prati parrśoḥ pratinidhikamiva paśyāmi |
paraśuryathā svasvāmino'bhimataṃ sādhayati tadvadityarthaḥ |
˅asya ˅varṣasaḥ rūpavato'syādityasya ˅anīkaṃ raśmisamūhaṃ ˅bhuje bhogāya ˅atyaṃ kathayāmyaham |
˅yadi ˅yasmāt sa ca devaḥ ˅sacā asmatsahāyaḥ san ˅pitumantam annavantaṃ ˅kṣayam ˅iva nivāsamiva ˅bharahūtaye saṃgrāme yajñe vā āhvānayuktāya ˅viśe yajamānāya ˅ratnaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ dhanaṃ ˅dadhāti dhārayati |
ubhayamapi dadātītyarthaḥ ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-śa-somaḥ |
2. kha-ca-ṇa- 'vā' nāsti |
3. ga-gha-na-ja-ṇa-śa- ˚parispandā˚ |
4. kha-ca-ṇa-svasvāmine matam; jha-ta-na-bha-ra-śa-svasvāmine'bhimatam |
5. kha- ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-kathayeyaṃ stotram; śa-kathayāmyahaṃ stotraṃ yadi |
6. kha-ca-ṇa-dadāti; jha-ta-na-bha-ra- dadhāti dhārayati dadāti |
sa jihvayā catūrnīka ṛñjate cāru vasāno varuṇo yatannarima | na tasya vidma puruṣatvatāṃ vayaṃ yato bhagaḥ savitā dāti vāryam || 5 || saḥ | jihvayā | catuḥ'anīkaḥ | ṛñjate | cāru | vasānaḥ | varuṇaḥ | yatan | arim | na | tasya | vidma | puruṣatvatāṃ | vayam | yataḥ | bhagaḥ | savitā | dāti | vāryam || 5 ||
yama: agniḥ catūrnīka: caturdikṣu prasṛtajvāla: san 'jihvayā 'ṛñjate gacchati' ghṛtādikam |
yadvā |
jihvayā catūrnīka: san ṛñjate alaṃkaroti svātmānam |
kiṃ kurvan |
'cāru ramaṇīyaṃ teja: 'vasāna: ācchādayan 'varuṇa: tamovāraka: san 'arim amitraṃ 'yatan uddharan |
'tasya |
tamimyartha: |
taṃ 'na 'vidma na jānīma: 'vayaṃ 'puruṣatvatā puruṣatvena kāmānāṃ pūrakatvena vā yuktam ||
aparo bhāvapratyaya: pūraṇa
prati prayāṇamasurasya vidvāntasuktairdevaṃ savitāraṃ duvasya |
upa bravītu namasā vijānañajyeṣṭhaṃ ca ratnaṃ vibhajantamāyoḥ ||
2 ||
prati | prayāṇam | asurasya | vidvān | su'uktaiḥ | devam | savitāram | duvasya | upa | bravītu | namasā | tri'jānan | jyeṣṭham | ca | ratnam | vi'bhajantama | āyoḥ ||2||
^asurasya śatrūṇāṃ nirasituḥ savituḥ ^prati prayāṇaṃ prasthāgatīṃ ^vidvān jānan ^sūktaiḥ ^savitāraṃ ^devaṃ ^duvasya paricara he antarātman |
kiṃca ^āyoḥ manuṣyāya ^jyeṣṭhaṃ ^ratnaṃ ^ca ^vibhajantama |
gamayantamityarthaḥ |
^namasā namaskāreṇa haviṣā vā ^vijānan |
viśeṣeṇa bhāvaya- nnityarthaḥ |
^yadvāntarṇitavyatho'yam ||
jñāpayan ^upa ^bravītu stautu bhavān ||
adatrayā dayate vāryāṇi puṣā bhago aditivrsta usraḥ |
indro viṣṇurvaruṇo mitro agnirhāni bhadrā janayanta dasmāḥ ||
3 ||
adatrayā | dayate | vāryāṇi | pūṣā | bhagaḥ | aditiḥ | vastai | usraḥ | indraḥ | viṣṇuḥ | varuṇaḥ | mitraḥ | agniḥ | ahāni | bhadrā | janayanta | dasmāḥ || 3 ||
ayamagniḥ ^adatrayā adanīyāni || aderauṇādiko'pratyayaḥ | ‘ supāṃ suluk ’ iti yājādeśaḥ | athavā attītyadatrā jihvā | tayā ^vāryāṇi varaṇīyāni kāṣṭhāni ^dayate | dayatirekakarmārtha dahane vartate | dahatītyarthaḥ | sāmarthyādagniriti gamyate | yadvā | adanīyāni^ varaṇīyānyajñāni dayate | dadāti yajamānāya || ‘ daya dānagatihiṃsādāneṣu ’ || kīdṛśo'gniḥ | ^pūṣā poṣakaḥ ^bhagaḥ bhajanīyaḥ ^aditiḥ akhaṇḍanīyaḥ | yadvā | pūṣādayastrayo'pi pratyeka madanīyāni vāryāṇi kāṣṭhāni dayate | ^usraḥ sūryaḥ ^vastai ācchādayati | sāmarthyāttejāṃsi | indrādayaḥ pañcāpi devāḥ ^dasmāḥ darśa- nīyāḥ ^ahāni sarvāṇyāgantṛṇi ^bhadrā śobhanāni ^janayanta janayanti | divasānāṃ yāga- dānādiviśiṣṭameva bhadratvam ||
tanno anarvā savitā varūthaṃ tasmindhava duṣayanto anu gman |
upa yadvocai adhvarasya hotā rāyaḥ syāma patayo vājratnāḥ ||
4 ||
tat | naḥ | anarvā | savitā | varūtham | tat | sindhavaḥ | duṣayantaḥ | anu | gman | upa | yat | vocai | adhvarasya | hotā | rāyaḥ | syāma | patayaḥ | vāja'ratnāḥ || 4 ||
1. kha-ca-ṇa-bha-pratyāvartim | 2. kha-ca-ṇa-bha-vāryāṇi | 3. kha-ca-ṇa-devatāḥ | 4. ga-gha-ja-śa- śobhanīyāni |
tat prasiddhaṃ varūthaṃ varaṇīyamasmadabhimaṃta dhanaṃ naḥ asmabhyam anarvā apracyutaḥ kenāpyatira skṛtaḥ savitā devaḥ prayacchatviti śeṣaḥ |
tat dhanaṃ sindhavaḥ syandanaśīlāḥ ipayantaḥ gacchantyo nadyaḥ anu anusṛtya gamna gacchantu |
dhanaṃ dātuṃ gacchantvityarthaḥ |
yat yasmāt adhva rasya yāgasya hotā homaniṣpādako'ham upa voce upetya bravīmi tasmāttatprayacchatviti śeṣaḥ |
vayaṃ cātrayaḥ rāyaḥ dhanasya bahuvidhasya patayaḥ svāminaḥ syāma bhavema |
vājaratnāḥ annena balena vā ramaṇīyāḥ |
athavā yadyasmāddoce staumi tasmāt savitā no varūthamāgacchatu |
tasmāccaśca nāṃ varūthaṃ yajñamanu gamna anugacchantviti vā yojyam ||
pra ye vasṷbhya i॒vadā namo̭ du॒rye mi॒tre varṷṇa suktavā̭caḥ | a॒vatva॒mbaṃ kṷṇutā varī̭yo di॒vaspṛ̭thi॒vyorva̭sā madema || 5 || pra | ye | vasṷ'bhyaḥ | ī̭vat | ā | na̭maḥ | duḥ | ye | mi॒tre | varṷṇe | su॒kta'vā̭caḥ | a॒tra | e॒tu | a॒bhva̭m | kṷṇutā | varī̭yaḥ | di॒vaḥ'pṛ̭thi॒vyoḥ | a॒vasā̭ | madḙma || 5 ||
ye yajamānāḥ vasubhyaḥ yajñanivāsebhyo devebhyaḥ īvata gamanavata namaḥ annaṃ paścātmakaṃ pra duḥ prāduḥ |
ye ca mitre deve varuṇe ca sūktavācaḥ sūktavacaso bhavanti tānasmān amvaṃ mahaddhanaṃ tejo vā avetu avagacchatu |
he devāḥ kuṇuta kuruta tebhyaḥ varīyaḥ urutaraṃ sukham |
divaspṛthivyoḥ dyāvāpṛthivyoḥ avasā rakṣaṇena madema havyema ||
3 ||
‘ viśvo devasya ’ iti pañcarcaṃ pañca sūktaṃ svastyātreyamunerārṣaṃ vaiśvadevam |
pañcamāṃ paṅktiḥ ādyāśastaso'nuṣṭubhaḥ |
taduktamanukramaṇyām—‘ viśvaḥ svastyātreyaḥ paṅktyantam ’ iti |
pṛṣṭhābhi plavaṣaṣṭhayoḥ prathama'hani vaiśvadevaśastre viśvo devasyeti pratipattasyādyaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ viśvo devasya neturyekā ’ ( āśva. śrau. 7. 6 ) iti |
ābhiplavikeṣu dvitīyacaturthapṛṣṭhavahaḥ svaipaiva vaiśvadevasya śastrasya pratipat |
‘ yummevevamabhiplave ’ ( āśva. śrau. 7. 6 ) iti sūtritavān ||
viśvo̭ de॒vasya̭ ne॒turmarto̭ vurīta sa॒khyam | viśvo̭ rā॒ya i̭ṣudhyati॒ dyumna̭ṃ vṛṇīta pu॒ṣyasḙ || 1 || viśva̭ḥ | de॒vasya̭ | ne॒tuḥ | marta̭ḥ | vurī̭ta | sa॒khyam | viśva̭ḥ | rā॒ye | i॒ṣudhyati̭ | dyumna̭m | vṛṇī̭ta | pu॒ṣyasḙ || 1 ||
viśvaḥ sarve mataḥ netuḥ devasya saviturityarthaḥ |
namya sakhyaṃ vurīta vṛṇīta |
viśvaḥ janaḥ rāye dhanasya iṣudhyati ||
pañcamalakāraḥ ||
īśita |
tasyānugrahāt sarvo janaḥ dyumnaṃ dhanaṃ vṛṇīta puṣyase puṣṭyai paryāptaṃ dhanam ||

yatra vahi̭rabhihito dudrava॒droṇya̭ḥ pa॒śuḥ | nṛmaṇā vīra॒pastyo'rṇā dhīrai̭va sa॒rnitā || 4 || yatra | vahiḥ | abhi'hitaḥ | dudravat | droṇyaḥ | paśuḥ | nṛmaṇāḥ | vīra'pastyaḥ | arṇāḥ | dhīra'iva | sarnitā || 4 ||

pṛṣṭhyasya ṣaṣṭhe'hani vaiśvadevaśastre ' svasti naḥ ' iti tracaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—' svasti no mimītā- maśvinā bhaga iti traca iti vaiśvadevam ' (āśva. śrau. 8.1) iti |
tathā bṛhaspatisave vaiśvadevativijñā- nārtho'yaṃ tracaḥ |
' svasti no mimītāmaśvinā bhaga iti vaiśvadevam ' (āśva. śrau. 9. 5 ) iti sūtritatvāt ||
svasti nau mimītāmaśvinā bhagaḥ svasti devyaditiranarvaṇaḥ |
svasti pūṣā asuro dadhātu naḥ svasti dyāvāpṛthivī sucetanā ||
11 ||
svasti | naḥ | mimītām | aśdinā | bhagaḥ | svasti | devī | adi̭tiḥ | a॒na॒rvaṇaḥ | svasti | pūṣā | asuraḥ | dadhātu | naḥ | svasti | dyāvāpṛthivī iti | su'cetanā || 11 ||
ᵥnaḥ : asmabhyam ᵥaśvinā aśvinau ᵥsvasti avināśaṃ kṣemaṃ ᵥmimītāṃ kurutām |
ᵥbhagaḥ ca ᵥsvasti kṣemaṃ mimītām |
tathā ᵥdevyaditiḥ ca svasti mimītām |
ᵥanarvaṇaḥ apracyutaḥ ᵥpūṣā ᵥasuraḥ śatrūṇāṃ nirasitā prāṇānāṃ balānāṃ dātā vā naḥ ᵥsvasti ᵥdadhātu |
ᵥnaḥ : asmabhyaṃ ᵥdyāvāpṛthivī dyāvāpṛthivyāvapi ᵥsucetanā śobhanena prajñānena viśiṣṭe ᵥsvasti mimītām ||
svastayḙ vāyumupa̭ bravāmahai somaṃ svasti bhuvanasya yaspatiḥ |
bṛhaspatiṃ sarva̭gaṇaṃ svastayḙ svastaya̭ ādityāso bhavantu naḥ ||
12 ||
svastayḙ | vā॒yum | upa̭ | bra॒vā॒ma॒hai | soma̭m | svasti | bhuvana̭sya | yaḥ | patiḥ | bṛhaspa̭tim | sarva̭'gaṇam | svastayḙ | svastayḙ | ā॒di॒tyāsaḥ | bha॒va॒ntu | naḥ || 12 ||
1. na-sūtritatvāt |
dīkṣitānāmupatāpe nividdhānārthamayaṃ tracaḥ |
tathā ca sūtritaṃ—svastyātreye nividaṃ dadhyāt |
prakṛtyā nigada iti |
daśarātrasya ṣaṣṭhe'hani vaiśvadevaśastre svasti no mimītāmiti tracaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—svasti no mimītāmaśvinā bhaga iti traca iti vaiśvadevamiti |
2. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-magadha |
ᵛsvastaye kṣemāya ᵛvāyumupa ᵛbravāmahai stuma ityarthaḥ |
ᵛsomaṃ copa bravāmahai |
ᵛyaḥ ca somaḥ ᵛbhuvanasya ᵛpatiḥ pālakaḥ |
sarvalokajīvanasya somāyattvāt |
tathā ᵛsarvagaṇaṃ sarvadevagaṇopetaṃ ᵛbṛhaspatiṃ bṛhataḥ karmaṇo mantrasya pālayitāraṃ ᵛsvastaye stumaḥ¹ |
ᵛādityāsaḥ ādityā aditaḥ putrāḥ sarve devā aruṇādayo dvādaśa vā ᵛnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵛsvastaye ᵛbhavantu ||
satramadhye dīkṣite vyādhite sati tasminnahani vaiśvadevaśastre'yaṃ tṛco nividdhānārthaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ svastyātreye nividaṃ dadhyāt ’ (āśva. śrau. 6. 9) iti |
svastyātreyaśabdenāyaṃ tṛco vivakṣita iti tatra vyākhyātam |
tathā bhayādike satyayaṃ tṛco japyaḥ ||
viśvḙ devā॒ no a॒dyā sva॒stayḙ vai॒śvāna̭ro va॒sura̭gniḥ sva॒stayḙ |
de॒vā a̭vantu॒bhava̭ḥ sva॒stayḙ sva॒sti no̭ ru॒draḥ pā॒tvaṃha̭saḥ ||
13 ||

anayā marutāṃ svarūpaṃ nirūpayati|
ha maruto yuṣmākaṃ ye prasiddhāḥ svabhāvaḥ svadīyasaḥ agniṣu ābharaṇeṣu śrayāḥ āśrayabhūtāḥ santi |

ye ca sakṣu mālyeṣu |
ye rukmeṣu urobhūpaṇeṣu |

‘ harateṣu khādiñca kṛtiśca ’ ( ṛ. saṃ. 1. 168. 3 ), ‘ patsu khādayaḥ ’ ( ṛ. saṃ. 5. 54. 11 ) iti hi śrutī bhavataḥ |
rathepu dhanvasu ca ye ca svabhānavaḥ śrāyāḥ santi |

tān sarvān stuma iti śeṣaḥ |

ᵥasme ᵥit asmāsveva ᵥastu ᵥsugnaṃ tvadāgamanajanitaṃ sukhaṃ ᵥvaḥ yuṣmākaṃ saṃbandhi yuṣmattvabhūtam |
taṃ vaḥ śardhaṃ rathānāṃ tveṣaṃ gaṇaṃ mārutaṃ narvyasīnām | anu pra yanti vṛṣṭayaḥ || 10 || tam | vaḥ | śardham | rathānām | tveṣam | gaṇam | mārutam | narvyasīnām | anu | pra | yanti | vṛṣṭayaḥ || 10 ||
ᵥtaṃ mārutamityanena saṃbadhyate |

yadvā |

he marutaḥ ᵥeṣāṃ ᵥvaḥ yuṣmākaṃ ᵥśardhaśardhaṃ tattadbalaṃ ᵥvrātavrātam |
avivakṣitagaṇaḥ² vrātaḥ |

yena tokāya tanayāya dhānyaṃ bījaṃ vahadhve akṣitam | asmabhyaṃ taddhattana yad īmahe rādho viśvāyu saubhagam || 13 || yena | tokāya | tanayāya | dhānyam | bījam | vahadhve | akṣitam | asmabhyam | tat | dhattana | yat | vaḥ | īmahe | rādhaḥ | viśva'āyu | saubhagam || 13 ||
1. kha-ca-ṇa-vācchanti; bha-vācchayanti |
2. ta-na-ra-vivakṣitagaṇaḥ |
he marutaḥ ᵥyena sadyena manasā ᵥtokāya putrāya ᵥtanayāya tatputrāya dhanāya vā ᵥdhānyaṃ ᵥbījam ᵥakṣitam akṣīṇaṃ ᵥvahadhve dhārayadhve tena cittena ᵥasmabhyaṃ ᵥtat dhānyaṃ bījaṃ ᵥdhattana |

‘pra śardhāya’ iti pañcadaśarcaṃ daśamaṃ sūktaṃ śyāvāśvasyārṣaṃ maruddevatākam |

ᵥvidyunmahasaḥ vidyunmānatejasaḥ ᵥnaraḥ vṛṣṭyādernetāraḥ ᵥaśmadidyavaḥ vyāsāyudhā aśmasāra- mayāyudhā vā ᵥvātatviṣaḥ prāptadīptayaḥ ᵥparvatacyutaḥ parvatānāṃ meghānāṃ vā cyāvayitāraḥ ᵥabdyā ᵥmuhuḥ udakānāṃ dātāraḥ ||
jamaḥᵡ yājādeśaḥ |

5. jha-ta- bha-ra-vivyatha |
tadvīryaṃ vo maruto mahitvanaṃ dīrghaṃ tatān sūryo na yojanam |
etā na yāme argṛbhītaśociṣo'naśdāṃ yatayatānā girima ||
5 ||
tat | vīryam | vaḥ | marutaḥ | mahi'tvanam | dīrgham | tatān | sūryaḥ | na | yojanam | etāḥ | na | yāme | argṛbhīta'śociṣaḥ | anaś'dām | yat | ni | aryātan | girima || 5 ||
he marutaḥ vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ tadvīryaṃ prasiddhaṃ sāmarthyaṃ mahitvanaṃ mahattvaṃ dīrgham atyantamāyataṃ tatāna tanoti |
loke sūryo na yojanaṃ sūryasteja iva |

yadvaitanmarutāṃ viśeṣaṇam |

atrāji | śardhaḥ | marutaḥ | yat | arṇasam | morṣatha | vṛkṣam | kapana'iva | vedhasaḥ | adha | sma | naḥ | aramatim | sa'joṣasaḥ | cakṣuḥ'iva | yantam | anu | neṣatha | su'gam || 6 ||
he vedhasaḥ vṛṣṭividhātāro he marutaḥ śardhaḥ bhavatāṃ gaṇam atrāji ājate | yat yasmāt arṇasam udakavantaṃ vṛkṣam | vṛśyate vidārayata iti vṛkṣo meghaḥ | taṃ mopatha tāḍayathetyarthaḥ | kapaneva | ityayanarthakaḥ | kampanāḥ santaḥ | yadvā | kapanāḥ kramayo vṛkṣaṃ ghṛṇādayaḥ | te yathā muṣṇanti tadvat | adha api ca | sma iti pūraṇaḥ | naḥ asmān | māmityarthaḥ | he sajoṣasaḥ yuṣmāsu parasparaṃ samānaprītayo yūyama aramatim āramaṇaṃ dhanādikaṃ prati yantaṃ gacchantam anu nepatha anukrameṇa nayatha sugaṃ sugamanaṃ mārgam | tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ | cakṣuriva | tadyathā mārgapradarśanena nāyakaḥ bhavati tadvat ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-śa- 'na' nāsti |
2. kha-ca-ṇa-te yajanam; jha-na-bha-me yajanam; ra-yāme yajanam; śa-yāme gamanāya yojanam |
3. kha-ca-ṇa-nihatavantaḥ sthāḥ; ga-gha-ja-śa-nihatavantaḥ |
4. ta-kapanā iva |
5. kha-ga-gha-ca-ja-ṇa-mārgapradarśane nāyakam; śa-mārgapradarśananāyakam |
na sa jīyate maruto na hanyate na sredhati na vyathate na riṣyati |
nāsya rāya upa dasyanti notaya ṛrṣi vā yaṃ rājānaṃ vā suṣūdatha ||
7 ||
na | saḥ | jīyate | marutaḥ | na | hanyate | na | sredhati | na | vyathate | na | riṣyati | na | asya | rāyaḥ | upa | dasyanti | na | ūtayaḥ | ṛrṣim | vā | yam | rājānam | vā | suṣūdatha || 7 ||
ha ˅marutaḥ yūyaṃ ˅yam ˅ṛrṣi mantradraṣṭāraṃ brāhmaṇaṃ ˅rājānaṃ ˅vā ˅suṣūdatha ˅kṣārayatha prerayatha satkarmasu ˅saḥ ṛṣī rājā vā ˅na ˅jīyate |
anyairna parābhāvyate |
‘ji jaye’ ‘jyā vayohānau’ ˅ityasya vā rūpam |
he marutaḥ ˅na ˅hanyate |
˅na ˅hiṃsyate atyarthaṃ prāṇairna viyujyate |
˅na ˅sredhati na kṣīyate |
˅na ˅vyathate na pīḍyate |
˅na ˅riṣyati na hiṃsyate |
atra hiṃsā bādhamātram |
˅asya ˅rāyaḥ dhanāni ˅na ˅upa2 ˅dasyanti nopakṣīyante |
tathā ˅notayaḥ rakṣā apyupa dasyanti |
kārīyāṃmeva tṛtīyasyāḥ piṇḍayā yājyā ‘niyutvantaḥ ’ ityeṣā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘niyutvanto grāmajito yathā naro'gre bādhamva vi mṛdho vi durgrahā ’ ( ādha. śrau. 2. 13 ) iti |
niyutvanto grāmajito yathā naro'ryamaṇo na marutaḥ kavandhinaḥ |
pinyantyutsaṃ yadināso asvaranyundanti pṛthivīṃ madhvo andhasā ||
8 ||
niyutvantaḥ | grāma'jitaḥ | yathā | naraḥ | aryamaṇaḥ | na | marutaḥ | kavandhinaḥ | pinyanti | utsam | yat | ināsaḥ | asvaran | vi | undanti | pṛthivīm | madhvaḥ | andhasā || 8 ||
˅niyutvantaḥ niyutsaṃjñakairśvastadvantaḥ marutaḥ |
˅grāmajitaḥ saṃghātatmakasya padārthasya viśleṣayitāraḥ ˅naraḥ narākārā netāro vā |
maruto niyutvantaḥ |
ayaṃ ˅śabdo'trāśvasāmānye vartate |
nitarāṃ yavana- vanto'śvavanto grāmajito grāmasya jenāro nara iva manuṣyā iva tathā bhavanti |
˅aryamaṇo ˅na aryamaprabhṛtaya ādityā iva |
dīptā iti śeṣaḥ |
tādṛkṣāḥ ˅marutaḥ ˅kavandhinaḥ udakavanto bhavanti |
˅yat yadā ˅ināsaḥ īśvarāḥ ˅utsaṃ kṛpādinimnapradeśaṃ meghaṃ vā ˅pinyanti pyāyayantyudakena |
yadā ˅asvaran śabdayanti yadā ˅dyundanti ˅pṛthivīṃ ˅madhvaḥ madhurasyodakasya ˅andhasā sāreṇa3 tadaivaṃ bhavati |
yadvā |
yadāsvaran tadā pinyantyusamināsaḥ tadāndhasaḥ pṛthivīṃ vyundantīti |
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-yaṃ suṣūdatha |
2. ta-tu upa |
3. kha-anena |
ṛgveda:
pravata̭vatīyaṃ pṛ̭thi॒vī ma॒rudbhya̭ḥ pravata̭vatī caurbhavati praya॒dbhya̭ḥ | pravata̭vatī | pṛ॒thvī | a॒ntari̭kṣyāḥ | pravata̭vantaḥ | pa॒rvatā̭ḥ jīradā̭navaḥ || 9 || pravata̭vatī | i॒yam | pṛ॒thi॒vī | ma॒rut'bhya̭ḥ | pravata̭vatī | cauḥ | bha॒vati | praya॒t'bhya̭ḥ | pravata̭vatī | pṛ॒thvī | a॒ntari̭kṣyāḥ | pravata̭vantaḥ | pa॒rvatā̭ḥ | jīra'dā̭navaḥ || 9 ||
^iyaṃ ^pṛthi॒vī ^marudbhyaḥ ^marutāmarthāya ^pravatavatī |
pravantaḥ prakarṣavanto vistīrṇāḥ pradeśā yasyāṃ sā pravatavatī |
tādṛśī ^bhavati |
kṛtsnāpi bhūmiḥ marutparā ^bhavatītyarthaḥ |
tāṃ sarvāmapi vyāmu- vantīti yāvat |
tathā ^cauḥ api ^prayadbhyaḥ prakarṣeṇa gacchadbhyastebhyaḥ ^pravatavatī bhavati |
sāpi marutāṃ saṃcārāya |
tathā ^antarikṣyāḥ antarikṣe bhavāḥ ^padhyāḥ ^manugato'pi ^pravatavatīḥ pravattvasya marudbhyo bhavanti |
^parvatāḥ ^adrayo meghā vā ^pravatavantaḥ bhavanti ^jīradānavaḥ kṣipradānā marudbhyaḥ ||
yanma̭rutaḥ sa॒bhara̭saḥ sva॒rnaḥ sūrya̭ u॒ditḙ ma॒rdathā̭ di॒vo na̭ḥ | na vo'śvā̭ḥ śrathayantā॒ha si̭srataḥ sa॒dyo a॒syādhva̭naḥ pā॒rama̭śrutha || 10 || yat | ma॒rutaḥ | sa॒'bhara̭saḥ | svaḥ'na̭raḥ | sūrya̭ | u॒t'i̭te | ma॒rdatha̭ | di॒vaḥ | naḥ | na | vaḥ | aśvā̭ḥ | śra॒tha॒ya॒ntā॒ha | si॒srata̭ḥ | sa॒dyaḥ | a॒sya | a॒dhvana̭ḥ | pā॒ram | a॒śrutha || 10 ||
he ^marutaḥ ^sabharasaḥ sahabalāḥ ^svarnaḥ sarvasya netāro yūyaṃ ^yat yadā ^sūrye ^udite madhyāhne ^madatha somena he ^divaḥ dyulokasya ^naraḥ netāraḥ |
yadā momaṃ pātumicchathetyarthaḥ |
tadā ^vo'śvāḥ ^sisrataḥ sarantaḥ ^na ^aha ^śrathayanta naiva śrathayanti |
^sadyaḥ tadānīmeva ^asyādhvanaḥ devajanamārgasya ^pāramaśrutha vyāmutha |
yadvā |
yadā somena madatha tadā tvadīyā aśvā na śithilā bhavanti |
yūyaṃ ca kṛtsnasya lokatrayamārgasya pāramaśrutha ||
15 ||
aṃsḙṣu va ṛ॒ṣaya̭ḥ patsu khā॒dayo̭ va॒kṣaḥsṷ ru॒kmā ma̭ruto ra॒the śu॒bhaḥ | agni̭bhrājaso vi॒dyuto॒ garbha̭styoḥ śi॒prāḥ śīrṣa̭su vi॒tatā॒ hira̭ṇyayīḥ || 11 || aṃsḙṣu | vaḥ | ṛ॒ṣaya̭ḥ | pat'su | khā॒daya̭ḥ | va॒kṣaḥ'su | ru॒kmāḥ | ma॒rutaḥ | ra॒the | śu॒bhaḥ | agni̭'bhrājasaḥ | vi॒dyuta̭ḥ | ga॒rbha'styoḥ | śi॒prāḥ | śīrṣa̭'su | vi॒tatā̭ḥ | hi॒ra॒ṇyayīḥ || 11 ||
aṃseṣu ^vaḥ ^ṛṣayaḥ | ^patsu ^khādayaḥ | ^vakṣaḥsu ^rukmāḥ | ^marutaḥ | ^rathe | ^śubhaḥ | ^agnibhrājasaḥ | ^vidyutaḥ | ^garbhastyoḥ | śiprāḥ | ^śīrṣasu ^vitatāḥ | ^hiraṇyayīḥ || 11 ||
1. kha-ca-ṇa-maruccārā; jha-bha-marutcārāya; ta-ra-marutsārāya; na-marutāmarthāya; śa-marudarthāya|
2. jha-na-bha-ra-taṃ parvatāḥ|
yūyaṃ rayiṃ marutaḥ spārhavīraṃ yūyamṛṣimavatha sāmavipram | yūyamarvantaṃ bharatāya vājaṃ yūyaṃ dhattha rājānaṃ śruṣmantam || 14 || yūyam | rayim | marutaḥ | spārha'vīram | yūyam | ṛṣima | avatha | sāma'vipram | yūyam | arvantam | bharatāya | vājam | yūyam | dhattha | rājānam | śruṣma'mantam || 14 ||
ha marutaḥ yūyaṃ rayiṃ dhanaṃ spārhavīraṃ spṛhaṇīyavīraiḥ putrabhṛtyādibhirupetaṃ dhātha dhattha |
ha marutaḥ yūyaṃ sāmavipraṃ sāmnāṃ trividhaṃ pragāyitāraṃ yadvā māmasahitā viprā yasya tādṛśam ṛṣimavatha rakṣatha |
ha yūyam arvantam aśvaṃ vājam annaṃ ca bharatāya devān bibhrate jyāvāśvāya dhattha |
ha marutaḥ yūyaṃ rājānaṃ dīptaṃ svāminaṃ vā śruṣmantaṃ sukhavantam |
putramityarthaḥ |
dhattha ||
tadvau yāmi draviṇaṃ sacaūtayo yenā svaḥrṇa tatanām nṛbhi | idaṃ su meṃ maruto haryatā vaco yasya tarema taramā śata himāḥ || 15 || tat | vaḥ | yāmi | draviṇam | sacaḥ'ūtayaḥ | yena | svaḥ | na | tatanām | nṛn | abhi | idam | su | me | marutaḥ | haryata | vacaḥ | yasya | tarema | tarasā | śatam | himāḥ || 15 ||
ha sacaūtayaḥ |
tadānīmeva rakṣā yeṣāṃ te tādṛśāḥ |
sadyogamanā vā vaḥ yuṣmān tat draviṇaṃ dhanaṃ yāmi yācāmahe |
yena dhanena nṛn asmatputrabhṛtyādīn abhi tatanām svarṇe āditya iva raśmīn |
he marutaḥ me mama svabhūtam idam idānīṃ kriyamāṇaṃ vacaḥ stotraṃ su suṣṭhu haryata kāmayadhvam |
yasya stotravacasaḥ tarasā balena śata śatasaṃkhyākān himāḥ hemantān tarema śatavatsaraṃ jīvemetyarthaḥ ||
16 ||
‘prayajyavaḥ’ iti daśarcamekādaśaṃ sūktaṃ jyāvāśvasyārṣaṃ mārutam |
daśamī triṣṭup śiṣṭā jagatyaḥ |
‘prayajyavo daśānyā ṛṣap’ ityanukramaṇikā |
ābhiluvike paṣṭhe'nyāgnimāruta idaṃ sūktaṃ mārutavinivedanīyam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘prayajyava imaṃ stomamityāgnimārutam’ ( ādha. śrau. 7. 7 ) iti ||
prayajyavo maruto ārjahaṣṭayo bṛhadvayo dadhire rukmavakṣasaḥ | īyante aśvaiḥ suyamaibhirāśubhiḥ śubhaṃ yātāmanu rathā avṛtsata || 1 || pra'yajyavaḥ | marutaḥ | ārjaha'ṛṣṭayaḥ | bṛhat | vayaḥ | dadhire | rukma'vakṣasaḥ | īyante | aśvaiḥ | suyamaibhiḥ | āśu'bhiḥ | śubham | yātām | anu | rathāḥ | avṛtsata || 1 ||
1. kha-ca-ṇa-yācāmi |
2. jha-ta-na-ra-kāmayata |
ᵛprayajavaḥ prakarṣeṇa yaṣṭāraḥ ᵛājahvayaḥ dīsāyudhāḥ ᵛmarutaḥ ᵛbṛhat prabhūtaṃ ᵛvayaḥ yauvanalakṣaṇaṃ prabhūtamnaṃ vā ᵛdadhire dhārayanti |
ᵛrumavakṣasaḥ hārāyatavakṣaskāḥ |
te marutaḥ ᵛsuyamebhiḥ sukhena niyamituṃ śakyaiḥ ᵛāśubhiḥ śīghragaiḥ ᵛaśvaḥ ᵛīyante prāpyante |
ᵛśubhaṃ śobhanaṃ yathā bhavati tathā |
yadvā |
udakamabhilakṣya ᵛyātāṃ gacchatāṃ mahatāṃ ᵛrathāḥ api ᵛanu ᵛavṛtsata anuvartante ||
svayaṃ dadhidhve tarvirṣī yathā vid bṛhanmahānta urviyā vi rājatha |
utāntarikṣaṃ mamire vyojasā śubhaṃ yātāmanu rathā avṛtsata ||
2 ||
svayam | dadhidhve | tarvirṣīm | yathā | vid | bṛhat | mahāntaḥ | urviyā | vi | rājatha | uta | antarikṣam | mamire | vi | ojasā | śubham | yātām | anu | rathāḥ | avṛtsata || 2 ||
he maruto yūyaṃ ᵛsvayam asahāyyenaiva ᵛdadhidhve dhārayadhve kurudhva ityarthaḥ |
kim |
ᵛtarvirṣīṃ balaṃ sāmarthyam |
ᵛyathā ᵛvid jānītha |
apratibaddhasāmarthyā ityarthaḥ |
haṃ ᵛmahāntaḥ yūyam ᵛurviyā uravaḥ santaḥ ᵛvi ᵛrājatha |
ᵛuta api ca ᵛantarikṣam ᵛojasā balena ᵛvi ᵛmamire vyāpnutha |
śubhamityādi gatam ||
sākaṃ jātāḥ subhvaḥ sākaṃmukṣitāḥ śriye cidā prataraṃ vāvṛdhurnaraḥ |
virokiṇaḥ sūryasyeva raśmayaḥ śubhaṃ yātāmanu rathā avṛtsata ||
3 ||
sākam | jātāḥ | su'bhvaḥ | sākam | ukṣitāḥ | śriye | cit | ā | pra'taram | vavṛdhuḥ | naraḥ | vi'rokiṇaḥ | sūryasya'iva | raśmayaḥ | śubham | yātām | anu | rathāḥ | avṛtsata || 3 ||
ᵛsākaṃ sahaiva ᵛjātāḥ utpannāḥ ᵛsubhvaḥ suṣṭhu bhavantaḥ |
mahānta ityarthaḥ |
tathaiva ᵛsākaṃ sahaiva ᵛukṣitāḥ sektāro varṣakāḥ ᵛśriye ᵛcit śobhāyā eva ᵛprataraṃ prakṛṣṭataram ᵛā sarvataḥ ᵛvavṛdhuḥ avadhayan |
ᵛnaraḥ karmaṇāṃ netāraḥ ᵛvirokiṇaḥ virocamānāḥ ᵛsūryasyeva ᵛraśmayaḥ sūryaśmaya iva |
śubhamityādi gatam ||
ābhuṣeṇyai vo maruto mahitvanaṃ didṛkṣeṇyaṃ sūryasyeva lakṣaṇam |
uto asā~ amṛtatve dadhātana śubhaṃ yātāmanu rathā avṛtsata ||
4 ||
ā'bhuṣeṇyam | vaḥ | marutaḥ | mahitvanam | didṛkṣeṇyam | sūryasya'iva | lakṣaṇam | uto iti | asmān | amṛta'tve | dadhātana | śu . m | yātām | anu | rathāḥ | avṛtsata || 4 ||
he ᵛmarutaḥ ᵛvaḥ yuṣmākaṃ ᵛmahitvanaṃ mahattvam ᵛābhuṣeṇyaṃ stutyam |
kiṃca ᵛsūryasyeva ᵛlakṣaṇaṃ rūpamiva ᵛdidṛkṣeṇyaṃ darśanīyam |
ᵛuto api ca ᵛasmān ᵛamṛtatve mokṣe svarga ityarthaḥ |
tatra ᵛdadhātana dhārayata |
śiṣṭaṃ gatam ||
kārīyāṃ prathamasyāḥ piṇḍyāḥ ' udīrathayā ' ityanuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—' udīrathayā marutaḥ samudrataḥ pra vo marutastaviṣā udanvataḥ ' ( āśva. śrau. 2. 13 ) iti ||
udīrathayā marutaḥ samudrato yūyaṃ vṛṣṭiṃ varṣayathā purīṣiṇaḥ |
na vo dastrā upa dasyanti dhenavaḥ śubhe yātāmanu rathā avṛtsata ||
5 ||
ut | īrathaya | marutaḥ | samudrataḥ | yūyam | vṛṣṭim | varṣayatha | purīṣiṇaḥ | na | vaḥ | dastrāḥ | upa | dasyanti | dhenavaḥ | śubham | yātām | anu | rathāḥ | avṛtsata ||5||
he marutaḥ ' yūyaṃ ' samudrataḥ samudravaṇādhanādantarikṣāt ' udīrathaya prerayatha ' vṛṣṭim |
ayamevārthaḥ punarucyate |
he ' purīṣiṇaḥ |
pṛṇateḥ prīṇātervā purīpamudakam |
he tadanto yūyaṃ vṛṣṭiṃ varṣayatha |
he ' dastrāḥ darśanīyāḥ śatrūṇāmupakṣapayitārā vā ' vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ ' dhenavaḥ prīṇayitāro meghāḥ na ' upa ' dasyanti na śuṣyanti ||
17 ||
yadaśvāndhurṣu pṛṣatīrayugdhvaṃ hiraṇyayānpratyatkāṃ amṛgdhvam |
viśvā itspṛdho maruto vyasyatha śubhe yātāmanu rathā avṛtsata ||
6 ||
yat | aśvān | dhuḥ'su | pṛṣatīḥ | ayugdhvam | hiraṇyayān | prati | atkān | amṛgdhvam | viśvāḥ | it | spṛdhaḥ | marutaḥ | vi | asyatha | śubham | yātām | anu | rathāḥ | avṛtsata || 6 ||
he maruto yūyaṃ ' yat yadā ' aśvān ' dhurṣu rathasambandhinīṣu ' ayugdhvaṃ yojitavantaḥ stha |
kīdṛśānaśvān |
' pṛṣatīḥ |
pṛṣatyo marutāmityuktatvāt pṛṣadgarbhā vaḍavāḥ |
sāraṅgī vātrāśvaśabda- vācyā |
' hiraṇyayān hiraṇyavarṇān ' atkān kavacān ' prati ' amṛgdhvaṃ pratyamuñcata |
evaṃ kṛtvā viśvā ' it ' spṛdhaḥ sarvānapi saṃgrāmān he ' marutaḥ ' vyasyatha vikṣipatha |
śubhamityādi gatam ||
na parvatā na nadyo varanta vo yatrācidhvaṃ maruto gacchatheḍu tat |
uta dyāvāpṛthivī yāthanā pari śubhe yātāmanu rathā avṛtsata ||
7 ||
na | parvatāḥ | na | nadyaḥ | varanta | vaḥ | yatra | acidhvam | marutaḥ | gacchatha | it | ū~ iti | tat | uta | dyāvāpṛthivī iti | yāthana | pari | śubham | yātām | anu | rathāḥ | avṛtsata ||7||
1. kha-ca-ṇa-bha-śarān |
he marutaḥ vaḥ yuṣmān parvatāḥ śiloccayāḥ na varanta na vārayantu tathā nadyaḥ ca na vārayantu |
kiṃtu yatra yasmin yajñādisthāne acidhvaṃ jānītha saṃkalpayatha tat sthānaṃ gacchathedu gacchathaiva |
uta api ca dyāvāpṛthivī ca pari paritaḥ yāthana yātha vṛṣṭyarthamiti ||
yatpuṇyaṃ maruto yacca nūtanaṃ yadudyatai vasavo yacca śasyate |
viśvasya tasya bhavathā narvedasaḥ śubhaṃ yātāmanu rathā avṛtsata ||
8 ||
yat | puṇyam | marutaḥ | yat | ca | nūtanam | yat | udyatai | vasavaḥ | yat | ca | śasyate | viśvasya | tasya | bhavatha | narvedasaḥ | śubham | yātām | anu | rathāḥ | avṛtsata || 8 ||
he marutaḥ yūyaṃ yat pūvyaṃ pūrvatanaṃ pūrvamanuṣṭata yata karmāsmi tadityarthaḥ |
yacca nūtanaṃ karmāsmi |
yat ca udyate ūrdhvaṃ prāpyate stūyate ityarthaḥ |
he vasavaḥ vāsakāḥ yacca śasyate śaṃsanaṃ kriyate tasya viśvasya uktasya narvedasaḥ jānantaḥ bhavatha ||
mūḻataṃ no maruto mā vadhiṣṭanāsmabhyaṃ śarma bahulaṃ vi yantana |
adhi stotrasya sruvasya gātanu śubhaṃ yātāmanu rathā avṛtsata ||
9 ||
mūḻata | naḥ | marutaḥ | mā | vadhiṣṭana | asmabhyam | śarma | bahulam | vi | yantana | adhi | stotrasya | sruvasya | gātanu | śubham | yātām | anu | rathāḥ | avṛtsata || 9 ||
he marutaḥ naḥ asmān mūḻata subhayata |
mā vadhiṣṭana |
asyakaraṇadijanitena kopena mā vadhidhvam |
kiṃtu asmabhyaṃ śarma sukhaṃ bahulam atyadhikaṃ vi yantana kuruta |
kiṃca stotrasya stotraṃ sravasya sravam abhi gātana adhigacchata |
stutimanbhuyāsmāsu sravyaṃ kurutetyarthaḥ ||
yūyasmānayata vasyo acchā niraihatibhyo maruto gṛṇānāḥ |
juṣadhvaṃ no havyadātiṃ yajatrā vayaṃ syāma patayo rayīṇām ||
10 ||
yūyam | asmān | nayata | vasyaḥ | acchā | niḥ | ahatibhyaḥ | marutaḥ | gṛṇānāḥ | juṣadhvam | naḥ | havyadātim | yajatrāḥ | vayam | syāma | patayaḥ | rayīṇām || 10 ||
1. kha-ca-ṇa-gamanāya |
2. ga-ca-jha-ṇa-bha-he vasavaḥ nivāsayitāraḥ yataḥ, ra-he vasavaḥ vāsa- yitāraḥ yat |
3. śa-uddhāryate uccāryate svaryate |
4. kha-ca-a-ṇa-bha-ra-he vasavo vāsakāḥ ' nāsti |
5. ga-gha-ja-śa-utkṛṣṭasya |
6. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-bha-ra-vadhadhvam |
he marutaḥ 'yūyamasmān 'vasyaḥ vasīyo dhanaṃ svargādilakṣaṇaṃ sthānaṃ vā 'accha abhilakṣya 'nayata prāpayata |
kiṃca 'aṃhatibhyaḥ pāpebhyaḥ 'niḥ nayata nirgamayata 'gṛṇānāḥ stūyamānāḥ ||
karmaṇi kartṛpratyayaḥ ||
'jupadhvaṃ sevadhvaṃ 'naḥ asmākaṃ 'havyadātiṃ havirdānavantaṃ yajñaṃ he 'yajatrāḥ yaṣṭavyā yūyam |
'vayaṃ ca 'rathīnāṃ bahuvidhānāṃ dhanānāṃ 'patayaḥ 'syāma bhavema ||
18 ||
'agne śardhantam ' iti navacaṃ dvādaśa sūktaṃ śyāvāśvasyārṣaṃ mārutam |
tṛtīyāsaptamyau satobṛhatyau śiṣṭā bṛhatyaḥ |
' agne nava bārhataṃ tṛtīyāsaptamyau satobṛhatyau ' ityanukramaṇikā ||
agne śardhantaṃ gaṇaṃ pṛṣṭaṃ rukmebhirañjibhiḥ | viśoṃ adya marutāmarva hvaye divaścidrocanādadhi || 1 || agne | śardhantam | ā | gaṇam | pṛṣṭam | rukmebhiḥ | añjibhiḥ | viśaḥ | adya | marutām | arva | hvaye | divaḥ | cit | rocanāt | adhi || 1 ||
he 'agne 'śardhantaṃ śatrūnabhibhavantaṃ yajñāyosasahamānaṃ vā 'gaṇaṃ marutāṃ gaṇam 'ā hvayeti śeṣaḥ |
kīdṛśaṃ gaṇam |
'rukmebhiḥ rukmaiḥ 2 rocamānaiḥ 'añjibhiḥ ābharaṇaiśca 'pṛṣṭam avayavitaṃ yuktamityarthaḥ |
'adya asmin yāgadine 'marutāṃ 'viśaḥ prajāḥ |
gaṇānityarthaḥ |
tān 'rocanāt rocamānāt 'divaḥ dyulokāt |
'adhi iti pañcamyarthānuvādī |
'cit pūraṇaḥ 3 |
'ava avasthādaramadabhimukhaṃ 'hvaye āhvayāmi ||
yathā cinmanyase hṛdā tadinme jagmurāśasaṃḥ | ye te nediṣṭhaṃ harvanānyāgamantānvardha bhīmasaṃdṛśaḥ || 2 || yathā | cit | manyase | hṛdā | tat | it | me | jagmuḥ | ā'śasaḥ | ye | te | nediṣṭham | harvanāni | ā'gaman | tān | vardha | bhīma'saṃdṛśaḥ || 2 ||
he agne tvaṃ 'hadā hṛdayena marutaḥ 'yathā 'cinmanyase |
ciditi pūjāyām |
yena prakāreṇa atipūjitaṃ jānāsi |
tevvādaraṃ karopīyarthaḥ |
'tadita tathaiva 'me madarthaṃ 'jagmuḥ gacchantu 'āśasaḥ āśaṃsitāra icchantaḥ śatrūn hiṃsanto vā |
anantaraṃ 'ye marutaḥ 'nediṣṭham atyantāntikaṃ saṃvidhāveva 'te tava 'havanāni āhvānāni śrutvā 'āgaman āgacchanti 'tān 'bhīmasaṃdṛśaḥ kālavilambāsahanane bhayaṃkaradarśanān 'vardha vardhaya haviṣvāpaṇena ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-asmaddattam |
2. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-śa- 'rukmaiḥ ' nāsti |
3. kha- ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra- 'cit pūraṇaḥ ' nāsti; śa-viditi pūraṇaḥ |
mīḻahuṣmatīva pṛthivī parā̭hatā mada̭ntyetya॒smatā | ṛkṣo na vo marutaḥ śimvīvā~ amau dudhro gauriva bhīmayuḥ || 3 || mīḻahuṣmatī'iva | pṛthivī | parā̭'hatā | mada̭ntī | eti | a॒smāt | ā | ṛkṣaḥ | na | vaḥ | marutaḥ | śimvī'vān | amaḥ | dudhraḥ | gauḥ'iva | bhīma'yuḥ || 3 ||
vmīḻahuṣmatī prabalasvāmikā vparāhatā anyairabhibhūtā vpṛthivī viva |
atra pṛthivīśabdaḥ tadadhiṣṭhitāṃ prajāṃ lakṣayati |
sā yathā svasvāminamupadrutābhigacchati tadvaditi |
marutāṃ sākalyena sarvathā prāsaṅgataḥ |
evaṃ vmadantī hṛṣyantī marutsenā vammana asmānityarthaḥ |
vā veti āgacchati |
he vmarutaḥ vvaḥ yuṣmākam vamaḥ balaṃ gaṇaḥ vṛkṣo vna abhiriva vśimīvān karmavān vdudhraḥ durdharaḥ vgauriva vbhīmayuḥ bhīmarvṛṣabhaiḥ yuktaḥ vgauriva |
sa yathā śimīvān karmavān ||
ni ye riṇantyoja̭sā vṛthā gāvo na dudhṷraiḥ | aśmānaṃ citsvarya̭ parva̭taṃ giri̭ pra cyā̭vayanti yāma̭bhiḥ || 4 || ni | ye | riṇanti | oja̭sā | vṛthā | gāvaḥ | na | duḥ'dhuraiḥ | aśmānam | cit | svarya̭m | parva̭tam | giri̭m | pra | cyā̭vayanti | yāma̭bhiḥ || 4 ||
vye marutaḥ vojasā sviyena balena vni vriṇanti hiṃsanti śatrūn vvṛthā anāyāsena svayaṃcāramātreṇa vgāvo vna vdudhuraḥ duḥkhena hiṃsyā aśvā iva |
te marutaḥ vsvaryaṃ śabdayantam vaśmānaṃ vyāsa vparvataṃ jagatpūrakodakavantam |
‘pavevānparvataḥ parva punaḥ : pṛṇāteḥ prīṇātervā’ (niru. 1. 20 ) iti niruktam |
tādṛśaṃ vgiriṃ megham |
yadvā |
parvata miti viśeṣyaṃ girimiti viśeṣaṇam nigirasyudakamiti giriḥ |
taṃ vyāmabhiḥ gamaneḥ vpra vcyāvayanti udakanirgamanārtham ||
uttiṣṭha nunameṣāṃ stomaiḥ samukṣitānām | marutāṃ purutamapūrvyaṃ gavāṃ sargamiva haye || 5 || ut | tiṣṭha | nunam | eṣām | stomaiḥ | sam'ukṣitānām | marutām | puru'tamam | apūrvyam | gavām | sargam'iva | haye || 5 ||
he maruto yūyam vuttiṣṭha uttiṣṭhata ||
vyatyayenaikavacanam ||
vnūnaṃ niścayam veṣāṃ vstomaḥ stotraiḥ vsamukṣitānāṃ vardhitānāṃ vmarutām |
yadvā |
stomairupahaya iti saṃbandhaḥ |
tādṛśāṃ marutāṃ vpurutamaṃ prabhūtatamam vapūrvyam |
na vidyate pūrvaḥ yebhyastamapūrvyaṃ vsargaṃ saṃghaṃ vgavām udakānāṃ prasiddhānāṃ gavāṃ vā sargaṃ saṃgham viva vhaye āhvaye ||
19 ||
1. kha-jha-na-bha-ra-amīva |
2. ta-yuktā |
3. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-pūrvāhaiḥ |
yuṅgdhvaṃ haruṣī rathe yuṅgdhvaṃ rathe ṣu rohitaḥ |
yuṅgdhvaṃ harī ajirā dhuri voḻhave vahiṣṭhā dhuri voḻhave ||
6 ||
yuṅgdhvam | hi | aruṣīḥ | rathe | yuṅgdhvam | rathe ṣu | rohitaḥ | yuṅgdhvam | harī iti | ajirā | dhuri | voḻhave | vahiṣṭhā | dhuri | voḻhave || 6 ||
ha maruto yūyaṃ rathe yuṣmadīye sarveṣāṃ sādhāraṇabhūte aruṣāḥ ārocamānā vaḍavāḥ yuṅgdhvaṃ yojayadhvam |
tathā rathe ṣu yuṣmadīyeṣu rohitaḥ rohitavarṇāḥ yuṅgdhvam |
harī aśvau ajirā ajirāśugamanau dhuri voḻhave vahanāya yuṅgdhvam |
ādarārthaṃ punarvacanam |
vahiṣṭhā voḍhṛtamau aśvau dhuri voḻhave ||
uta sya vājyaruṣastuvisvarniha sma dhāyi darśataḥ |
mā vo yāmeṣu marutaściraṃ karataṃ taṃ rathe ṣu codata ||
7 ||
uta | sya | vājī | aruṣaḥ | tuvisvarniḥ | iha | sma | dhāyi | darśataḥ | mā | vaḥ | yāmeṣu | marutaḥ | ciram | karata | pra | tam | rathe ṣu | codata || 7 ||
uta api ca sya saḥ vājī vejanavān arupaḥ ārocamānaḥ tuvisvarṇiḥ prabhūtadhvaniraśvaḥ |
arupa ityanena rohita ityuktaṃ bhavati |
'prirvahati rohitaḥ ' (ṛ. saṃ. 1. 39. 6) iti yuktam |
iha idānīṃ darśataḥ darśanīyaḥ san dhāyi rathe niyojitaḥ |
sma iti pūraṇaḥ |
he marutaḥ vo yāmeṣu gamaneṣu so'śvaḥ ciraṃ vilambaṃ mā karata mā karotu |
taṃ rathe ṣu rathe yuktamiti śeṣaḥ |
pra codata prerayata |
yathā vilambaṃ na kuryāttathā prerayata ||
rathaṃ nu mārutaṃ vayaṃ śravasyumā huvāmahe |
ā yasminstathau suraṇāni bibhratī sacā marutsu rodasī ||
8 ||
ratham | nu | mārutam | vayam | śravasyum | ā | huvāmahe | ā | yasmin | tasthau | su'raṇāni | bibhratī | sacā | marutsu | rodasī || 8 ||
vayama ātreyāḥ rathaṃ rahanasvabhāvaṃ mārutaṃ marutāṃ sambandhanaṃ rathaṃ śravasyum anvecchuṃ nu adya ā huvāmaheṃ āhvayāmaḥ |
yasmin rathe suraṇāni jalāni bibhratī dhārayantī rodasī rudrasya patnī marutāṃ mātā |
yadvā rudro vāyuḥ |
tatpatnī mādhyamikī devī marutsu sacā sahitā ā tasthau āsthitavatī taṃ rathamāhuve ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-ra-punarūcyate |
2. kha-ca-ṇa- sa vegavān; jha-na-bha-ra-vegavān |
3. kha- ga-gha-ca-ṇa-rathamāhuvema |
taṃ vaḥ śardhaṃ ratheśubhaṃ tveṣaṃ panasyumā huve |
yasmintsujātā subhargā mahīyate sacā marutsu mīḻhuṣī ||
9 ||
tam | vaḥ | śardham | rathe'śubham | tveṣam | panasyum | ā | huve | yasmin | su'jātā | su'bhargā | mahīyate | sacā | marut'su | mīḻhuṣī || 9 ||
he marutaḥ vaḥ śardhaṃ yuṣmadīyaṃ balaṃ gaṇaṃ ratheśubhaṃ rathe śobhanaṃ tveṣaṃ dīptaṃ panasyuṃ stutyam ā huve āhvaye |
yasmin śarye sujātā suṣṭhu pravṛddhā subhagā śobhanabhāgyātimahatī mīḻhuṣī |
‘mīḻhuṣṭama śivatama’ ityādī darśanāt mīḻvān rudraḥ |
tatpatnī mīḻhuṣī |
marutsu sacā saha mahīyate pūjyate taṃ śardhamā huve |
āpastambo'pi rudrapatnīṃ mīḻhuṣīsaṃjñayā vyavajahāra—‘uttarayā dakṣiṇa- syāmīśānamāvāhayati laukikyā vācottarasyāṃ mīḻhuṣīṃ madhye jayantam’ ( āpa. gṛ. 20. 1-3 ) iti ||

4 ||
pañcame'nuvāke ṣoḍaśa sūktāni |
tatra ‘ā rudrāsaḥ’ ityaṣṭacaṃ prathamaṃ sūktaṃ jyāvāśvasyārṣaṃ māhatam |
saptamyaṣṭamau triṣṭubhau śiṣṭāḥ ṣaḍ jagatyaḥ |
‘ārudrāso'ṣṭau dvitriṣṭubantam’ ityanukramaṇikā |
viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||
ā rudrāsa indravantaḥ sajopaso hiraṇyarathāḥ suvitārya gantana |
iyaṃ vo asmatprati haryate matistṛṣṇaje na diva utsa utdanyave ||
1 ||
ā | rudrāsaḥ | indra'vantaḥ | sa'joṣasaḥ | hiraṇya'rathāḥ | suvitārya | gantana | iyam | vaḥ | asmat | prati | haryate | matiḥ | tṛṣṇa'jai | na | divaḥ | utsaḥ | uda'nyave || 1 ||
he rudrāsaḥ rudraputrāḥ indravantaḥ indreṇa yuṣmatsvāminā tadvantaḥ sajoṣasaḥ parasparaṃ samāna- prītayaḥ hiraṇyarathāḥ hiraṇyamayarathāḥ mantaḥ suvitārya sugamanāya tatsādhanāya suṣṭhu sarvairgantavyāya yajñāya tadartham ā gantana āgacchata |
kimatra vidyate iti taducyate |
iyam asmat asmadīyā matiḥ stutiḥ vaḥ yuṣmān prati haryate kāmayate |
tasmādāgacchata |
udanyave udakecchave tṛṣṇaje gotamāya divaḥ dyulokasakāśāt utsaḥ udakaniṣyandā yathā yuṣmābhiḥ preritāstadvadasmadarthamapi āgatyābhimaṃtaṃ dadatetyarthaḥ ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-ma-prasiddhā |
2. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-saptamīm |
ṛ. 2-115
vāśīmanta ṛṣṭimanto manīṣiṇaḥ sudhanvāna iṣunto niṣviṇaḥ | svaśvāḥ stha surathāḥ pṛśnimātaraḥ svāyudhā maruto yātanā śubham || 2 || vāśī'mantaḥ | ṛṣṭi'mantaḥ | manīṣiṇaḥ | su'dhanvānaḥ | iṣu'mantaḥ | niṣviṇaḥ | su'aśvāḥ | stha | su'rathāḥ | pṛśni'mātaraḥ | su'āyudhāḥ | marutaḥ | yātana | śubham ||2||
he marutaḥ yūyaṃ vāśīmantaḥ | vāśīti lakṣaṇamādhanamāyudham | tadvantaḥ stha | ṛṣṭimantaḥ | ṛṣṭīnāṃ churikā | tadvantaḥ stha | evaṃ sarvatra yojyam | manīṣiṇaḥ manasa īśvarā manasvinaḥ | sudhanvānaḥ śobhanadhanuṣkāḥ | iṣumantaḥ bāṇavantaḥ | niṣviṇaḥ niṣvavantaḥ | svaśvāḥ stha śobhanāśvāḥ bhavatha | surathāḥ śobhanarathāḥ | pṛśnimātaraḥ pṛśneḥ putrāḥ | svāyudhāḥ svajñaparathādisakalāyudhopetāḥ | evaṃ mahātmānaḥ mantaḥ | śubhaṃ śobhanaṃ yathā bhavati tathā śubhamudakāya vā rathena yāthana gacchatha ||
dhunutha dyāṃ parvatāndāśuṣe vamu ni vo vanā jihate yārmano bhiyā | kopayatha pṛthivīṃ pṛśnimātaraḥ śubhe yadugrāḥ pṛṣatīrayugdhvam || 3 || dhunutha | dyām | parvatān | dāśuṣe | vasu | ni | vaḥ | vanā | jihate | yārmanaḥ | bhiyā | kopayatha | pṛthivīm | pṛśni'mātaraḥ | śubhe | yat | ugrāḥ | pṛṣatīḥ | ayugdhvam || 3 ||
he marutaḥ cām |
dinātyarthe |
parvatān meghān dāśuṣe havirdātre yajamānāya vasu dhanāni ca dhunutha prāpayatha |
vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ yāmanaḥ gamanasya niyā bhītyā vanā vanāni vṛkṣādisamūhāḥ ni jihate nitarāṃ kampante |
avanatā bhṛśaṃ śamantā ityarthaḥ |
he pṛśnimātaraḥ pṛśneḥ putrāḥ |
pṛśnaye vai payasāṃ marutāṃ jātāḥ ( tai. saṃ. 2. 2. 11.4 ) iti hi śrutiḥ |
he marutaḥ pṛthivīṃ kopayatha abhinantyāḥ kṣobhayatha |
he ugrāḥ udbhaṭabalā yūyaṃ yana yadā śubhe udakārthaṃ pṛṣatīḥ yuṣmadīyāḥ aśvāḥ ayugdhvaṃ yojayatha tadā kopayatha pṛthivīm ||
vātatvipo maruto varṣāninirjā yamāīva susaṃdṛśaḥ supeśasaḥ | piśaṅgāśvā aruṇāśvā arepasaḥ pratvakṣaso mahinā dyaurivoravaḥ || 4 || vāta'tviṣaḥ | marutaḥ | varṣa'nirnajaḥ | yamāḥ'iva | su'saṃdṛśaḥ | su'peśasaḥ | piśaṅga'aśvāḥ | aruṇa'aśvāḥ | arepasaḥ | pra'tvakṣasaḥ | mahinā | dyauḥ'iva | uravaḥ ||4||
1. ma-ga-gha-ca-ja-ṇa-ta-na-ma-yāthana; jhā-yothana; ra-yathena |
2. kha-ca-ṇa-ta-na-ma-gacchata; kṣa-gaccha |
3. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-yadā dyām |

^vātasvipa: sarvadā saṃprasadīsaya: ^varṇanirnaja: sṛṣṭe: śodhayitāra: |
athavā nirṇigiti rūpanāma |

^yamāiva yugalotpannā iva ^susadṛśa: parasparaṃ balarūpādibhiratyantaṃ sarūpā: ^supeśasa: śobhanarūpā: ^piśaṅgāśvā: piśaṅgavarṇāśvopetāstathā ^aruṇāśvā: ^arepasa: apāpā: ^pravakṣasa: prakṛṣṭa tanukartāro dveṣṭṛṇāṃ ^mahinā mahattvena ^dyauriva antarikṣamiva ^urava: vistīrṇā: |
uttalakṣaṇā nāma bhejire ityuttareṇa sambandha: ||
purudrupsā añjimantaḥ sudānavastveṣasaṃdṛśo anavabhrāṃdhasa: |
sujātāsau januṣā rukmavakṣaso divo arkā amṛtaṃ nāma bhejire ||
5 ||
purudrupsā: | añji'mantaḥ | su'dānava: | tveṣa'saṃdṛśa: | anavabhra'rādhasa: | su'jātāsa: | januṣā | rukma'vakṣasa: | diva: | arkā: | amṛtam | nāma | bhejire ||5||
^purudrupsā: prabhūtoṃdakā: ^añjimantaḥ ābharaṇavantaḥ ^sudānava: śobhanadānā: ^tveṣasaṃdṛśa: dīptarūpā: ^anavabhrarādhasa: anavabhradhanā: ^sujātāsa: śobhanajananā: ^januṣā janmanaivoktarūpā: ^rukmavakṣasa: hāravakṣaskā: ^arkā: pūjyā maruta: ^diva: dyulokādāgatya ^amṛtam amaraṇasādhanaṃ ^nāma udakaṃ namanahetukamuktarūpaṃ havirvā ^bhejire labdhavantaḥ ||

ṛṣṭayo vo maruto aṃsayoradhi saha ojo bāhvorbale hitam |
numṇā śīrṣasvāyudhā rathepu vo viśvā va: śrīradhi tanūpu pipiśa ||
6 ||

he ^maruta: ^va: yuṣmākam ^aṃsayoradhi ^ṛṣṭaya: āyudhaviśeṣā hitā: āśritā: |
vakṣyamāṇa- hitaśabdo vipariṇantavya: |
adhīti saptamyarthānuvādā |
tathā ^va: ^bāhvo: adhi ^saha: śatrūṇā- mabhibhāvukam ^oja: |
ojo nāmāṣṭamo dhātu: ^tadvat ^balaṃ ^hitaṃ nihitam |
^śīrṣasu śira:su ^numṇā numnāni hiraṇmayāni ^paṭṭaṇāpādīni nihitāni |
^rathepu ^āyudhā āyudhāni yuddhasādhanāni nihitāni |
^va: ^śrā: yuṣmākaṃ kānti: ^tanūpu yuṣmadīyeṣu ^adhi ^pipiśe adhiṣṭhitā āśritā ||
1. kha-ca-ṇa-sarvatrapradīsaya: ; ga-gha-ja-sarvadīpraya: ; śa-sarvadeśa prāptadīsaya: |
2. kha-ca-ṇa-śa-śobhana- jātā: |
3. na-dhātu: |
rasāsṛṅamāṃsamedo'sthimajjāśukrāṇi dhātava: iti saptadhātūnāṃ sāro'ṣṭamastu ojaso'pi mukhe yo (yā) kāntirbhāti sa ojo balaṃ ca lakṣyate |
4. kha-ca-ṇa-bha-śa-hiraṇmayāni |
gomadaśvāvadrathavatsuvīraṃ candravadrādho maruto dadā naḥ | praśasti naḥ kṛṇuta rudriyāso bhakṣīya vo'vaso daivyasya || 7 || gomat | aśvāvat | rathavat | suvīram | candravat | rādhaḥ | marutaḥ | dada | naḥ | pra'śastim | naḥ | kṛṇuta | rudriyāsaḥ | bhakṣīya | vaḥ | avasaḥ | daivyasya || 7 ||
ha marutaḥ naḥ asmabhyaṃ gomata bahubhirgobhirupetam aśvāvata bahubhiraśvairupetaṃ rathavata rathopetaṃ suvāra suṣṭhu putropetaṃ candravata hiraṇyopetaṃ rādhaḥ annaṃ dada dadata ||
vyatyayena ekavacanam ||
athavā loḍarthasya liṭo madhyamabahuvacanam ||
ha rudriyāsaḥ rudraputrā marutaḥ naḥ asmadīyāṃ praśastim samṛddhimītyarthaḥ |
tāṃ kṛṇuta kuruta |
vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ svabhūtam avasaḥ avaḥ rakṣaṇaṃ daivyasya daivyaṃ devāhaṃ bhakṣīya bhajeya ||
haye naro maruto mṛḻatā ntuviṃmaghāso amṛtā ṛtajñāḥ | satyaśrutaḥ kavayo yuvāno bṛhadgirayo bṛhadukṣamāṇāḥ || 8 || haye | naraḥ | marutaḥ | mṛḻata | naḥ | tuviṃ'maghāsaḥ | amṛtāḥ | ṛtajñāḥ | satya'śrutaḥ | kavayaḥ | yuvānaḥ | bṛhat'girayaḥ | bṛhat | ukṣamāṇāḥ || 8 ||
haye ha naraḥ netāraḥ marutaḥ naḥ asmān mṛḻata sukhayata asmabhyaṃ hitāya vā sukhino bhavata |
ha tuviṃmaghāsaḥ prabhūtadhanāḥ amṛtāḥ amaraṇasvabhāvāḥ ṛtajñāḥ udakasya yajñasya vā jñātāraḥ |
kartāra ityarthaḥ |
satyaśrutaḥ satyena satyaphalatvena prasiddhāḥ kavayaḥ medhāvinaḥ yuvānaḥ nityataruṇāḥ bṛhadgirayaḥ prabhūtastutayaḥ bṛhata atyadhikam ukṣamāṇāḥ havirbhiḥ sevitāḥ udakaṃ vā mihanto yūyaṃ mṛḻata ||

‘ namu nūnam ’ ityaṣṭacaṃ dvitīyaṃ sūktaṃ śyāvāśvasyārṣaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ mārutam |

viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||
tamu nūnaṃ tarviṣīmantameṣāṃ stuṣe guṇaṃ mārutaṃ navyasīnām | ya āśraśvā amaṃvṛddhahanta utteśire amṛtasya svarājaṃḥ || 1 || tam | ū~ iti | nūnam | tarviṣī'mantam | eṣām | stuṣe | guṇam | mārutam | navyasīnām | ye | āśu'aśvāḥ | ama̭'vat | vahante | uta | īśire | amṛtasya | sva'rājaḥ || 1 ||
1. kha ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra- ‘ rathavata... putropetaṃ ’ nāsti |
2. kha-ca-ṇa-bha-bahuvacanena; jha-ta-na-ra- bahuvacanam |
3. ta-tuvimaghāsaḥ |
4. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-bha-ra-sevitāḥ; śa-secitāḥ |
^tamu tameva pūrvaṃ stutameva ^mārutaṃ marutāṃ sambandhinaṃ ^taṃ ^gaṇaṃ ^tavipīmantaṃ dīptimantaṃ ^nūnam atha ^stuṣe |
kīdṛśāṃ marutām |
^najyamīnām ||
liṅgavyatyayaḥ ||
navatarāṇāṃ stutyānāṃ vā |
^eṣāṃ ^mārutaṃ gaṇam |
gaṇāvayavabhūtā marutaḥ |
kīdṛśā ityucyate |
^ye marutaḥ ^āśvathāḥ śīghragamya- śraupetāḥ ^amavata balavanto yathā bhavanti tathā ^vahante ^gacchanti^2 |
^uta api ca ^īśire īśvarā bhavanti ^amṛtasya udakasya ^svarājaḥ svāyattadīsayaḥ svayameva rājamānāḥ teṣāṃ gaṇaṃ stuṣe ||
tveṣaṃ guṇaṃ tavasaṃ khādihastaṃ dhuniṃvrataṃ māyinaṃ dātivāram |
mayobhuvo ye armitā mahitvā vandasva vipra tuvirādhaso nṛn ||
2 ||
tveṣam | gaṇam | tavasam | khādi'hastam | dhuni'vratam | māyinam | dāti'vāram | mayaḥ'bhuvaḥ | ye | armitāḥ | mahi'tvā | vandasva | vipra | tuvi'rādhasaḥ | nṛn || 2 ||
^tveṣaṃ dīptaṃ ^gaṇaṃ mārutaṃ ^tavasaṃ balavantaṃ ^khādihastaṃ kaṭakahastaṃ ^dhuniṃvrataṃ kampayitṛ- karmāṇaṃ ^māyinaṃ prajñāvantaṃ ^dātivāraṃ dattadhanaṃ ha ^vipra hotaḥ ^vandasva stuhīti sambandhaḥ |
^ye marutaḥ ^mayobhuvaḥ sukhasya bhāvayitāraḥ ^mahitvā mahattvena ^amitāḥ aparicchinnāḥ |
tān ^tuvirādhasaḥ prabhūtadhanāna ^nṛn netṛn vandasva ||
ā vo yantūdvāhāso adya vṛṣṭiṃ ye viśve maruto junanti |
ayaṃ yo agnirmarutaḥ samiddha etaṃ juṣadhvaṃ kavayo yuvānaḥ ||
3 ||
ā | vaḥ | yantu | ut'vāhāḥ | apa | vṛṣṭim | ye | viśve | marutaḥ | junanti | ayam | yaḥ | agniḥ | marutaḥ | sam'iddhaḥ | etam | juṣadhvam | kavayaḥ | yuvānaḥ || 3 ||
^ye ^viśve vyāptāḥ ^marutaḥ ^vṛṣṭiṃ ^junanti prerayanti te ^udavāhāḥ udakasya voḍhāro marutaḥ ^adya idānīṃ ^vaḥ yuṣmāna ^ā ^yantu āgacchantu ha ṛtvijyamānāḥ |
atha pratyakṣavādaḥ |
^he ^marutaḥ ^vayaḥ^4 pramiddhaḥ ^ayam ^agniḥ ^samiddhaḥ samyak dīpitaḥ ^etam agni ^juṣadhvaṃ sevadhvaṃ he ^kavayaḥ medhāvinaḥ ^yuvānaḥ nityataruṇāḥ sarvatra vyāptā vā ||
yuyaṃ rājānamirya janāya vimvataṣṭaṃ janayatha yajatrāḥ |
yuṣmadaiti muṣṭihā bāhujūto yuṣmatsadśvo marutaḥ suvīraiḥ ||
4 ||
yuyam | rājānam | iryam | janāya | vi'vat'taṣṭam | janayatha | yajatrāḥ | yuṣmat | eti | muṣṭi'hā | bāhu'jūtaḥ | yuṣmat | sat'aśvaḥ | marutaḥ | su'vīraiḥ || 4 ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-^eṣāṃ ^nāsti |
2. kha-ca-ṇa-bha-vahanto gacchanti; jha-ta-na-ra-vahanto gacchantaḥ; śa-vahanti gacchantaḥ |
3. jha-na-bha-marutaḥ yūyam |
4. kha-ga-gha-ca-ja-ṇa-ayaṃ prasiddho yaḥ |
he marutaḥ| ᵥyūyaṃ ᵥjanāya yajamānāya ᵥrājānaṃ svāminaṃ rājamānaṃ vā ᵥhyaṃ śatrūṇāṃ prerakaṃ cyāvayitāraṃ ᵥviśvatapm | viśvā nāma ṛbhūṇāṃ madhyamaḥ | sa kuśalī | tena nirmitam | atyantarūpavantamityarthaḥ | tādṛśaṃ putraṃ ᵥjanayatha utpādayata he ᵥyajatrāḥ yaṣṭavyāḥ | he ᵥmarutaḥ ᵥyuṣmat yuṣmattaḥ ᵥṇati gacchati putraḥ | kīdṛśaḥ | ᵥmuṣṭihā | muṣṭihabdā bāhūpalakṣakaḥ | svabhujavalenaiva hantā śatrūṇām | tadavocyate | ᵥbāhūntaḥ | bāhuḥ prerakaḥ śatrūṇāṃ yasya tādṛśaḥ putra ᵥiti | tathā ᵥyuṣmat yuṣmattaḥ ᵥsadṛśaḥ vidyamānāḥ | bahvaḥ ityarthaḥ | ᵥsuvīraḥ śobhanavīryaḥ putra ᵥiti | jāyate ityarthaḥ ||
varuṇapraghāse mārutyā āmikyāyāḥ ‘ arāiva ’ iti yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca-- ‘ arāiveda- caramā ahevame varu ṇa śrudhi ’ ( āśva. śrau. 2. 17 ) iti |
ekādaśine mārate paśāveva paśupuroḍāśasya yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca-- ‘ arāivedacaramā aheva yā vaḥ śarma śaśamānāya santi ’ ( āśva. śrau. 3. 7 ) iti ||
arāivedacaramā aheva prapra jāyante arkavā mahobhiḥ |
pṛśraiḥ putrā upamāmo ramiṣṭhāḥ svaryā matyā marutaḥ saṃ mimiksuḥ ||
5 ||
arāḥ'iva | it | acaramāḥ | ahā'iva | pra'pra | jāyante | arkavāḥ | mahaḥ'bhiḥ | pṛśraiḥ | putrāḥ | upa'māsaḥ | ramiṣṭhāḥ | svaryā | matyā | marutaḥ | sam | mimiksuḥ || 5 ||
ᵥarāivedat rathaśakta iva ᵥacaramāḥ anikṛṣṭāḥ |
sarvasamāḥ sahaivopakṣāśreyarthaḥ |
ᵥaheva ahānīva |
divasā yathā sarve'pi paṣṭivaṭikātmakāntadassasamanvayopetasaptagaṇarūpeṇa samā ityarthaḥ |
anena dṛṣṭāntadvayena vaisādṛśyābhāvaḥ ᵥpratipāditaḥ |
evamuktalakṣaṇāḥ ᵥmahābhiḥ tejobhiḥ ᵥprapa ᵥjāyante prakarṣeṇa prādurbhavanti ||
ekaḥ ‘ prasamupohaḥ pādapūraṇe ’ ( pā. sū. 8. 1. 6 ) ityupasargā- bhyāsaḥ sa ca pūraṇaḥ ||
te ca ᵥakavāḥ analpāḥ |
yadvā mahābhirityasya viśeṣaṇam |
ᵥpṛśraiḥ mādhyamikāyā vāco gaॉrūpāyā antarikṣasya vā putrasthānīyāḥ |
ᵥupamāsaḥ pratyekaṃ samānāḥ ᵥramiṣṭhāḥ prakṛṣṭavegāḥ |
evaṃ mahānubhāvāḥ ᵥmarutaḥ ᵥsvayā ᵥmatyā svakīyayaivānugrahabuddhyā ᵥsaṃ ᵥmimiksuḥ vṛṣṭyā samyak siñcanti ||
yatprāyāsiṭa pṛṣatībhiraśvairvīḻupavibhirmaruto rathaubhiḥ |
kṣodanta āpo riṇate vanānyavosriyo vṛṣabhaḥ krandatu dyauḥ ||
6 ||
yat | pra | ayāsiṭa | pṛṣatībhiḥ | aśvaiḥ | vīḻupavi'bhiḥ | marutaḥ | rathaibhiḥ | kṣodante | āpaḥ | riṇate | vanāni | ava | usriyaḥ | vṛṣabhaḥ | krandatu | dyauḥ || 6 ||
1. jha-ta-ra-rājānaḥ |
2. kha-ca-ṇa-eva sādṛśyabhāvaḥ ; jha-na-bha-eva sādṛśyabhāvaḥ; śa-baisādṛśya- bhāvaḥ |
he marataḥ yat yadā prāyāsiṣṭa pragacchatha pṛṣatībhiḥ pṛṣatsaṃjñakaiḥ aśvaiḥ vāhanasādhanairaśvaiḥ |
aśvaśabdo'tra vāhanasāmānyavacanaḥ |
ataḥ puṃliṅgatā |
tathānyatra - 'yadaśvāndhūrṣu pṛṣatīrayudhvam' ( ṛ. saṃ. 5. 55. 6 ) |
rāṃhidaśvo'jāśva iti sāmānādhikaraṇyaprayogaḥ |
evaṃbhūtāśvayutaiḥ vīḻu- pavibhiḥ dṛḍharathanemibhiḥ rathaibhiḥ prāyāsiṣṭa |
yadvā |
aśva rathaiśca saha maruto yadā prāyāsiṣṭa tadā āpaḥ kṣodante kṣaranti |
vanāni vṛkṣamūhāḥ riṇante hisyante vegena bhayyante |
ummriyaḥ uṣāḥ sūryyaraśmayaḥ |
tatsambandhī parjanyaḥ vṛṣabhaḥ apāṃ varṣitā dyauḥ dyotamānaḥ ava krandatu avāṅmukhaṃ śabdayatu vṛṣṭyartham ||
prathiṣṭa yāmanpṛthivīṃ cideṣāṃ bhaṃtaiva garbhaṃ svāmicchavau dhuḥ |
vātānvyaśvāṃdhuryāyuyuñje varṣa svedaṃ cakrire rudriyāsaḥ ||
7 ||
prathiṣṭa | yāman | pṛthivī | cit | eṣām | bhartā'iva | garbham | svam | ita | arveḥ | dhuḥ | vātān | hi | aśvān | dhuri | ā'yuyuñje | vṛṣabham | svedam | cakrire | rudriyāsaḥ || 7 ||
eṣāṃ marutāṃ yāman yāmani gamane pṛthivī cit bhūmiḥ |
ciditi pūraṇaḥ |
prathiṣṭa prayātābhavat |
garbhayogyābhavadityarthaḥ |
te ca marutaḥ bhartā laukikaḥ patiḥ garbham iva bhāryāyāḥ tathā bhūmyāḥ svamin svabhūtaṃ garbhaṃ garbhasthānīyam śavaḥ udakaṃ dhuḥ sthāpitavantaḥ |
hi yasmāt vātān gantun aśvān dhuri rathasaṃbandhinyām āyuyuñje āyojitavantaḥ tasmāt varṣa svedaṃ svedasthānīyaṃ vṛṣṭisaṃsthāyaṃ cakrire kurvanti rudriyāsaḥ rudraputrāḥ ||
haye naro maruto mṛḻatāṃ nasturvīmaghāso amṛtā ṛtajñāḥ |
satyaśrutaḥ kavayo yuvāno bṛhadbhirayo bṛhadukṣamāṇāḥ ||
8 ||

‘pra vaḥ spad’ ityaṣṭacaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sūktaṃ śyāvāśvasyārṣaṃ mārutam |

‘pra vaḥ spad triṣṭubantam’ ityanukramaṇikā |
viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-puṃliṅganāma |
2. na-rathaibhiḥ rathaiḥ |
3. kha-ca-ṇa-pustakaṣvasya mantrasya saṃpūrṇa bhāṣyaṃ vidyate |
pra vaḥ spaḻkantusuvitārya dāvane'rcī dive pra pṛthivyā ṛtaṃ bhare |
ukṣante adhvāntaruṣanta ā rajo'nu svaṃ bhānuṃ śrathayante arṇavaiḥ ||
1 ||
pra | vaḥ | spaṭ | akan | suvitārya | dāvane | arca | dive | pra | pṛthivyai | ṛtam | bhare | ukṣante | adhvān | taruṣante | ā | rajaḥ | anu | svam | bhānum | śrathayante | arṇavaiḥ || 1 ||
he marataḥ ᵥvaḥ yuṣmabhyaṃ ᵥsuvitāya suṣṭhu prāptavyāya ᵥdāvane haviṣpradānāya ᵥspaṭ spaṣṭa hotā ᵥpra prakarṣeṇa ᵥakan krandati stauti |
vaḥ yuṣmān vā suṣṭhu prāptavyāya akan ||
krandateḥ śabdakarmaṇo labdharthe laṅi tipi rūpam ||
he hota: ᵥdive dyotamānāya yudevāya ᵥpra ᵥarca prakarṣeṇa stuhi he ātman |
tathā ᵥpṛthivya
he maruto yūyaṃ vgavāṃ vśrum viva vuttamam vutkṛṣṭamuṣṇīṣapvādikaṃ vśriyese śriye dhārayatheti śeṣaḥ |
vsūryo vna sūrya iva |
sa yathā vrajasaḥ tejamaḥ vvisarjana vcakṣuḥ darśanasādhanaṃ maṇḍalaṃ dhatte tadvadrajo vvṛṣṭivisarjane visarjanāya cakṣuḥ sarvasya prakāśakaṃ tejo dhārayatha |
vatyāiva aśvāiva vsubhvaḥ suṣṭhu sarvatra bhavanto vegavantaḥ vcāravaḥ darśanīyāḥ vmthana bhavatha |
vmaryāḥ martyāḥ yajamānāḥ viva |
te yathā vśriyese jānanti yajñādikaṃ tadvacchriye vcetana he vnaraḥ netāro marutaḥ ||
ko vo̭ ma॒hānta̭ maha॒tāmuda̭śra॒tvakā॒vyā ma̭rutaḥ |
ko ha॒ pauṃsyā̭ |
yu॒gaṃ ha॒ bhūmī̭ ki॒ra॒ṇaṃ na rḙjatha॒ pra ya॒dvara̭dhve su॒vitārya̭ dā॒vanai̭ ||
4 ||
kaḥ | vaḥ | ma॒hānta̭ | ma॒hatā̭m | ut | a॒śra॒vat | kaḥ | kā॒vyā | ma॒ruta̭ḥ | kaḥ | ha | pauṃsyā̭ | yu॒gam | ha | bhūmī̭m | ki॒ra॒ṇam | na | rḙjatha | pra | ya॒t | bha॒ra॒dhve | su॒vitārya̭ | dā॒vanai̭ || 4 ||
he marutaḥ vvaḥ yuṣmākaṃ vmahatāṃ pūjyānāṃ vkaḥ yajamānaḥ vmahānta śreyāṃsi vudabhavata utkṛṣṭaṃ prāpnuyāt |
vkaḥ ca tadarthaṃ vkāvyā kāvyāni kavīnāṃ yuṣmākaṃ sambandhīni stotrāṇi udabhavata |
vko vha kaśca vpauṃsyā pauṃsyāni parākramān udabhavata |
ādarārthaṃ punarvacanam |
vyūyaṃ vha yūyaṃ khalu vbhūmiṃ vkiraṇaṃ vna kiraṇamiva vrejatha |
kiraṇaṃ yathā vṛṣṭyarthaṃ cālayatha tadvanmapi rejatha |
vyata yasmāt vsuvitārya suṣṭhu prāptavyāya |
prāptavyasyetyarthaḥ |
tādṛśasya dhanasya vṛṣṭyudakasya vā vdāvane dānāya vpra vbharadhve prakarṣeṇa saṃpādayatha vṛṣṭim |
yasmādyūyamevaṃ kurutha tasmāt evaṃ karotītyarthaḥ ||
aśvā̭ive॒darṷṣāsaḥ sa॒rbandha̭vaḥ śū॒rā i̭va pra॒yudha̭ḥ pro॒ta yṷyudhuḥ |
ma॒ryā i̭va su॒vṛdho̭ vā॒vadhṷrna॒raḥ sū॒ryasya॒ cakṣṷḥ pra mi̭nanti vṛ॒ṣṭibhi̭ḥ ||
5 ||
aśvā̭ḥ | i॒va | i॒t | a॒ru॒ṣāsa̭ḥ | sa॒rbandha̭vaḥ | śū॒rāḥ | i॒va | pra॒yudha̭ḥ | pra | u॒ta | yu॒yudhṷḥ | ma॒ryā̭ḥ | i॒va | su॒vṛdha̭ḥ | va॒vṛdhṷḥ | na॒raḥ | sū॒ryasya̭ | cakṣṷḥ | pra | mi॒na॒nti | vṛ॒ṣṭi'bhi̭ḥ || 5 ||
maruta ete vaśvāiva śīghragantāraḥ varupāsaḥ ārocamānāḥ vsambandhavaḥ |
samāna eka eva bandhurbandhako rudro yeṣāṃ te tādṛśāḥ |
parasparaṃ bandhavaḥ snehayuktā vā vśūrāḥ bhaṭāḥ viva vprayudhaḥ prayoddhāraṃ vairibhiḥ saha tādṛśāḥ vuta api ca vpra vyuyudhuḥ prakarṣeṇa yudhyante |
vmaryāiva manuṣyā yathā vardhante tathā vsuvṛdhaḥ suṣṭhu vardhayitāro vardhamānā vā vnaraḥ netāraḥ vvavṛdhuḥ vardhante |
evaṃ mahātmānaḥ vsūryasya vcakṣuḥ tejaḥsamūhaṃ maṇḍalaṃ vā vvṛṣṭibhiḥ vpra vminanti prakarṣeṇa hiṃsanti āvṛṇvantītyarthaḥ ||

⁹sujātāsaḥ suṣṭhu saṃbhṛtāḥ ¹⁰januṣā janmanā ¹¹pṛśnimātaraḥ pṛśneḥ putrāḥ ¹²maryāḥ manuṣyebhyo hitā yūyaṃ ¹
vna: asmadarthaṃ vdyau: antarikṣaṃ vmimātu nirmimānu vṛdhim | tadanukūlaṃ karotvityartha: | kīdṛśī | vaditi: adīnā | yadvā | dyaurnimātvaditirbhūrmiśra mimātu suvam | kimartham | vvītaye upattaye prajananāya vā | vdānucitrā: vicitraprakāśādidānā: vupasa: vsaṃ vyatanantām | he vṛṣe tvayā vguṇānā: stūyamānā: vmarutaḥ vrudramya | putrā iti śeṣa: | vte vdivyaṃ vkośaṃ meghamudakam vācucyavu: ācyāvayanti || 24 ||
‘īḻe agniṃ mavasam’ ityṛcaṃ caturthaṃ sūktaṃ śyāvāśvasyārṣam |
saptamyaṣṭamyau jagatyau śiṣṭā: ṣaṭ triṣṭubha: |
maruddevatākamaprimaruddevatākaṃ vā |
‘īḻe dvijagatyannimāgneyaṃ ca vā’ iti |
sūktaviniyogo laiṅgika: |
ādyā kārīyāṃ nāmidhenīṣu yājyā |
mūtritaṃ ca--‘
bhīyā |
ᵥpṛthivī ᵥcita pṛthivyāpi ᵥrejate |
ᵥparvataścina parvatā'pi rejate kampate |
uttarārdhaḥ pratyakṣakṛtaḥ |
ᵥpūrvādheṃ ya evamakurvan teṣāṃ stomamadhyāmiti pūrvatrānvayaḥ' |
kārīyāṃ mārutasya saptakapālamya 'parvataścina' ityeṣā yājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca— 'parvataścinmahi vṛddho bibhāya sṛjanti raśmimojasā ' ( āśva. śrau. 2. 13 ) iti |
parvataścinmahi vṛddho bibhāya divaścinmānu rajata svane veḥ |
yatkīḻatha maruta ṛṣṭimanta āpaiva madhyañjo dhavadhve |
