parvataḥ | cit | mahi | vṛddhaḥ | bibhāya | divaḥ | cit | sānu | rajata | svane | vaḥ | yat | kīḻatha | marutaḥ | ṛṣṭi'mantaḥ | āpaḥ'iva | madhyañcaḥ | dhavadhve || 3 ||
he marutaḥ ᵥvaḥ yuṣmākaṃ ᵥsvane bhayaṃkaraśabde sati ᵥparvataścina parvato'pi ᵥmahi mahāna ᵥvṛddhaḥ sannapi ᵥbibhāya bibheti |
ᵥdivaścita antarikṣasyāpi ᵥmānu samucchritaḥ ᵥpradeśaḥ ᵥrejata kampate |
ᵥyat yadā ᵥkīḻatha he ᵥmarutaḥ yūyam ᵥṛṣṭimantaḥ āyudhamantaḥ sanmastadā ᵥāpaiva udakānāṃva ᵥmadhyañcaḥ mahājanāḥ ᵥdhavadhve dhāvadhve gacchatha |
varāivadraivatāso hiraṇyairabhi svadhābhistanvaḥ pipiśre |
śriye śreyāṃsastvaso ratheṣu satrā mahāṃsi cakrire tanūṣu |
4 |
varāḥ'iva | it | raivatāsaḥ | hiraṇyaiḥ | abhi | svadhābhiḥ | tanvaḥ | pipiśre | śriye | śreyāṃsaḥ | tvasaḥ | ratheṣu | satrā | mahāṃsi | cakrire | tanūṣu || 4 ||
ᵥvarāiva vivāheyogyā yuvāna iva |
te yathā ᵥraivatāmaḥ dhanavantaḥ ᵥhiraṇyaiḥ hiraṇya- mayairābharaṇaviśeṣaiḥ ᵥsvadhābhiḥ udakaiśca ᵥtanvaḥ svīyāni śarīrāṇi ᵥabhi ᵥpiviśre saṃyojayanti alaṃkurvanti tadvadete maruto'pi raivatāmaḥ dhanavantaḥ ᵥhiraṇyaiḥ hiraṇyasthānīyairviśuddhasyairābharaṇaiḥ svadhābhiśca tanvaḥ svīyāni śarīrāṇi pipiśre |
ᵥśriye śobhāyai ᵥśreyāṃsaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ ᵥtavasaḥ balavanto marutaḥ ᵥratheṣu ᵥsatrā satyaṃ saha vā ᵥtanūpu ᵥmahāṃsi ᵥcakrire kṛtavantaḥ |
sarvābharaṇairudakaiścopetāḥ rathānadhiṣṭhāya4 śarīreṣu tejāṃsyadhārayannityarthaḥ' |
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-bha-ra-pūrvata; na-pūrvata saṃbandhaḥ |
2. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-samucchritapradeśam |
3. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-rejatha (rejata-na) kampayatha (kampayathe-jha) |
4. kha-ca-ṇa-na-ratheṣvadhiṣṭhāya; jha-bha-ra-rathādhiṣṭhāya |
5 ga-gha-ja-dhārayannityarthaḥ |
itpūraṇaḥ |
ajyeṣṭhāso akaniṣṭhāsa ete saṃ bhrātaro vāvṛdhuḥ saubhagāya |
yuvāṃ pitā khapā rudra eṣāṃ sudaghā pṛśniḥ sudinā marudbhyaḥ ||
5 ||
ajyeṣṭhāsaḥ | akaniṣṭhāsaḥ | ete | sam | bhrātaraḥ | vāvṛdhuḥ | saubhagāya | yuvām | pitā | su'apāḥ | rudraḥ | eṣām | su'dughā | pṛśniḥ | su'dinā | marut'bhyaḥ ||5||
ajyeṣṭhāsaḥ akaniṣṭhāsaḥ parasparaṃ jyeṣṭhakaniṣṭhabhāvarahitāḥ sahaivonmaṣāḥ samānabalāḥ |
ete mahataḥ bhrātaraḥ parasparaṃ bhrātṛbhūtāḥ santaḥ saubhagāya subhagatvāya saṃ vāvṛdhuḥ vardhante |
yuvā nitya- taruṇaḥ svapāḥ śobhanakarmā eṣāṃ marutāṃ rudraḥ pitā sudughā suṣṭhu dogdhrī pṛśniḥ godevatā mātṛbhūtā marudbhyaḥ marudarthaṃ sudinā śobhanadināni |
akurūnmati śeṣaḥ ||
yaduttame maruto madhyame vā yadvāvame subhagāso divi ṣṭha |
ato no rudrā uta vā nṛsyāgre vittāddhaviṣā yadyajāma ||
6 ||
yat | ut'tame | marutaḥ | madhyame | vā | yat | vā | avame | su'bhagāsaḥ | divi | stha | ataḥ | naḥ | rudrāḥ | uta | vā | nu | asya | agre | vittāt | haviṣaḥ | yat | yajāma ||6||
he marutaḥ yūyaṃ yaduttame utkṛṣṭa uttame carame vā divi dyuloke madhyame vā divi yadvāvame divi he subhagāsaḥ stha bhavatha |
trayo vā ime trivṛto lokāstatro divaḥ pṛthivī ityādiśruteryulāṃkamya traividhyam |
ataḥ asmāt sthānatrayāta naḥ asmadarthaṃ he rudrāḥ āgaccha- teti śeṣaḥ |
uta vā athavā nu adya yadyajāma yajñaviḥ prakṣapāma he agne tvam asya haviṣaḥ etaddhaviḥ vittāt viddhi ||
agniśca yanmarutāṃ viśvavedaso divo vahdhu uttarāta adhi snubhiḥ |
te maṃdasānā dhunayo riṣādaso vāmaṃ dhatta yajamānāya sunvate ||
7 ||
agniḥ | ca | yat | marutaḥ | viśva'vedasaḥ | divaḥ | vahdhve | ut'tarāt | adhi | snu'bhiḥ | te | maṃdasānāḥ | dhunayaḥ | riṣādasaḥ | vāmam | dhatta | yajamānāya | sunvate ||7||
he viśvavedasaḥ sarvasya jñātāro viśvadhanā vā he marutaḥ yat yūyaṃ agniśca divaḥ dyu- lokāt uttarāt utkṛṣṭatarāt adhi upari snubhiḥ mānubhiruparīpradeśe vahdhve uhvadhe |
tatra nivasathetyarthaḥ |
yadvā snuśabdena tatsadṛśā aśvā ucyante |
tairvāvahve |
te yūyaṃ maṃdamānāḥ māṃda-
1. ta-ut |
2. ga-gha-ja-śa-vettha |
3. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra- ‘yat’ nāsti |
mānā asmadīyeḥ stotrairvimiśra 'dhunāyaḥ śatrūṇāṃ kampayitāraḥ 'viśādasaḥ asmadvisakānāmtāraḥ santa: 'vāmaṃ vananīyaṃ dhanaṃ 'dhatta datta 'yajamānāya 'sunvate abhipavaṃ kurvate ||
‘ agne marudbhiḥ ’ ityaṣṭamyāgnimārutasya yājyā |
‘ agne marudbhiḥ śubhayadbhirrkabhiḥ ’ ( āśva. śrau. 5. 20 ) iti sūtritatvāt ||
agne marudbhiḥ śubhayadbhirrkabhiḥ somaṃ piba mandasāno gaṇaśribhiḥ |
pāvakairviśvamanvebhirvāyubhirvaiśvānara pradivā ketunā majūḥ ||
8 ||
agne | marut'bhiḥ | śubhayat'bhiḥ | ṛk'bhiḥ | somam | piba | mandasānaḥ | gaṇaśri'bhiḥ | pāvakaiḥ | viśvam'anvebhiḥ | āyu'bhiḥ | vaiśvānara | pradivā | ketunā | majūḥ || 8 ||
ha 'agne 'marudbhiḥ saha 'mandamānaḥ mana 'somaṃ 'piba |
kīdṛśaiḥ marudbhiḥ ? 'śubhayadbhiḥ śobhamānairanyāna 'śubhayadbhirvā 'rkabhiḥ stutyaiḥ 'gaṇaśribhiḥ gaṇabhāvamāśrayadbhiḥ 'pāvakaiḥ śodhakaiḥ 'viśvamanvebhiḥ viśvaṃ dṛṣṭyā prīṇayadbhiḥ 'āyubhiḥ āyuṣmadbhiḥ |
ha 'vaiśvānara viśvanarahitāgne 'pradivā purāṇena 'ketunā jvālāpuñjena 'sajūḥ sahitaḥ ||
25 ||
‘ ke chā naraḥ ’ ityekonaviṃśatyṛcaṃ pañcamaṃ sūktam |
anukramaṇikā— ‘ ke dvekonā gāyatraṃ śyāvāśvo'tra vaidadaśvī tarantapurumīḻhau dābhyaṃ ’ rathavīti marutaśca dāntuṣṭaḥ praśaśaṃsa tudadhvā ca taranta- mahiṣīṃ śaśīyasīṃ ’ pañcamyanuṣṭup navamī satānuṣṭubhī ’ iti |
śyāvāśva ṛṣiḥ |
pañcamyanuṣṭup navamī satobṛhatī śiṣṭā gāyatryaḥ |
ādyāśratasro maruddevatākāḥ |
‘ manasā ’ ityādīnāṃ catasṛṇāṃ śaśīyasīṃ nāma tarantasya rājño bhāryā devatā |
‘ uta meḍarapata ’ ityasyā vaidadaśvaḥ purumīḻho devatā |
‘ yo me dhenām ’ ityasyā vaidadaśvastaranto rājā devatā |
‘ ya īṃ vahante ’ ityādyāḥ paṇamārutaḥ |
‘ etaṃ me ’ ityasya tracasya dābhyo rathavītirnāma rājā devatā |
viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||
atrāścaryaṃ purāvṛttamāhurāgamapāragāḥ |
arcanānāḥ putreyo dābhyena 'rathavītinā ||
āśvijyāya vṛto yajñe vinate hautra āsthitaḥ |
rathavītisutāṃ kanyāṃ dadarśa pitarantike ||
yathāce svakumārāya śyāvāśvāya ca tāṃ sutām |
sa pradānamanā bhāryāmpṛcchatīka prayacchati ||
iti pṛṣṭā punaḥ prāha kathamasmai pradāsyasi |
itaḥ pūrvaṃ sutā dattā nāmīdanupaye kvacit ||
tattathaiveti niścitya pratyā- caṣṭārcanānasam |
śyāvāśvaḥ saṃsthite yajñe tena rājā nirākṛtaḥ ||
tatpratyāśānvito viprastapastepe sudāruṇam |
brahmacaryataḥ śānto bhikṣārthaṃ paryaṭan dvijaḥ ||
taranta mahiṣīṃ sādhvīṃ bibhikṣe'sau śaśī- yasīm |
sā saṃprāpyāntikaṃ patyuḥ provācāgatavānṛṣiḥ ||
ityukto nṛpatibhyāṃ pratyāhaṃ prapūjaya |
sānujñātā gavāṃ yūthaṃ prādādābharaṇāni ca ||
taranto'pi punastasmai prādāddhanamupekṣitam |
datvā ca

'eṣāṃ marutāṃ sthānaṃ 'ko 'veda ko jānāti |

'tata maṇḍalam 'apaśyam |
athavā vāṃ yuvayormadhye sūryasya maṇḍala- mapaśyamiti vyākhyeyaṃ 'maṃtra vā aha: ' (tai. brā. 1. 7. 10. 1) iti śrutermitramyaiva sūryatvādi- vyākhyena |
tatsu vāṃ mitrāvaruṇā mahitvamīmāṃ tasthuṣīrahaṃbhirduduhe |
viśvā: pivantha: svasarasya dhenā anu vāmeka: pavirā vavarva ||
2 ||
tat | su | vām | mitrāvaruṇā | mahitvam | īrmā | tasthuṣī: | ahaṃbhi: | duduhe | viśvā: | pivantha: | svasarasya | dhenā: | anu | vām | eka: | pavi: | ā | vavarva || 2 ||
ha 'mitrāvaruṇā 'vāṃ yuvayo: 'tata 'mahitvaṃ mahattvaṃ 'su suṣṭhu atipraśastamityartha: |
kiṃ tadityucyate |
'īrmā matatagantā sarvasya prerako vāditya: 'ahabhi: ahobhirvarṣatusaṃbandhibhi: 'tasthuṣī: sthāvarabhūtā apa: 'duduhe dugdhe |
kiṃca 'svamamasya svayaṃ sarturādityasya 'viśvā: sarvā: 'dhenā: lokānāṃ prīṇayitrīrdhutī: 'pivantha: vardhayatha: |
'vāṃ yuvayo: 'eka: apratiyogī 'pavi: |
paviriti rathasya nemi: 'pavro rayanemirbhavati ' (niru 5. 5) iti yāskavacanāta |
tathāpyatra lakṣanalakṣaṇayā rathe vartate kevalacakrabhyāvartanāyogāt |
yuvayoreko ratha: 'anu 'ā 'vavarna anukrameṇa paribhramati |

3 ||
adhārayatam | pṛthivīm | uta | dyām | mitrarājānā | varuṇā | maha:'bhi: | vardhayatam | oṣadhī: | pivantam | gā: | ava | vṛṣṭim | sṛjatam | jīradān iti jīradānū || 3 ||
ha 'mitrarājānā |
mitrabhūtā: stotāro rājāna: svāmina īśvarā bhavanti yayo: sakā- śāt 'tau mitrarājānau |
he 'varuṇā |
pratiyogyapekṣayā dvivacanam |
atra pādādivādādyaṃ padaṃ na nighante |

naivaṃ bhavati |
'āmantritaṃ samānādhikaraṇe sāmānyavacanam 'iti varuṇetyasyāmantritasamānā- dhikaraṇatvādasya ca sāmānyavacanatvādavidyamānavattvābhāvena pādādivābhāvāduttarasya nighātatvaṃ yuktam |
he devau 'mahobhi: tejobhi: svasāmarthyai: 'pṛthivīmuta ca 'dyām 'adhārayatam |
he devau yuvām 'oṣadhī: 'vardhayataṃ vṛṣṭipreraṇena |
'gā: 'pivantaṃ gavāśvādīna vardhayatam |

1. ta-yata |
2. kha-ca-ṇa-upāsakatvāt; jha-bha-ra-upāsakāvartī; na-upāsakā yayo: |
ā vāmaśvāsaḥ suyujā vahantu yataraśmaya upa yantarvāk | ghṛtasya nirṇiganu vartate vāmupa sindhavaḥ pradivi kṣaranti || 4 || ā | vām | aśvāsaḥ | su'yujaḥ | vahantu | yataḥ'raśmayaḥ | upa | yantu | arvāk | ghṛtasya | niḥ'nika | anu | vartate | vām | upa | sindhavaḥ | pra'divi | kṣaranti || 4 || he mitrāvaruṇau vvāṃ yuvām vaśvāsaḥ aśvāḥ vsuyujaḥ suṣṭhu rathe yuktāḥ santaḥ vā vvahantu | vyataraśmayaḥ sārathiniyatapragrahahaste varvāk vupa vyantu | vghṛtasya udakasya vnirṇiga rūpaṃ vvām vanu vvartate anugacchati | kiṃca vpradivi purāṇāmetat | purāṇāḥ vsindhavaḥ vupa vkṣaranti upagacchanti yuvayoranugrahāt ||
anu śrutāmatiṃ vardhurvau bahiriva yajuṣā rakṣamāṇā | narmasvantā ghṛtadakṣādhi garte mitrāsāthe varuṇoḻosvantaḥ || 5 || anu | śrutām | amatim | vardhat | urvīm | barhiḥ'iva | yajuṣā | rakṣamāṇā | narmasvantā | ghṛta'dakṣā | adhi | garte | mitra | āsāthe | iti | varuṇa | iḻāsu | antariti ||5|| vśrutāṃ viśvatām vamatim | rūpanāmaitat | śarīradīptimityarthaḥ | tām vanu vvardhata anuvardha- yantau | vbarhiḥ yajñaḥ | saḥ viva sa yathā vyajuṣā mantreṇa rakṣyate tadvat vurvī vrakṣamāṇā pālayantau vnamasvantā annavantau he vghṛtadakṣā āttabalau he vmitra he vvaruṇa he mitrāvaruṇau yuvāmuktalakṣaṇau mantau viḻāsu yāgabhūmiṣu vantaḥ madhye vgarte vadhi rathe vāsāthe upaviśathaḥ | ' ratho'pi garte ucyate ' ( niru. 3. 5 ) iti yāskaḥ | ' ā rohatho varuṇa mitra gartam ' ( ṛ. saṃ. 5. 62. 8 ) iti || || 30 ||
arkavihastā sukṛtai paraspā yaṃ trāsāthe varuṇoḻosvantaḥ | rājānā kṣatramahaṇīyamānā sahasrasthūṇaṃ bibhrathaḥ saha dvau || 6 || arkavi'hastā | su'kṛtai | puraḥ'pā | yam | trāsāthe | iti | varuṇa | iḻāsu | antariti | rājānā | kṣatram | ahaṇīyamānā | sahasra'sthūṇam | bibhrathaḥ | saha | dvau || 6 || varkavihastā akupaṇahastau | dānaśūrāvityarthaḥ | kasmai | vsukṛte śobhanastutikartre vparaspā parastāt pātārau rakṣitārau he vvaruṇa mitrāvaruṇau yuvāṃ vyaṃ yajamānam viḻāsu yāgabhūmiṣu vantaḥ madhye vtrāsāthe rakṣathaḥ tasmai sukṛte varkavihastā vparaspā ca bhavata iti saṃbandhaḥ | kiṃca yuvāṃ vrājānā rājamānau vahaṇīyamānā akudhyantau vdvau parasparaṃ vsaha sāhāyyena vkṣatraṃ dhanaṃ vsahasrasthūṇam ane-

athavā |

yat | baṃhiṣṭham | na | ati'vidhe | sudānu iti su'dānu | achidram | śarma | bhuvanasya | gopā | tena | naḥ | mitrāvaruṇau | aviṣṭam | sipāsantaḥ | jigīvāṃsaḥ | syāma || 9 ||

ṛtasya gopāvadhi tiṣṭhatho rathaṃ satyadharmāṇā parame vyomani | yamatra mitrāvaruṇāvatho yuvaṃ tasmai vṛṣṭirmadhumatpinyate divaḥ || 1 || ṛtasya | gopā | adhi | tiṣṭhathaḥ | ratham | satyadharmāṇā | parame | vi'omana | yam | atra | mitrāvaruṇā | avathaḥ | yuvam | tasmai | vṛṣṭiḥ | madhu'mat | pinyate | divaḥ ||1||
he ‘ṛtasya udakasya’ gopā rakṣitārau satyadharmāṇā satyadharmāṇau yuvāṃ ratham adhi tiṣṭhathaḥ |
āruhatho'smadyajñamāgamanārtham |
kutra |
parame vyomani niratiśaye ākāśe |
he mitrāvaruṇau yuvāṃ yuvām atra asmin yajñe yaṃ yajamānam avathaḥ rakṣathaḥ tasmai yajamānāya vṛṣṭiḥ parjanyaḥ madhumat udakaṃ divaḥ dyulokān pinyate siñcati vardhayati ||
samrājāvasya bhuvanasya rājatho mitrāvaruṇā vidathe svardṛśā | vṛṣṭiṃ vāṃ rādhāṃ amṛtatvamīmahe dyāvāpṛthivī vi caranti tanvaḥ || 2 || sam'rājau | asya | bhuvanasya | rājathaḥ | mitrāvaruṇā | vidathe | svaḥ'dṛśā | vṛṣṭim | vām | rādhaḥ | amṛtatvam | īmahe | dyāvāpṛthivī iti | vi | caranti | tanvaḥ ||2||
he mitrāvaruṇā mitrāvaruṇau samrājau samyak rājamānau yuvām asya bhuvanasya rājathaḥ īśāthe |
svardṛśā svargasya draṣṭārau yuvāṃ vidathe asamayajñe samrājāviti sambandhaḥ |
vāṃ yuvāṃ vṛṣṭiṃ rādhaḥ dhanaṃ vṛṣṭyākhyaṃ dhanam amṛtatvaṃ svargaṃ ca īmahe prārthayāmahe |
yuvayoḥ tanvaḥ visṛtā raśmayaḥ dyāvāpṛthivī vi caranti vividhaṃ prāpnuvanti |
athavā vṛṣṭiṃ dhanamamṛtatvaṃ cemahe ||
1. kha-jha-bha-udakasya vā; ta-yajñasyodakasya vā; na-udakasya yajñasya vā |
2. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na- bha-ra-satyakarmāṇau |
3. na-jha-na-bha-ra-‘mitrāvaruṇā’ nāsti |
4. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-prārthayāmi |
5. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-bha-‘vividhaṃ’ nāsti |

ṛgveda: [ a. 4. a. 4. va. 1 ]
samrājā ugrā vṛṣabhā divaspatī pṛthivyā mitrāvaruṇā vircarṣaṇī |
citrebhirbharai rupa tiṣṭhatho ravaṃ dyāṃ varṣayatho asurasya māyayā ||
3 ||
samrājau | ugrā | vṛṣabhā | divaḥ | patī iti | pṛthivyāḥ | mitrāvaruṇā | vircarṣaṇī iti vi'carṣaṇī | citrebhiḥ | abhraiḥ | upa | tiṣṭhathaḥ | ravam | dyām | varṣayathaḥ | asurasya | māyayā || 3 ||
'samrājau' samyak rājamānau 'ugrā' udbhaṭabalau 'vṛṣabhā' varṣitārau 'divaḥ' dyulokasya 'patī' svāminau 'pṛthivyāḥ' ca patī 'vircarṣaṇī' sarvasya draṣṭārau mitrāvaruṇau |
evaṃ mahānubhāvau 'citrebhiḥ' cāyanīyaiḥ 'abhraiḥ' meghaiḥ saha 'ravaṃ' stotram 'upa' 'tiṣṭhathaḥ |
paśdhāna 'yāṃ' ca 'varṣayathaḥ 'asurasya udakanirasituḥ parjanyasya 'māyayā prajñayā sāmarthyena ||
māyā vāṃ mitrāvaruṇā divi śritā sūryo jyotiścarati citramāyudham |
tamabhreṇa vṛṣṭyā gūhatho divi parjanya drapsā madhumanta īrate ||
4 ||
māyā | vām | mitrāvaruṇā | divi | śritā | sūryaḥ | jyotiḥ | carati | citram | āyudham | tam | abhreṇa | vṛṣṭyā | gūhathaḥ | divi | parjanya | drapsāḥ | madhu'mantaḥ | īrate || 4 ||
he 'mitrāvaruṇā 'vāṃ yuvayoḥ 'māyā 'divi 'śritā āśritā |
kaiṣā yuvayormāyāyocyate |
'sūryaḥ sarvasya prerakaḥ 'jyotiḥ dīyamānaḥ 'citraṃ cāyanīyam 'āyudham utlakṣaṇāyudharūpaḥ 'carati paribhramannantarikṣe |
śatrumārakatvādāyudhamucyate |
'taṃ sūryam 'abhreṇa meghena 'vṛṣṭyā 'divi 'gūhathaḥ |
he 'parjanya deva' mitrāvaruṇābhyāṃ vṛṣṭyarthaṃ preśitena tvayā 'madhumantaḥ 'drapsāḥ 'īrate īryante |
svatsṛṣṭā vā gacchanti ||
rathe yuñjate marutaḥ śubhe sukhaṃ śūro na mitrāvaruṇā gaviṣṭiṣu |
rajāṃsi citrā vi caranti tanvo divaḥ samrājā paryasā na ukṣatam ||
5||
ratham | yuñjate | marutaḥ | śubhe | su'kham | śūraḥ | na | mitrāvaruṇā | go'iṣṭiṣu | rajāṃsi | citrā | vi | caranti | tanvaḥ | divaḥ | samrājā | paryasā | naḥ | ukṣatam ||5||
he mitrāvaruṇau yuvayoranugrahāt 'marutaḥ 'mahataḥ 'śubhe udakārthaṃ 'sukhaṃ śobhanākṣadvāraṃ 'rathaṃ 'yuñjate aśvairyojayanti |
'śūro 'na śūra iva |
sa yathā yuddhārthaṃ rathaṃ yunakti tadvat |
'gaviṣṭiṣu gavāmudakānām
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-śa-samrājā |
2. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-deva tayā |
eṣaṇeṇu nimitteṣu ᵥcitrā cāyanīyāni ᵥrajāṃsi lokān ᵥtanvyaḥ tatāste marutaḥ ᵥvi ᵥcaranti vividhaṃ caranti |
tasmāt taiḥ mahitau he ᵥsamrājā yuvāṃ ᵥdivaḥ dyulokāna ᵥpayasā udakena ᵥnaḥ asmān ᵥukṣataṃ siñcatam ||
vācaṃ su mitrāvaruṇāvirāvartī parjanyaścitrāṃ vadati tviṣīmatīm |
annā vaṃsata marutaḥ su māyā yāṃ varṣayatamaruṇāmarepasam ||
6 ||
vācam | su | mitrāvaruṇau | irā'vatīm | parjanyaḥ | citrām | vadati | tvirbi'matīm | annā | vasata | marutaḥ | su | māyayā | yām | varṣayatam | aruṇām | arepasam || 6 ||
he ᵥmitrāvaruṇau yuvayoranugrahāt ᵥparjanyaḥ meghaḥ ᵥsu suṣṭhu ᵥirāvatīm annavatīm annasādhikāṃ ᵥcitrāṃ cāyanīyāṃ ᵥtviṣīmatīṃ dīptimatīṃ ᵥvācaṃ garjanaśabdaṃ ᵥvadati |
śabdayati vṛṣṭyartham |
ᵥmarutaḥ ca ᵥannā abhrāṇi meghāna ᵥsu suṣṭhu ᵥvasata ācchādayanti ᵥmāyayā svaprajñayā |
yuvāṃ ca parjanyena marudbhiḥ saha ᵥaruṇām aruṇavarṇām ᵥarepasam apāpāṃ vṛṣṭyavighātinīṃ ᵥyāṃ ᵥvarṣayatam ||
dharmeṇā mitrāvaruṇā vipaścitā vratā rakṣethe asurasya māyayā |
ṛtena viśvaṃ bhuvanaṃ vi rājathaḥ sūryamā dhattho divi citryaṃ ratham ||
7 ||
dharmeṇā | mitrāvaruṇau | vipaḥ'citā | vratā | rakṣethe iti | asurasya | māyayā | ṛtena | viśvam | bhuvanam | vi | rājathaḥ | sūryam | ā | dhatthaḥ | divi | citryam | ratham || 7 ||
he ᵥmitrāvaruṇā ᵥvipaścitā prājñau yuvāṃ ᵥdharmeṇā jagaddhārakeṇa vṛṣṭyādilakṣaṇena karmaṇā ᵥvratā yajñādikarmāṇi ᵥrakṣethe pālayathaḥ |
ᵥasurasya meghānāṃ nirasituḥ parjanyasya ᵥmāyayā prajñayā ca ᵥṛtena udakena yajñena vā nimittena ᵥviśvaṃ ᵥbhuvanaṃ sarvaṃ bhūtajātaṃ ᵥvi ᵥrājathaḥ vidāpayathaḥ 2 ityarthaḥ |
ᵥsūryamā sūrya ca ᵥcitryaṃ pūjyaṃ ᵥrathaṃ rahaṇasvabhāvaṃ ᵥdivi dyuloke ᵥdhatthaḥ dhārayatho jagadupakārārtham ||

‘ ā cikitāna ’ iti ṣaḍ‌caṃ daśamaṃ sūktaṃ rātahavyasyārṣam ’ ānuṣṭubhaṃ maitrāvaruṇam |

ā | yat | yonim | hiraṇyayam | varuṇa | mitra | sadyaḥ | dhartārāṃ | carṣaṇīnām | yantam | sumram | riṃśādasā || 2 ||
yan yasmāt hiraṇyayaṃ hitaramaṇāya yāni yajñabhāmi he varuṇa he mitra ā sadyaḥ āgacchathaḥ tasmān carṣaṇīnāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ dhatārāṃ dhārakau ca he riṃśādasā hiṃsakānāṃ kṣatārau yuvām asmabhyaṃ sujñaṃ sumraṃ yantaṃ kurutamityarthaḥ ||

4. kha-na-pā-na-ma-śa- bahuvacanaṃ kartavyam |
5. kha-ca-a-ṇa-na-ma-ra- 'padāniva' nāsti |
te hi khalu satyāḥ satyabhūtāḥ satyaphalā vā ṛtaspṛśaḥ udakasparśasya kartāraḥ ṛtāvānaḥ yajñavantaḥ janeṣu sarveṣu yajamāneṣu munīthām sunayanāḥ sudānavaḥ sudānāḥ|
hīti prasiddhavāco sarvatra saṃbandhanīyaḥ|
evaṃ mahānubhāvā varuṇādayaḥ aṃhośrita|
cidapyarthe|
pāpino'pi bhavantāḥ urucakrayaḥ prabhūtadhanādikartāraḥ ||
ko nu vāṃ mitrāstaṃto varṷṇo vā ta॒nūnā̭m |
tatsa॒ vāmḙṣate ma॒tiri̭tri॒bhya ḙpate ma॒tiḥ ||
5 ||
kaḥ | nu | vām | mitra | astaṃtaḥ | varuṇaḥ | vā | tanūnām | tat | su | vām | ā | īṣate | matiḥ | atri'bhyaḥ | ā | īṣate | matiḥ || 5 ||
haṃ mitra vāṃ yuvayormadhye yaṃ ca varuṇo vā ko nu astaṃtaḥ|
saṃvarattyamānaḥ ko'sti |
ubhāvapi stutyāviśyarthaḥ |
tanūnām iti nṛtīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī |
tanubhiḥ stutibhiḥ³ astaṃta iti saṃbandhaḥ ||
atra mitraśabdena mitrasyaikasyaiva saṃbodhanam varuṇāsvantaḥ ( ṛ. saṃ. 5. 62. 6 ) ityevevātra dvivacanasūcakasyākārasya anāmnātaḥ|
lokeṣu bahuṣu saṃnihiteṣu ekameva saṃbodhya yuvayoridaṃ yuṣmākamidarmiti prayuñjate ||
tata tasmāna yasmātsarve stuvanti tasmāt |
tannāmityetata vyavahitamapyuttaratra vā yojyam |
tannām alpamartānāmasmākaṃ matiḥ stutiḥ vāṃ yuvāṃ suṣṭhu eṣate abhigacchati |
atribhyaḥ atrigotrabhyo'smabhyam asmadīyamartiḥ eṣate |
ādarārthaṃ punarvacanam ||
5 ||
pra vo mitrāya ' śāṃta pañcarca dvādaśaṃ sūktamātreyam yajatasyāpa gāyatraṃ maitrāvaruṇam |
tathā cānukramyate— ' pra vo gāyatram ' iti |
agniṣṭome prātaḥsavane maitrāvaruṇasya etatsaktam |
sūtritaṃ ca— ' pra vo mitrāya pra mitrayorvaruṇayoḥ ' ( āśva. śrau. 5. 10 ) iti |
abhiplavapahe pṛṣṭhya- pahe ca stomanimittāvāpārthamidamārdāni dvitīyārvajanāni catvāri muktāni |
tathaiva sūtritaṃ— ' pra vo mitrāyati nṛṇāṃ dvitīyamudareta ' ( āśva. śrau. 9. 5 ) iti ||
pra vo mi॒trāya̭ gā॒yatu॒ varṷṇāya vi॒pā gi॒rā |
ma॒hikṣa̭trāvṛtaṃ bṛ॒hat ||
1 ||
pra | vaḥ | mitrāya | gāyatu | varuṇāya | vipā | girā | mahi'kṣatrau | ṛtam | bṛhata || 1 ||
haṃ madīyā ṛtvijaḥ vaḥ |
yūyamiyarthaḥ |
mitrāya varuṇāya ca vipā vyāmayā girā stutyā pra gāyatu stutiṃ kuruta |
mahuyā stuvatetyetata pāka pacantītivata |
haṃ mahikṣatrau prabhūtabalau yuvām ṛtaṃ yajñaṃ bṛhata mahata atipraśastam |
mahuyarthamāgacchatamiti śeṣaḥ |
athavā mahata prabhūtam ṛtaṃ stotraṃ śṛṇutabhiti śeṣaḥ ||
1. jha-na-bhara-te khalu |
2 kha-ca-ṇa-udakaspṛśaḥ tasya ; ga-pa-ja-śa-udakasya ; jha-ta-bhara- udakaspṛśatvasya |
3. kha-ca-ṇa-alpastutibhiḥ ; a-ra--astutibhiḥ |
4 kha-ca-ṇa-bha-abhāvāta ; jha-anāstanomāta ; ta-ra-śa-āmnānāta |
5 ma-na-a-na-na-ma-ra- ' maṣṭa ' nāsti |
samrājā yā ghṛtayaunī mitraśobhā varuṇaśca | devā deveṣu praśastā || 2 || sama'rājā | yā | ghṛtayaunī iti ghṛta'yaunī | mitraḥ | ca | ubhā | varuṇaḥ | ca | devā | deveṣu | pra'śastā || 2 ||
' yā yau ' mitraśca ' varuṇaśca |
parasparapekṣayā caśabdaḥ |
' ubhā ubhau ' samrājā sarvasya svāminau ' ghṛtayaunī udakasyotpādakau ' devā ' dyotamānau ' deveṣu madhye ' praśastā prakarṣeṇa stutyau |
tau gāyateti pūrvatrānvayaḥ ||
tā naḥ śakta pārthivasya maho rāyo divyasya | mahi vāṃ kṣatraṃ deveṣu || 3 || tā | naḥ | śaktam | pārthivasya | mahaḥ | rāyaḥ | divyasya | mahi | vām | kṣatram | deveṣu ||3||
' tā tau ' devau ' naḥ asmadarthaṃ ' pārthivasya pṛthivīsaṃbaddhasya ' divyasya divi bhavasya ca ' mahaḥ mahataḥ ' rāyaḥ dhanasya ' śaktaṃ samarthau bhavatama |
dātumiti śeṣaḥ |
he ' devau ' vāṃ yuvayoḥ ' mahi mahataḥ pūjyaṃ ' kṣatraṃ balaṃ ' deveṣu prasiddhaṃ stuma iti śeṣaḥ ||
ṛtamṛtena sarpantoṣpirdakṣamāśāte | aduhau devau vardhate || 4 || ṛtam | ṛtena | sarpantā | iṣiram | dakṣam | āśāte iti | aduhau | devau | vardhate iti ||4||
' ṛtena udakena nimittena ' ṛtaṃ yajñaṃ ' sarpantā spṛśantau ' iṣiram eṣaṇavantaṃ ' dakṣaṃ pravṛddhaṃ yajamānaṃ havirvā ' āśāte vyāpnutaḥ |
' aduhau adroghārau ' devau mitrāvaruṇau yuvāṃ ' vardhate pravṛddhau bhavataḥ ||
vṛṣṭidyāvā rityāṃpeṣapatī dānumatyaḥ | bṛhantaṃ gartemāśāte || 5 || vṛṣṭi'dyāvā | riti'āpa | iṣaḥ | patī iti | dānu'matyaḥ | bṛhantam | gartem | āśāte iti || 5 ||
' vṛṣṭidyāvā | ' vṛṣṭyarthā dyauḥ stutiyayorvā ' vṛṣṭidyāvā | athavā ' vṛṣṭirvipikā dyaurantarikṣaṃ yābhyāṃ tāṃ tādṛśau | ' rityāpā || ' rītiḥ prāptiḥ | maiva ' āpā āsiḥ ' abhimata- prāptiryayostau tādṛśau | ' iṣaḥ annasya ' patī svāminau | ' vṛṣṭipradatvāta svāmitvam | ' dānumatyaḥ dānavatyāḥ | ' dātumacitāyāḥ ' ityarthaḥ | etadviśeṣaṇam | evaṃ mahānubhāvau ' bṛhantaṃ mahāntaṃ ' gataṃ ratham ' āśāte vyāpnutaḥ | adhitiṣṭhato yāgārtham || || 6 ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-devī |
2. kha-ca-ṇa-stutau; ta-stutyā |
3. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra- barhirvā |
4. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra- saiva ātiḥ ; ga-gha-ja-saiva apā |
5. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra- dānamucitāyāḥ ; ga-gha-ja-śa- dātṛpūcitāyāḥ |
‘ trī rocanā ’ iti caturṛcaṃ trayodaśaṃ sūktam | ūrucakrirnāmatreya ṛṣiḥ | triṣṭup chandaḥ | mitrāvaruṇau devatā | anukramaṇe ca—‘ trī rocanā catuṣkamurucakriḥ ’ iti | viniyogo laiṅgikaḥ ||
trī ro̭canā varṷṇa trīru॒ta dyūn trīṇi̭ mi॒tra dhā॒raya̭tho ra॒jāmi̭ |
vāvṛdhā॒nāvama̭tiṃ kṣa॒triya̭syānu॒ vrata̭ṃ ra॒kṣamā̭ṇāvajuryam ||
1 ||
trī | rocanā | varuṇa | trīn | una | yūn | trīṇi | mitra | dhārayathaḥ | rajāṃsi | vāvṛdhānau | amatim | kṣatriyasya | anu | vratam | rakṣamāṇau | ajūryam || 1 ||
he varuṇa he mitra yuvāṃ trī trīṇi rocanā rocanāni dyulokān dhārayatha iti saṃbandhaḥ |
una api ca trīn yūn dyotamānāntarikṣalokān dhārayathaḥ |
tathā trīṇi rajāṃsi bhūlokān |
kīdṛśau yuvām |
vāvṛdhānau vardhamānau kṣatriyasya kṣatraṃ balam |
tadvā indrasya |
yadvā |
kṣatriya- jātīyasya yajamānasya amatim |
rūpāmaitana |
rūpaṃ vrataṃ karma ca anaryama ajīrṇamavirataṃ vā rakṣamāṇau ||
i॒rā॒vatī̭varuṇa dhe॒navo̭ vāṃ ma॒dhumdā̭ si॒ndhavo̭ mi॒tra dṷhe |
trya̭sta॒sthurvṛ̭ṣa॒bhāstvi॒ṣūṇā̭ṃ dhi॒ṣaṇā̭nāṃ re॒todhā॒ vi ghu॒manta̭ḥ ||
2 ||
irā'vatī | varuṇa | dhenavaḥ | vām | madhu'mat | vām | sindhavaḥ | mitra | duhe | trayaḥ | tasthuḥ | vṛṣabhāsaḥ | tisṛṇām | dhiṣaṇānām | retaḥ'dhāḥ | vi | ghumantaḥ || 2 ||
he varuṇa he mitra vāṃ yuvayorjaya dhenavaḥ gāvaḥ irāvatīḥ |
irā kṣīralakṣaṇā |
taddyo bhavanti |
tathā sindhavaḥ syandanaśīlā āpo nadyā vā madhumata madhuramudakaṃ duhe |
tathā trayaḥ trimavyākāḥ vṛṣabhāsaḥ varṣitāraḥ retodhāḥ udakasya dhārakāḥ ghumantaḥ dīptimanto'gni- vāyvādityāḥ tisṛṇāṃ trimavyākānāṃ dhiṣaṇānāṃ sthānānāṃ pṛthivyantarikṣalokānāṃ svāminaḥ santaḥ vi vividhaṃ pratyekaṃ tasthuḥ tiṣṭhanti |
yuvayoranugrahāna trayo'pi devāsriṣu sthāneṣu vartante ityarthaḥ ||
prā॒tarde॒vīṃ ma̭di॒taṃ jo̭havīmi ma॒dhya̭ṃdi॒na udi̭tā॒ sūrya̭sya |
rā॒ye mi॒trāvarṷṇā ma॒rvatā̭te॒ tokā̭ya॒ tana̭yāya śa॒ṃ yoḥ ||
3 ||
prātaḥ | devīm | aditima | johavīmi | madhyaṃdine | uta | itā | sūryasya | rāye | mitrāvaruṇā | sarva'tātā | tokāya | tanayāya | śam | yoḥ || 3 ||
1. sva-ca-a-ga-ta-na-ma-ra-śa- ‘ chandaḥ ’ nāsti |
2. sva-ca-a-ga-bha-ra-śa- rocamānāni ; na- rocanāni rocamānāni |
3. sva-na-ṇa-bha- ‘ vāvṛdhānau vardhamānī ’ nāsti |
4. ma-ca-ṇa- ‘ sthānānāṃ... lokānāṃ ’ nāsti ; a-na-na-ma-ra-śa- sthānānāma |
ahamupiḥ ᵥprātaḥ prātaḥkāle ᵥaditim akhaṇḍanīyāṃ devānāṃ jananīṃ ᵥdevīṃ dyotamānāṃ ᵥjohavīmi āhvayāmi |
tathā ᵥmadhyadine ᵥsūryasya ᵥuditā uditau tatsamṛddhikāle mādhyadine savane johavīmi |
he ᵥmitrāvaruṇā yuvāṃ ᵥrāye dhanaprāptaye sarvatāto yajñe ᵥīḻe staumi |
punaḥ kimartham |
ᵥtokāya putrāya ᵥtanayāya tatputrāya ca ᵥśam ariṣṭaśamanāya ᵥyoḥ sugasya miśraṇāyā ceti ||
yā dhṛtārga rajaso rocanasyotādityā divyā pārthivasya |
na vāṃ devā amṛtā ā minanti vratāni mitrāvaruṇā dhruvāṇi ||
4 ||
yā | dhṛtārga | rajasaḥ | rocanasya | uta | ādityā | divyā | pārthivasya | na | vām | devāḥ | amṛtāḥ | ā | minanti | vratāni | mitrāvaruṇā | dhruvāṇi || 4 ||
ᵥyā yau ᵥrocanasya rocamānasya ᵥrajasaḥ lokasya |
svargākhyasyetyarthaḥ |
ᵥuta api ca ᵥpārthi- vasya rajasaḥ yā yau dhṛtārgau ᵥādityā aditeḥ putrau ᵥdivyā divi bhavantau yuvāmīḻe iti pūrvatra sambandhaḥ |
he ᵥmitrāvaruṇā ᵥvāṃ yuvayoḥ ᵥdhruvāṇi ᵥvratāni karmāṇi ᵥdevāḥ anye indrādyaḥ ᵥamṛtāḥ amaraṇadharmāṇo'pi ᵥna ᵥā ᵥminanti na hiṃsanti |
yato ᵥdhruvāṇyata ityabhiprāyaḥ ||
7 ||
‘purūraṇā’ iti caturṛcaṃ caturdaśa sūktamurukairapaṃ gāyatraṃ mitrāvaruṇam |
‘purūraṇā gāyatraṃ tu’ ityanukramaṇikā |
pṛṣṭhābhiplavaṣaḍahayoḥ prātaḥsavane āvāpārthatvena idamapi sūktaṃ pūrvasūktena saha |
‘pra vo mitrāyeti caturṇāṃ dvitīyamuddharena’ iti uktam |
cāturviṃśake'hani prātaḥsavane mitrāvaruṇaśastre ādyamṛcaḥ ṣaḍahastotrasaṃjñakaḥ |
mūtritaṃ ca— ‘purūraṇā cidrayasi prati vāṃ mūra udite’ ( āśva. śrau. 7. 2 ) iti ||
purūraṇā cidrayastyavau nūnaṃ vāṃ varuṇa | mitra vaṃsi vāṃ sumatim || 1 || purūraṇā | cit | rayiḥ | asti | avaḥ | nūnam | vām | varuṇa | mitra | vaṃsi | vām | sumatim || 1 ||
he mitrāvaruṇau ᵥvāṃ yuvayoḥ ᵥpurūraṇā ᵥcit |
atra moḥ ‘supāṃ suluk’ ityākāraḥ ||
purorapi bahu uru bahutaram |
athavā puru ca taduru ca purūru |
atyantamuttaramityarthaḥ |
nāhaka ᵥavaḥ rakṣaṇaṃ ᵥnūnaṃ niścayena ᵥasti |
ᵥhi prasiddhau |
ᵥciditi pūraṇaḥ |
he ᵥvaruṇa he ᵥmitra ᵥvāṃ yuvayoḥ ᵥsumatim anugrahabuddhiṃ ᵥvaṃsi saṃbhajeya |
yasmādavo'sti tasmādvaṃsi iti bhāvaḥ ||
1. ta-āhvayāmīti |
2. jha-ta-na-ra-dhṛtārga tā~ |
tā vo̭ su॒mya॒gdu॒ha॒ṇepa̭ma॒śyāma̭ dhā॒yamḙ | va॒yaṃ tḙ ru॒drā syā̭ma || 2 || tā | vām | samyak | adṛhāṇā | iṣam | aśyāma | dhāyase | vayam | te | rudrā | syāma ||2||
he adṛhāṇā he adṛgdhārau tā tau prasiddhau vāṃ yuvāṃ samyak |
stuma iti śeṣaḥ |
stotāro vayam iṣam annaṃ dhāyase pānāya bhojanāya aśyāma prāpnuyāma |
he rudrā duḥkhān drāvayitārau rudriḥ' dravaṇīyā~ vā te stotāraḥ vayaṃ syāma bhavema |
samṛddhā iti śeṣaḥ |
yuvābhyāṃ svabhūtā vā syāma ||
pā॒taṃ naṷ ru॒drā pā॒yubhi̭ru॒ta trā॒yethā̭ṃ su॒trātrā̭ | tu॒ryā̭ma॒ dasya̭ntū॒rbhi̭ḥ || 3 || pātam | naḥ | rudrā | pāyubhiḥ | uta | trāyethām | sutrātrā | turyāma | dasyan | tūrbhiḥ ||3||
he rudrā ukta rūpau devau yuvāṃ naḥ asmān pāyubhiḥ rakṣaṇaiḥ pātaṃ rakṣatam |
uta api ca sutrātrā śobhanena trāṇena trāyethāṃ pālayathām |
iṣṭaprāptyaniṣṭaparihārabhedena bhedaḥ |
stotrādi- vaikalyāt śatrorvā trāyethām abhimataprāpaṇena rakṣatamityarthaḥ |
vayaṃ ca tūrbhiḥ putrādibhiḥ sahitāḥ svairvā dasyan śatrūn turyāma hiṃsyāma tarema vā ||
mā ka॒syā̭da॒takra̭tu॒ yakṣa̭ṃ bhu॒ṃjemā̭ ta॒nūbhi̭: | mā śe॒ṣā mā ta॒na̭sā || 4 || mā | kasya | adbhutakratu | yakṣam | bhuṃjema | tanūbhiḥ | mā | śeṣasā | mā | tanasā ||4||
he adbhutakratu āścaryakarmāṇau vayaṃ kasya anyasya kasyacana yakṣaṃ pūjitaṃ dhanaṃ mā bhuṃjema mā bhuṃjīmahi tanūbhiḥ asmadīyaiḥ avayavaiḥ |
yuvayoranugrahāta samṛddhā vayam anyācitadhanena śarīrapoṣaṇaṃ na kurma ityarthaḥ |
tathā śeṣasā apatyena sahitāḥ mā bhuṃjema |
tanamā pautrādinā sahitāḥ mā bhuṃjema |
asmatkule na ke'pi bhuṃjīmahītyarthaḥ ||
6 ||
‘ā no gantam’ iti tṛcātmakaṃ pañcadaśaṃ sūktaṃ vāyudṛṣṭasyātreyasyārṣaṃ gāyatraṃ maitrāvaruṇam |
‘ā naśca’ bāhuvṛktaḥ’ ityanukramaṇikā |
uktaḥ sūktaviniyogaḥ |
agniṣṭome prātaḥ- savane maitrāvaruṇaśastre ayamanurūpastṛcaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ā no mitrāvaruṇā no gantaṃ riśādasā’ (āśva. śrau. 5. 10) iti ||
ā naṷ ga॒ntaṃ ri̭śāda॒sā varṷṇa mi॒tra ba॒rhaṇā̭ | u॒pema̭ṃ cā॒ruma̭dhva॒ram || 1 || ā | naḥ | gantam | riśādasā | varuṇa | mitra | barhaṇā | upa | imam | cārum | adhvaram ||1||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra- rudriḥ |
2. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ra-trāṇena ; ga-gha-ja-śa- 'trāṇena' nāsti |
3. ga-gha-ja- 'pālayethāṃ' nāsti ; ta-pālayetām ; ra-pālayatām ; śa-pālanam |
4. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta- na-bha-ra-śa--madīyeḥ |
he ᵥvaruṇa he ᵥmitra he ᵥriśādasā śatrūṇāṃ prerakau yuvāṃ ᵥnaḥ asmākam ᵥhyaṃ ᵥcāruṃ caraṇīyam ᵥadhvaram ahiṃsakaṃ yajñam ᵥupa ᵥā ᵥgantam upāgacchatam |
kīdṛśau |
ᵥbarhaṇā barhaṇau nibarhaṇau hantārau śatrūṇām ||
viśvasya hi pracetasā varuṇa mitra rājathaḥ |
īśānā pipyataṃ dhiyaḥ ||
2 ||
viśvasya | hi | pra'cetasā | varuṇa | mitra | rājathaḥ | īśānā | pipyatam | dhiyaḥ || 2 ||
he ᵥmitra he ᵥvaruṇa ᵥpracetamā prakṛṣṭajñānau yuvāṃ ᵥviśvasya ᵥrājathaḥ sarvasya svāminau bhavataḥ |
ᵥhi iti prasiddhau |
he ᵥīśānā asmākamīśvarau yuvāṃ ᵥdhiyaḥ asmadīyāni karmāṇi ᵥpipyataṃ pyāyataṃ phalaiḥ ||
upa naḥ sutamā gataṃ
vratena stho dhruvakṣemā dharmmaṇā yā tayajjanā |
ni barhiṣi sadataṃ somapītaye ||
2 ||
vratena | sthaḥ | dhruvakṣemā | dharmmaṇā | yā tayajjanā | ni | barhiṣi | sadatam | somapītaye || 2 ||
he mitrāvaruṇau yuvāṃ dharmmaṇā jagaddhārakeṇa vratena karmmaṇā yāṃgena dhruvakṣemā sthaḥ |
avi- calitasthānau bhavataḥ |
yā tayajjanā |
yā tayantaḥ karmmasu pravarttayanto janā ṛtvijo yayostau yā tayajanī |
athavā yā tayantaḥ śatrūn hiṃmanto janā bhṛtyā yayostau |
śiṣṭaṃ gatam ||
mitraśca no varuṇaśca juṣetāṃ yajñamiṣṭaye |
ni barhiṣi sadatāṃ somapītaye ||
3 ||

na | saṃskṛtam | pra | mimītaḥ | gamiṣṭhā | anti | nūnam | aśvinau | upa'stutā | iha | divā | abhipitve | avasā | ā'gamiṣṭhā | prati | avartim | dāśuṣe | śam'bhaviṣṭhā ||2||
aśvinau saṃskṛtaṃ gharmaṃ na pra mimītaḥ na hiṃsām |
kintu anti antike gharmamamīpe nūnam idānīm iha yajñe gamiṣṭhā gantṛtamau aśvinā aśvinau upastutā upastutau bhavataḥ |
divābhipitve divasasyābhipatane prātaḥkāle avasā rakṣaṇena saha āgamiṣṭhā āgantṛtamau |
avati prati dvandvabhūtau |
vartijīvanam |
tadabhāvo'vartiḥ |
tadrahitamannaṃ yathā bhavati tathāgantṛtamau |
āgatya ca dāśuṣe havirdanavate yajamānāya śaṃbhaviṣṭhā sukhasya bhāvayitārau bhavatām ||
utā yātaṃ saṃgave prātarahau mṛṣyaṃdinaṃ uditā sūryasya |
divā naktamavasā śaṃtamena nedānīṃ pītiraśvinā tatāna ||
3 ||
uta | ā | yātam | sam'gave | prātaḥ | ahaḥ | madhyaṃdine | ut'ītā | sūryasya | divā | naktam | avasā | śam'tamena | na | idānīm | pītiḥ | aśvinau | ā | tatāna ||3||
aho dvedhā tredhā pañcadhā pañcadaśadhā samānāḥ vibhāgāḥ santi |
iha pañcadhā vibhāga āstaḥ |
uta api ca ā yātam āgacchatam |
kadā saṃgave saṃgavakāle |
saṃgacchante gāvo dohabhūmiṃ yasmin kāle sa saṃgavaḥ |
rātryaparakāle hi gāvāṃ vane himatṛṇāni bhakṣayitvā punardodhāya saṃgave pratinivartante |
tathā prātaḥ prātaḥkāle'pi |
tathā madhyaṃdine ahaḥ madhyakāle sūryasya uditā uditau abhyudaye atyantapravṛddhasamaye aparāhṇe ityarthaḥ |
etat sāyāhrasyāpyupalakṣaṇam |
na kevalam
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-āpyāyitaṃ gharmaṃ śaradrūpam | 2. ta-yāge āyātam | 3. kha-ca-jha- ṇa-na-bha-ra-hiṃsam | 4. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-gantṛtamau | yuvām | 5. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra- bhavatam | 6. ta-rakṣaṇenajena ca | 7. ta-āgantṛtamau | nāśayantāviti śeṣaḥ | athavā | 8. kha- jha-bha-ra-‘prati...ca’ nāsti ; na-dāridryaṃ nāśayantau | 9. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-bhavatāṃ’ nāsti ; ga-gha-ja-ta-bhavatam | 10. ta-pañcadaśadhetimānā ; bha-pañcadaśadhātmanā | 11. ga-gha-ja-‘dohabhūmi’ nāsti | 12. ta-bhakṣyanti bhakṣayitvā ca |
ukteṣveva kāleṣu kiṃ tarhi ? divā naktaṃ sarvadā śaṃtamena sukhatamena avasā rakṣaṇena haviṣā vā nimittena ā yātam |
kimarthamāgampate ? pūrvamevānyairdevaiḥ svīkṛtatvāt netyāha |
idānīm api vītiḥ itaradevānāṃ pānaṃ na ā tanāna na tanoti aśvinā aśvinau vihāyeti śeṣaḥ ||
idaṃ hi vāṃ pradivi sthānamorka ime gṛhā aśvinedaṃ duroṇam |
ā nau divo bṛhataḥ parvatādāśvayo yātamiṣamūrjaṃ vahantā ||
4 ||
idam | hi | vām | pra'divi | sthānam | orkaḥ | ime | gṛhāḥ | aśvinā | idam | duroṇam | ā | naḥ | divaḥ | bṛhataḥ | parvatāt | ā | at'imyaḥ | yātam | iṣam | ūrjam | vahantā || 4 ||
ha aśvinā vāṃ yuvayoḥ pradivi purāṇam idaṃ hi okaḥ nivāsayogyaṃ sthānam uttaravedyākhyam |
atha saumikavedyabhiprāyeṇāha |
ime gṛhāḥ prācīnavaṃśadayaḥ |
idaṃ duroṇam devayajanagṛham |
naḥ asmadarthaṃ divaḥ dyulokāt bṛhataḥ parvatāt parvatato meghāt aśvaḥ apāṃ kāraṇādantarikṣāt ā yātam āgacchatam |
kiṃ tarṇīm |
na |
iṣamūrjaṃ ca annaṃ balaṃ ca |
valasādhanamannaṃ sārabhūtabhityarthaḥ |
tādṛśaṃ vahantā vahantau ||
samaśvinoravasā nūtanena mayobhuvā supraṇītī gamema |
ā nau rayiṃ vahatamūta vīṟānā viśvānyamṛtā saubhagāni ||
5 ||
sam | aśvinauḥ | avasā | nūtanena | mayaḥ'bhuvā | su'praṇītī | gamema | ā | naḥ | rayim | vahatam | ā | uta | vīṟān | ā | viśvāni | amṛtā | saubhagāni ||5||
vayaṃ saṃ gamema saṃgacchema aśvināḥ nūtanena avamā ananyakṛtena rakṣaṇena mayobhuvā sukhasya bhāvayitvā supraṇītā supraṇītyā suṣṭhāgamanena ca |
api vaite ubhe avaso viśeṣaṇe |
uktalakṣaṇena saṃ gamema |
naḥ asmabhyaṃ rayim ā vahatam |
uta vīṟān putrādīn ā vahatam |
he amṛtā amaradharmāṇau viśvāni saubhagāni subhagatvāni ā vahatam ||
7||
‘prātaryāvāṇā’ iti pañca ca pañcamaṃ sūktamātrayaṃ traiṣṭubhamāśvinam |
‘prātaryāvāṇā’ iti ānukrāntam |
prātaranuvākāśvinaśastrayoḥ pūrvasūktena sahokto viniyogaḥ |
asoryāme maitrāvaruṇā- tiriktokthye idameva śasyam |
tathā ca sūtritam—‘prātaryāvāṇā kṣetrasya pate madhumantamūrjam’ (ādha. śrau. 9. 11) iti ||
1. sva-na-ṇa-netyāha ; jha-bha-ra-tenyāhāvā ; na -‘kiṃ tarhi’ nāsti |
2. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra -‘ā’ nāsti |
3. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-kimarthaṃ gamyate |
4. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-śa -‘idaṃ hi’ nāsti |
5. ta -vibhā viśvāni |
prātaryāvāṇā prathamā yaṃjadhvaṃ purā gṛdhradaruṣaḥ pibātaḥ |
prātarhi yajñamaśvinā dadhāte pra śaṃsanti kavayaḥ pūrvabhājaḥ ||
1 ||
prātaḥ | ryāvāṇā | prathamā | yajvam | purā | gṛdhrāt | aruṣaḥ | pibātaḥ | prātaḥ | hi | yajñam | aśvinā | dadhāte iti | pra | śaṃsanti | kavayaḥ | pūrvabhājaḥ || 1 ||
he madīyā ṛtvijaḥ 'prātaryāvāṇā prātareva yajñaṃ gantārau' 'prathamā ata evaṃtarebhyo devebhyaḥ pūrvabhāvinau prakṛṣṭamau vā aśvinau 'yajadhvam |
kimarthaṃ prathamayāganiyāgaḥ |
ucyate |
'gṛdhrāt abhiāṅkṣanaḥ 'arupaḥ adāt rakṣaḥprabhṛteḥ 'purā pūrvaṃ 'pibātaḥ pibatām |
anāgataṃ kathaṃ yaṣṭuṃ śakyāviyata āha |
'aśvinā aśvinau 'prātarhi 'yajñaṃ 'dadhāte dhārayataḥ saṃbhajata |
'pūrvabhājaḥ pūrvakālīnāḥ 'kavayaḥ anucānā ṛṣayaḥ prātarevainau 'pra 'śaṃsanti |
'ye vā anucānānte kavayaḥ ' (ai. brā. 2. 38) iti hi nigamaḥ ||
prātaryajvamaśvinā hinota na sāyamasti devayā arjuṣṭam |
utānyo asmadyajate vi cāvaḥ pūrvaḥpūrvoṃ yajamāno vanīyān ||
2 ||
prātaḥ | yajvam | aśvinā | hinota | na | sāyam | asti | devayāḥ | arjuṣṭam | uta | anyaḥ | asmat | yajate | vi | ca | āvaḥ | pūrvaḥ | pūrvaḥ | yajamānaḥ | vanīyān ||2||
he madīyāḥ puruṣāḥ 'aśvinā aśvinau 'prātaḥ prātareva 'yajadhvaṃ pūjayadhvaṃ studhvam |
'hinota prahiṇuta havīṃṣi |
'sāyaṃ sāyaṃkāle haviḥ 'devayāḥ devagāmi 'na 'asti na vidyate |
devā na svīkurva- ntītyarthaḥ |
'arjuṣṭam asevyaṃ tadbhavati |
'pūrvāhṇo vai devānām ' iti hi śrutiḥ |
'uta 'asmat asmat 'anyaḥ ko'pi 'yajate yajeta momena 'vi 'cāvaḥ vitarpayecca haviṣā |
ato'smāsvanyeṣu ca madhye 'pūrvaḥ pūrvo yajamānaḥ yaḥ pūrvaḥ pūrvo yaṣṭā bhavati saḥ 'vanīyān devānāṃ saṃbhajanāyaḥ saṃbhāgyo bhavati ||
'hiraṇyatvaka ' ityepāśvine paśau haviṣā yājyā |
' hiraṇyatvaḍamaśvavarṇo ghṛtasnubhi ṛgveda bhuśru umanu ' ( āśva. śrau. 3. 8 ) iti hi sūtritam ||
hiraṇyatvaḍ madhuvarṇo ghṛtasnuḥ pṛkṣo vahnā rathoṃ vartate vām |
manojavā aśvinā vārtaraṃhā yenātithātho duritāni viśvā ||
3 ||
hiraṇyatvak | madhuvarṇaḥ | ghṛtasnuḥ | pṛkṣaḥ | vahan | ā | rathaḥ | vartate | vām | manaḥjavāḥ | aśvinā | vārtaraṃhāḥ | yena | atithāthaḥ | duḥitāni | viśvā || 3 ||
1. jha-bha-ra-yajñagantārau |
2. kha-ca-ṇa-bha-prathamaṃ yāgavinayogaḥ ; jha-na-prathamayāgavinayogaḥ |
3. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra-'uta' nāsti |
ᵥvāṃ yuvayoḥ ᵥrathaḥ ᵥpṛkṣaḥ annamamṛtaṃ ᵥvahan ᵥā ᵥvartate asmadabhimukhamāgacchati |
kīdṛśo rathaḥ |
ᵥhiraṇyatvak hiraṇyācchāditarūpaḥ ᵥhiraṇyāvṛtaḥ ᵥmadhuvarṇaḥ manoharavarṇaḥ ᵥghṛtasnuḥ udakasya prasnavanaḥ ᵥmanāṃjavāḥ manovegaḥ ᵥvātaraṃhāḥ vātasadṛśavegaḥ |
he ᵥaśvinā ᵥyena rathena ᵥviśvā sarvāṇi ᵥduritāni durgamanān mārgān ᵥatiyāthaḥ atikramya gacchathaḥ ||
yo bhūyiṣṭhaṃ nāsatyābhyāṃ viveṣa caniṣṭhaṃ pitvo rarate vibhāge | sa tokamasya pīparaccharmībhirūrdhvabhāsaḥ saṭamirtuturyāt || 4 || yaḥ | bhūyiṣṭham | nāsatyābhyām | viveṣa | caniṣṭham | pitvaḥ | rarate | vi'bhāge | saḥ | tokam | asya | pīparat | śarmībhiḥ | anūrdhvabhāsaḥ | saṭam | ita | tuturyāt ||4||
ᵥyaḥ yajamānaḥ ᵥvibhāge havirvibhāgavati ᵥyāge ᵥnāsatyābhyām aśvibhyāṃ ᵥbhūyiṣṭhaṃ ᵥcaniṣṭham |
‘cana ityannanāma ’ ( niru. 6. 16 ) |
bahvannaṃ karma ᵥviveṣa karoti |
ᵥpitvaḥ ||
karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī ||
annaṃ ca ᵥrarate dadāti ᵥmaḥ yajamānaḥ ᵥasya ātmana eva ᵥtokaṃ putraṃ ᵥpīparat pālayeta ᵥ ᵥśamībhiḥ karmabhiḥ |
ᵥanūrdhvabhāmaḥ anunnatatejaskāna |
yadvā |

agni- rahitānayaṣṭan ᵥsaṭamit sadeva ᵥtuturyāt hiṃsyāta ||

16 ||
‘aśvināveha ’ iti navacaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ sūktam |
atrānukramaṇikā— ’ aśvinau nava masavādhisyu- dhiṇagādi caturthī triṣṭuppñjāṣṭubhaḥ ’ iti |
masavādhināmātreya ṛṣiḥ |
āditastitra uṣṇihaścaturthī taviṣṭup śiṣṭāḥ pañcānuṣṭubhaḥ |
prātaranuvāka āśvine kratau auṣṇihe chandasi āśvine śastre cādyastṛcā viniyuktaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca— ‘aśvinā vartirasmadāśvināveha gacchatamiti tṛcau ’ ( āśva. śrau. 4. 15 ) iti |
daśarātre tṛtīye'hani praugaśastre'yamāśvinastṛcaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca— ‘aśvināveha gacchatamā yāhyadbhiḥ sutam ’ ( āśva. śrau. 7. 10 ) iti ||
1. kha-ca-ṇa-hiraṇyavaditarūpaḥ |
2. ta-havirbhāgavati |
3. kha-ca-ṇa-pālayata ; jha-ta-na-bha- pālayata |
kha-ca-ṇa-pustakasthasya mantrasya saṃpūrṇa bhāṣyaṃ vigate (parvasaptānte draṣṭavyam ) |
ma. 2-122
aśvināveha gacchataṃ nāsatyā mā vi venatam | haṃsāviva patatamā sutāṃ upa || 1 || aśvinau | ā | iha | gacchatam | nāsatyā | mā | vi | venatam | haṃsau'iva | patatam | ā | sutān | upa || 1 || ha 'aśvinau 'iha asmin yāge 'ā 'gacchatam | ha 'nāsatyā 'mā 'vi 'venatam | venatiḥ kāntikarmā | mā vikārau bhavatam | niḥspṛhāvakāmau vā na bhavatamityarthaḥ | 'haṃsāviva nau yathā nirmalodakamā gacchatastadvana 'sunāna abhipṛtāna somāna 'upa 'ā 'patatam upāgacchatam ||
aśvinā hariṇāviva gaurāvivā nu yarvasam | haṃsāviva patatamā sutāṃ upa ||2|| aśvinā | hariṇau'iva | gaurau'iva | anu | yarvasam | haṃsau'iva | patatam | ā | sutān | upa || 2 || ha aśvinau 'hariṇāviva 'gaurāviva gauramṛgāviva ca | tau yathā 'yavasaṃ' ghāmam 'anu dhāvataḥ tāviva 'haṃsāviva ca somamāgacchatam ||
aśvinā vājinīvasū juṣethāṃ yajñamiṣṭaye | haṃsāviva patatamā sutāṃ upa ||3|| aśvinā | vājinīvasū iti vājinī'vasū | juṣethām | yajñam | iṣṭaye | haṃsau'iva | patatam | ā | sutān | upa || 3 || he 'vājinīvasū annasya vāmayitārau 'aśvinā 'yajñam 'ammadīyam 'iṣṭaye abhīṣṭayeṣaṇāya vā 'juṣethāṃ sevethām ||
atrirgarttamavarohabīsamajauhvīṣvādhamāneva yoṣā | śyenasya cijjarvasā nūtanenāgacchatamaśvinā śaṃtamena || 4 || atriḥ | yat | vām | ava'arohan | ṛbīsam | ajauhvīt | nārdhamānā'iva | yoṣā | śyenasya | cit | jarvasā | nūtanena | ā | agacchatam | aśvinā | śam'tamena || 4 ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ga-yavage dhāsāya |
atriḥ asmatpitā yat yadā vāṃ yuvām ajohavīt astaupīt nādhamānā yācamānā yogā yopidiva |
sā yathā patiṃ prīṇayati tadvat kurvan ṛbīmaṃ nṛpābhimabhikṛṇḍe kṣisaḥ san avarohan vimuñcan ajohavīt |
iyenasya cijjavamā iyenasya garutmata iva vegena |
cidiśyupamārthe |
athavā iyenasya javasā vegena nūtanena prathamotpannena satataṃ gacchataḥ iyenasya prathamajavena śāṃtamena asmākaṃ suvatamena ca rathena taṃ rakṣitum āgacchataṃ he aśvinā ||
10 ||
atra bruvantītihāmaṃ saptavadheḥ purāvidaḥ |
ātrayyāḥ peṭikāyāṃ tamṛṣiṃ prakṣipya yatnataḥ ||
mudrāṃ kṛtvā gṛhaṃ svīye rakṣyitvā nyaveśayan |
na saṃgaccheta bhāryā svāṃ yathā rātrau tathā kṛtam ||
prātaḥprātaḥ samudāhya nirmathiṣyanti taṃ munim |
evaṃ sthitvā ciraṃ kālaṃ peṭāyāṃ duḥkhitaḥ kṛśaḥ ||
upāyaṃ cintayāmāma nirgamasya ciraṃ muniḥ |
hṛdi niścitya nāsatyāvastaupīddhṛṣṭamānamaḥ ||
tamaśvinau mamāgattha samudāhya ca peṭikām |
uddhṛtya tamṛṣiṃ śīghraṃ tāvaṣṭau babhūvataḥ ||
sa ṛṣirbhāryayā sārdhaṃ ramitvātha punardhyāta |
prātaretya punaḥ peṭāma praviśya prāgiva sthitaḥ |
peṭānivāmamamaye dṛṣṭavāndrayaṃ tadā ||
vi jihīṣva vanaspate yoniḥ sūryantyāiva |
śrutaṃ me aśvinā have saptavadhi ca muñcatam ||
5 ||
vi | jihīṣva | vanaspate | yoniḥ | sūryantyāḥ iva | śrutam | me | aśvinā | havam | saptavadhim | ca | muñcatam || 5 ||
haṃ vanaspate vanaspativikārarūpe peṭike vi jihīṣva vigaccha |
vivṛtā bhava |
sūryantyāiva prasavonmukhyāḥ striyāḥ yoniḥ iva bhago yathā triviyate tathā tvamapi vivṛtā bhava |
tadarthaṃ he aśvinā aśvinau me havam āhvānaṃ śrutaṃ śruṇutam |
śrutvā ca saptavadhiṃ ca māmṛṣiṃ muñcataṃ mocayatam ||
bhītāya nārdhamānāya ṛṣaye saptavadhaye |
māyābhiraśvinā yuvaṃ vṛkṣaṃ saṃ ca vi cāthaḥ ||
6 ||
bhītāya | nārdhamānāya | ṛṣaye | saptavadhaye | māyābhiḥ | aśvinā | yuvam | vṛkṣam | sam | ca | vi | ca | acathaḥ || 6 ||
1. ta-prakṣipya | 2. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-śiśuḥ | 3. na-tāvaṣṭyāṃ 4. ta-ramāyitvā | 5. kha-ca-bha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-tathā |
bhītāya nirgamān bibhyuṣe ॰nādhamānāya vimokṣaṃ yācamānāya ॰matavadhaye ॰ṛṣaye he ॰aśvinā aśvinau ॰yuvaṃ yuvāṃ ॰māyābhiḥ ॰vṛkṣaṃ vṛkṣavikāraṃ peṭikāṃ ॰maṃ ॰cācathaḥ mama nirga- mārthaṃ saṃgacchathaḥ |
॰tri acathaḥ ॰tra vibhaktaṃ kuruthaḥ ? ||
yathā vātaḥ puṣkariṇīṃ samiñjayati sarvataḥ | evā te garbha ejatu niraitu darśamāsyaḥ || 7 || yathā | vātaḥ | puṣkariṇīm | samiñjayati | sarvataḥ | eva | te | garbhaḥ | ejatu | niḥaitu | darśamāsyaḥ || 7 ||
etādi ṛṛtrayaṃ garbhasrāviṇyūpanipat |
etādyaṛtrayeṇamau masravadhiḥ svaryāpitaḥ |
garbhiṇyāḥ pramavāyāśu stutavānaśvinādṛṣiḥ ||
yathā vātaḥ ॰puṣkariṇīṃ saraādikaṃ ॰sarvataḥ ॰samiñjayati samyak cālayati ॰eva evaṃ tava ॰garbhaḥ ॰ejatu kampatām itastataḥ saṃcaratu |
॰darśamāsya
‘ mahaṃ no adya ’ iti daśacaṃ saptamaṃ sūktamātreyasya satyaśravasa ārṣaṃ pāṅktamupasthayam |
anukramyate ca — ‘ mahaṃ daśa satyaśravā upasthaṃ tu pāṅktam ’ iti |
prātaranuvāke uṣasye kratau pāṅktaṃ chandasyābhinaśale cedaṃ sūktam |
sūyate hi — ‘ maha nāṃ ayeti pāṅktam ’ ( āśva. śrau. 4. 14 ) iti ||
mahe no adya bodhayoṣo rāya divitmatī | yathā cittro abodhayaḥ satyaśravasi vāyye sujāte ardhasūnṛte || 1 || mahe | naḥ | adya | bodhaya | urṣaḥ | rāya | divitmatī | yathā | cit | naḥ | abodhayaḥ | satyaśravasi | vāyye | sujāte | ardhasūnṛte || 1 ||
‘ adya asmin yāgadine he ‘ upaḥ uperdāva ‘ divitmatāṃ dīptimatī tvaṃ ‘ naḥ asmān ‘ mahe mahate ‘ rāya dhanaprāptaye ‘ bodhaya prajñāpaya |
prakāśayetyarthaḥ |
mati prakāśe kratudvārā dravyasyāpārjayituṃ śakyatvāt |
‘ yathā ‘ cit yathaiva pūrvaṃ ‘ naḥ asmān ‘ abodhayaḥ atīteṣu divaseṣu yathā bodhitavatī tadvadadyāpi ityarthaḥ |
he ‘ sujāte śobhanaprādurbhūte ‘ aśvasūnṛte |
aśvārthā priyasatyātmikā stutivāk yasyāḥ sā |
he tādṛśi devi ‘ vāyye vayaputre ‘ satyaśravasi mayyanugṛhāṇetyarthaḥ ||
yā sunīthe śaucadrathe vyauccho duhitardivaḥ | sā vyuccha sahīyasi satyaśravasi vāyye sujāte aśvasūnṛte || 2 || yā | sunīthe | śaucadrathe | vi | aucchaḥ | duhitaḥ | divaḥ | sā | vi | uccha | sahīyasi | satyaśravasi | vāyye | sujāte | aśvasūnṛte || 2 ||
he ‘ divaḥ ‘ duhitaḥ divaḥ sūryasya putryaḥ ‘ yā tvaṃ ‘ sunīthe etannāmake ‘ śaucadrathe śucadrathasyāpatye pūrvaṃ ‘ vyaucchaḥ vyāmayastamāṃsi ‘ sā tvaṃ ‘ sahīyasi atiśayena balavati ‘ vāyye vayaputre ‘ satyaśravasi mayi ‘ vyuccha tamo vivāsaya |
‘ ucchā vivāse ’ |
vivāsāṃ varjanam |
śiṣṭamuktam ||

he uṣaḥ devi maghoni maghavati tvam etu suripu stotṛṣu vā vīravadyaśaḥ vīraiḥ putrādi- bhirupetamannaṃ yaśo vā ā dhāḥ ādhehi|
ādhehi ānaya ye maghavānaḥ dhanavantaḥ sūrayaḥ naḥ asmabhyaṃ tava stotṛbhyaḥ rādhāṃsi dhanāni ahyā akṣīṇi arāsata dadhuḥ teṣvityarthaḥ ||
tebhyo yuṣmaṃ bṛhadyaśa uṣo maghonyā vaha |
ye no rādhāṃsyaśyā guhyā bharjanta surayaḥ sujāte aśvasūnṛte ||
7 ||
tebhyaḥ | yuṣmam | bṛhat | yaśaḥ | uṣaḥ | maghoni | ā | vaha | ye | naḥ | rādhāṃsi | aśyā | guhyā | bharjanta | sūrayaḥ | sujāte | aśvasūnṛte || 7 ||
he uṣā maghoni tvaṃ tebhyo yuṣmaṃ dyotamānaṃ hiraṇyādirūpaṃ dhanaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ mahadyaśa mahatīṃ kīrtiṃ vā tebhyo yajamānebhyaḥ ā vaha |
ye ādhyāḥ naḥ asmabhyaṃ rādhāṃsi dhanāni aśyā aśvairyuktāni guhyā gābhiryuktāni gosamūhānaśvasamūhānca bhajanta bhajeran dadhuḥ sūrayaḥ dātāraḥ||
uta no gormatīriṣa ā vahā duhitardivaḥ |
mākaṃ sūryasya raśmibhiḥ śukraiḥ śocadbhirarcibhiḥ sujāte aśvasūnṛte ||
8 ||

uta api ca naḥ asmabhyaṃ gormatīḥ gobhirupetāni iṣaḥ annāni ā vaha ānaya he divaḥ duhitaḥ upaḥ |
kadā |

he savitaḥ deva uta api ca trīṇi rocanā rocamānān dhulokān yāsi gacchasi|
'tisro divaḥ pṛthivīḥ' (ṛ. saṃ. 4. 53. 5) ityuktam|
uta api ca sūryasya raśmibhiḥ 'samucyasi saṃgacchasi||
'uca samavāye' ityasyedaṃ rūpam||
udayāt pūrvabhāvī savitā udayāsta- mayavartī sūrya iti|
uta api ca rātrīmubhayataḥ ubhayapārśve parīyase parigacchasi|
uta api ca he deva savitastvaṃ dharmabhiḥ jagaddhārakaiḥ karmabhiḥ mitro bhavasi mitrākhyo devo bhavasi|
athavā prakāśādipradānena sakhā bhavasi sarvajagatām||
utteśiṣe prasavasya tvameka iduta pūṣā bhavasi deva yārmabhiḥ|
uttedaṃ viśvaṃ bhuvanaṃ vi rājasi śyāvāśvaste savitaḥ stomaṃmānaśe ||
5 ||
uta| īśiṣe| prasavasya| tvam| ekaḥ| it| uta| pūṣā| bhavasi| deva| yārmabhiḥ| uta| idam| viśvam| bhuvanam| vi| rājasi| śyāvāśvaḥ| te| savitariti| stomam| ānaśe || 5 ||
he savitaḥ tvameka it eka eva prasavasya sarvakarmāṇujñākaraṇasya īśiṣe samartho bhavasi|
ye laukikaṃ gamanādirūpaṃ vaidikamagnihotrādirūpaṃ karmānutiṣṭhante teṣāṃ sarveṣāmanujñātum eka prabhavasi|
uta api ca pūṣā poṣakaḥ bhavasi he deva yārmabhiḥ gamanaiḥ|
uta api ca idaṃ viśvaṃ bhuvanaṃ bhūtajātaṃ vi rājasi īśiṣe dhārayitum|
yasmādevaṃ mahānubhāvastasmāt he savitaḥ te tubhyaṃ śyāvāśvaḥ ṛṣiḥ stomaṃ stotram ānaśe vyāpnoti karoti|
ityevamātmānaṃ parokṣatayā nirdiśannāha ||
24 ||
'tatsavi tuḥ' iti navacaṃ daśamaṃ sūktaṃ śyāvāśvasyārṣam|
atreyamanukramaṇikā—'tatsavi turnava gāyatramādyānuṣṭup' iti|
prathamānuṣṭup śiṣṭā gāyatryaḥ|
'sāvitraṃ tu' ityuktatvāt sāvitram|
adyā nau deva savitaḥ prajāvatsāvīḥ saubhāgyam | parā duḥsvapnyaṃ suva || 4 || adha | naḥ | deva | savitariti | prajā'vat | sāvīḥ | saubhāgyam | parā | duḥsvapnyam | suva || 4 || he savitaḥ deva naḥ asmabhyam adya asmin yāgadine prajāvat putrādyupetaṃ saubhāgyaṃ dhanaṃ sāvīḥ preraya | duḥsvapnyaṃ duḥsvapnaṃ duḥsvapnaduḥkhakaraṃ dāridryaṃ parā suva dūre preraya ||
viśvāni deva savitarduritāni parā suva | yadbhadraṃ tanna ā suva || 5 || viśvāni | deva | savitaḥ | duḥ'itāni | parā | suva | yat | bhadram | tat | naḥ | ā | suva || 5 || he savitaḥ deva tvaṃ viśvāni duritāni parā suva | yadbhadraṃ prajāpaśugṛhādikaṃ tat naḥ asmabhyam ā suva asmadabhimukhaṃ preraya | 'prajā vai bhadraṃ paśavo bhadraṃ gṛhaṃ bhadram' iti hi śrutiḥ || || 25 ||
anāgaso aditaye devasya savituḥ save |
viśvā vāmāni dhīmahi ||

vayamanāgasaḥ savituḥ prerakasya devasya save anujñāyāṃ satyām aditaye akhaṇḍa- nīyāyai devyai bhūyai anāgasaḥ syāma anaparādhino bhavema |
bhūmyāṃ hi pāpāḥ saṃbhavanti |
apagateṣvāgaḥsu viśvā sarvāṇi vāmāni vananīyāni dhanāni dhīmahi dhārayāma ||
cāturmāsyeṣu vaiśvadeve parvaṇi sāvitrasya dvādaśakapālasya 'ā viśvadeva' ityanuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'ā viśvadevaṃ sapatiṃ vāmamadya savitaḥ' (āśva. śrau. 2.16 ) iti |
prāyaṇīye eṣaiva savitaranuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'ā viśvadevaṃ sapatiṃ ya imā viśvā jātāni' (āśva. śrau. 4. 3 ) iti |
devasuvāṃ haviḥṣu eṣaiva savitaranuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'ā viśvadevaṃ sapatiṃ na pramiye saviturdaivyasya tat' (āśva. śrau. 4. 11 ) iti ||
ā viśvadevaṃ sapatiṃ sūktairyā vṛṇīmahe | satyasaṃvaṃ savitāram || 7 || ā | viśva'devam | sat'patim | su'uktaiḥ | atha | vṛṇīmahe | satya'saṃvam | savitāram || 7 ||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-bha-ra-preraya prajāpaśugṛhādikaṃ preraya ; ta-preraya prajāpaśugṛhādikam |
viśvadevam |
viśve devā yasya vaśe bhavanti taṃ tādṛśam |
sa hi sarvātmā, 'indraṃ mitraṃ varuṇamagnimāhuḥ' ( ṛ. saṃ. 1. 164. 46 ) ityādiśruteḥ itareṣāṃ tadvibhūtitvāt |
satyataṃ satā- manuṣṭhātṛṇāṃ pālakaṃ satyasavaṃ satyānujñaṃ savitāraṃ devam atha asmin yāgadine sūktaiḥ bhā vapaṇīmahe saṃbhajāmahe ||
ya ime ubhe ahanī pura etyaprayucchan |
svādhīrdevaḥ savitā ||
8 ||
yaḥ | ime iti | ubhe iti | ahanī iti | puraḥ | eti | aprayucchan | su'ādhīḥ | devaḥ | savitā || 8 ||
yaḥ savitā devaḥ svādhīḥ śobhanādhyānaḥ sukarmā vā san ime ahanī rātryahanī tayoḥ puraḥ purastāt aprayucchan apramādyan eti gacchati tam ā vṛṇīmahe iti saṃbandhaḥ ||
prāyaṇīye 'ya imā viśvā' iti savituryājyā |
sūtritaṃ ca— 'ya imā viśvā jātāni sutrāmāṇaṃ pṛthivīṃ dyāmedhasam' ( āśva. śrau. 4. 3 ) iti |
aśvamedhe'śvamutsṛjyānusavanaṃ sāvitrīṣṭiḥ kāryā |
tasyāmeṣā mādhyaṃdine savane'nuvākyā |

ya imā viśvā jātānyāśrāvayati ślokena |
pra ca suvāti savitā ||
9 ||

jajñamānityarthaḥ |
tān ślokena yaśasā āśrāvayati |
sarve'pyasya stutiṃ śṛṇvantītyarthaḥ |
athavā garjanaśabdena sarvāṇi imānyutpannānyāśrāvayati vṛṣṭyunmukhaḥ san |
kiṃca savitā pra suvāti ca prerayati taṃ vṛṇīmaha iti ||

'acchā vada' iti daśarcemekādaśaṃ sūktaṃ bhaumasyātreyārṣam |

1 ||
acchā | vada | tavasam | gīḥ'bhiḥ | ābhiḥ | stuhi | parjanyam | namasā | vivāsa | kanikradat | vṛṣabhaḥ | jīradānuḥ | retaḥ | dadhātu | oṣadhīṣu | garbham || 1 ||
1. ga-gha-ja- 'ca' nāsti | 2. ga-gha-ja-iti | ca pūraṇaḥ | 3. ta-sūktena sūktena |
he stotaḥ ˅tavasaṃ balavantaṃ ˅parjanyam ˅accha abhiprāya ˅vad prārthaya |

˅ābhiḥ ˅gobhiḥ stutivāgbhiḥ ˅stuhi |

ayaṃ mantro nirukte spaṣṭaṃ vyākhyātaḥ |

rathīva kaśayāśvā~ abhikṣipannāviridūtānkṛṇute vṛṣṇyā'he | durātsiṃhasya stanathā udīrate yatparjanyaḥ kṛṇute vṛṣṇyaṃ nabhaḥ || 3 || rathī'iva | kaśayā | aśvān | abhi'kṣipan | āviḥ | dūtān | kṛṇute | vṛṣṇyān | ahe | durāt | siṃhasya | stanathāḥ | ut | īrate | yat | parjanyaḥ | kṛṇute | vṛṣṇyam | nabhaḥ ||3||
˅rathīva rathasvāmīva |
sa yathā kaśayā aśvān abhikṣipan dūtān bhaṭān āviṣkaroti tadvadasau parjanyo'pi ˅kaśayā ˅aśvān meghān ˅abhikṣipan abhiprerayan ˅vapyāna varṣakān ˅dūtān dūtavat vṛṣṭiprarakān meghān maruto vā ˅āviḥ ˅kṛṇute prakaṭayati |
˅aha iti pūraṇaḥ |

bhūmistome caiṣā marutvatīyaśastre sūktamukhīyā ( āśva. śrau. 9. 5 ) ||
bali̭tthā pa॒rvatā̭nāṃ khi॒draṃ bi̭bharṣi pṛthi॒vi |
pra yā bhūmī̭ pra॒tvati̭ ma॒hā ji̭noṣi ma॒hini̭ ||
1 ||
bat | i॒tthā | pa॒rvatā̭nām | khi॒dram | bi॒bharṣi̭ | pṛ॒thi॒vi | pra | yā | bhūmī̭m | pra॒tvati̭ | ma॒hā | ji॒noṣi̭ | ma॒hini̭ || 1 ||
1. jha-ta-bha-ra-udutkṛṣṭam |
2. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-jalavata: ( ta-jalapradeśān ) dhanvāni nirudakapradeśānaka: |
dvirūpā pṛthivī caiṣā pratyakṣā¹ devatāpi ca |
madhyasthānā devatoktā sātra saṃbodhya² varṇyate ||
he pṛthivi prathanavati madhyasthānadevate |
tvam itthā ittham amutra antarikṣe vā yad satyaṃ parvatānāṃ meghānāṃ vā khidaṃ khedanaṃ bhedanaṃ bibharṣi dhārayasi |
he mahin mahati he pradhvati prakarṣavati pravaṇodakavati vā yā tvaṃ bhūmiṃ pratyakṣāṃ pṛthivīṃ mahnā mahattvena mahodakena vā pra jinoṣi prakarṣeṇa prīṇayasi ||
stomā̭sa॒tvā vi॒cāri̭ṇi॒ prati̭ ṣṭoma॒ntyu॒kthai̭ḥ |
pra yā vā॒jaṃ na hḙṣa॒ntaṃ pe॒ruma̭sya॒syarjṷni ||
2 ||
stomā̭saḥ | tvā | vi॒'cāri̭ṇi | prati̭ | sto॒bha॒nti॒ | a॒kthai̭ḥ | pra | yā | vā॒jam | na | he॒ṣa॒ntam | pe॒rum | a॒sya॒si | a॒rju॒ni || 2 ||
he vicāriṇi vividhaṃ caraṇaśīle pṛthivi devi tvā tvāṃ stomāsaḥ stotāraḥ akthaiḥ gamanaśīlaiḥ stotraiḥ prati ṣṭomanti abhiṣṭuvanti |
kiṃca yā tvaṃ hepantaṃ śabdayantaṃ vājaṃ na aśvamiva uddhataṃ peruṃ pūrakaṃ meghaṃ pra asyasi prakṣipasi he arjani śubhravarṇe gamanaśīle vā ||
devānāṃ haviḥpu 'dṛlhā cit' iti pṛthivyā yājyā |
evaiva bhūmistome niṣkevalye sūktamukhyā |
sūtraṃ tu pūrvamevodāhṛtam ||
dṛ॒lhā ci॒dvana॒spatī॒nkṣa॒mayā॒ dardha̭yo॒jasā̭ |
yattḙ a॒bhrasya̭ vi॒dyuto॒ divo̭ va॒rṣanti̭ vṛ॒ṣṭaya̭ḥ ||
3 ||
dṛ॒lhā | ci॒t | va॒na॒spatī̭n | kṣa॒mayā̭ | da॒rdhaḥ | o॒jasā̭ | yat | te | a॒bhrasya̭ | vi॒'dyuta̭ḥ | di॒vaḥ | va॒rṣanti̭ | vṛ॒ṣṭaya̭ḥ || 3 ||
he pṛthivi yā tvaṃ dṛlhā cit 'supāṃ suluk' iti tṛtīyāyā ākāraḥ |
dṛḍhayā kṣamayā bhūmyā saha vanaspatīn vṛkṣān ojasā balena dardharṣi dhārayasi |
athavā dṛḍheti vidheya- viśeṣaṇam |
vanaspatīn dṛḍhān kṛtvā dhārayasi ityarthaḥ |
yat yasyāḥ te tava saṃbandhin vṛṣṭayaḥ varṣakā meghāḥ vidyutaḥ vidyotamānāt abhrasya pañcamyarthe ṣaṣṭhī |
abhrāt apāṃ hartuḥ divaḥ antarikṣāt ādityādvā varṣanti |
athavā yat yasyāste tavābhrasya meghasya vṛṣṭaya udakasaṃghātā vidyuto vidyotamānāyā divaḥ sakāśāt varṣanti patanti ||

1. ra-pratyakṣadevatāpi | 2. ta-saṃbodhyate | 3. ta-pṛthivīdevi | 4. kha-ga-gha-ca-ja-jha-ṇa-na- bha-śa-abhistuvanti | 5. ta-pṛthivi devi | 6. ta-dṛlheti | 7. ta-asyāḥ | 8. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na- bha-ra-divo dyotamānāt | 9. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra-divaḥ nīcaiḥ | 10. kha-ga-gha-ca-ja-jha-ṇa-na-bha- 'athavā... patanti' nāsti |

1 ||
pra | sam'rāje | bṛhat | arca | gabhīram | brahma | priyam | varuṇāya | śrutāya | vi | yaḥ | jaghāna | śamitā'iva | carma | upa'stare | pṛthivīm | sūryāya || 1 ||
atraḥ' svātmānaṃ saṃbadhya bravīti |
he atra tvaṃ 'samnāje samyaka rājamānāyeśvarāya 'śrutāya sarvatra śrūyamānāya 'varuṇāya upadravasya nivārakāya janānāmāvarakāya caitannāmakāya devāya 'bṛhat prabhūtaṃ 'gabhīraṃ duravagāhaṃ baddhathopetaṃ 'priyaṃ priyabhūtaṃ 'brahma stotrarūpaṃ karma 'pra 'arca prārcaya |
proccāra- yetyarthaḥ |

'tṛtīyamyāṃ' pṛthivyām' (tai. saṃ. 1. 2. 12. 1) iti śruteḥ antarikṣasyāpi pṛthivīśabdavācyatvam ||
'sūryāya sūryasya 'upastare āstaraṇāya 'vi 'jaghāna vistārayāmāsa |
vistṛtaṃ hi vyavahārayogyaṃ bhavati tasmāna vyavahārāya antarikṣaṃ vistāritavān |
'uraṃ hi rājā varuṇaścakāra' (ṛ. saṃ. 1. 24. 8) iti yuktam ||
vaneṣu vyantarikṣaṃ tatān vājamarvatsu parya usriyāsu |
hṛtsu kratuṃ varuṇo apsvagniṃ divi sūryamadhātsomamadrau ||
2 ||
vaneṣu | vi | antarikṣam | tatān | vājam | arvatsu | paryaḥ | usriyāsu | hṛt'su | kratum | varuṇaḥ | ap'su | agnim | divi | sūryam | adhāt | somam | adrau || 2 ||
ayaṃ 'varuṇaḥ 'vaneṣu vṛkṣāmreṣu 'antarikṣaṃ 'vi 'tatāna vistāritavān |
tathā vājasaneyakaṃ — 'vaneṣu hīdameṣu vṛkṣāmreṣvantarikṣaṃ vitataṃ tasmādāha vaneṣu vyantarikṣaṃ tatāna' iti |
'vājaṃ balam 'arvatsu aśveṣu 'vi tatāna |
'payaḥ kṣīram 'usriyāsu goṣu |
usriyeti gonāma ustrāviṇo'syāṃ bhogā iti tadrutpattiḥ |
'hṛtsu hṛdayeṣu 'kratuṃ karmasaṃkalpam |
yadi manasā dhyāyati' tatkarmaṇā karoti |
'apsu udakeṣu 'agniṃ vaidyutabhaumaṃ vā |
sarvatra vi tatāneti saṃbandhaḥ |
kiṃca 'divi dhuloke 'sūryam 'adhāt sthāpitavān |
'somaṃ ca 'adrau parvate'dadhāt ||

cit prakarṣeṇa pūjyau | ciditi pūjāyām | tādṛśau mahānubhāvau devau arbate aśvalābhāya puro dadhe puraskurve | aṃśeva | aṃśo nāma dvādaśādityamadhyavartī devaḥ | sa bhagasyāpyupalakṣakaḥ | aṃśāviva ādityāviva | dīptāvityarthaḥ ||

32 ||
‘pra vo mahe’ iti navacaṃ pañcadaśaṃ sūktam evayāmarudasya ātreyasya munerārṣaṃ maru-he- vatākamatijagatīcchandaskam |
tathā cānukramaṇikā—‘pra vo navaivayāmarunmārutamatijagatam’ iti |
daśarātre paṣṭhe'hani tṛtīyasavaneḍacchāvākaśāstre idaṃ sūktam |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘evayāmarudukto vṛṣākapinā’ ( āśva. śrau. 8. 4 ) iti ||
pra vo̭ ma॒he ma॒tayo̭ yantu viṣṇa̭ve ma॒rutva̭te॒ giri̭jā e॒vayā̭marut |
pra śardhāya॒ parya̭jyave su॒khāda̭ye॒ tava̭se॒ bhanda̭diṣṭaye॒ dhuni̭vratāya॒ śava̭se ||
1 ||
pra | vaḥ | mahe | matayaḥ | yantu | viṣṇave | marutvate | girijāḥ | evayāmarut | pra | śardhāya | paryajyave | sukhādaye | tavase | bhandadiṣṭaye | dhunibratāya | śavase || 1 ||
pra yantu pragacchantu girijāḥ vāci niṣpannāḥ | matayaḥ stutayaḥ | kasmai | vaḥ tubhyam | vacana- vyatyayaḥ | viṣṇave vyāsāyendrāya viṣṇave vā marutvate marudbhiryukte | kasya stutaya ityucyate | eva- yāmarūt etannāmakasya ṛṣeḥ || ṣaṣṭhyā luk || athavā ayamgirijāḥ stuterjanayitā bhavati | uttarādheṃ marutstutiḥ | kiṃca pra yantu stutayaḥ | kasmai | śardhāya balāya mārutāya | itarat sarvaṃ balaviśeṣaṇam | paryajyave prakarṣeṇa yaṣṭavyāya sukhādaye śobhanābharaṇāya | khādiḥ ābharaṇaviśeṣaḥ | ‘hasteṣu khādiśca ( ṛ. saṃ. 1. 168. 3 ) ; ‘paśu khādayaḥ’ ( ṛ. saṃ. 5. 54. 11 ) iti ca śruteḥ | tavase
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra- ‘rayiṃ dhanaṃ ca’ nāsti |
sa cakrame mahato nirukramaḥ samānasmātsardasa evayāmarut | yadāyukta tmanā svādhiva ṇubhirvirṣadhaso vimahaso jigāti śevadho nṛbhiḥ ||4|| saḥ | cakrame | mahataḥ | niḥ | uru'kramaḥ | samānasmāt | sardasaḥ | evayāmarut | yadā | ayukta | tmanā | svāt | adhi | snu'bhiḥ | vi'spardhasaḥ | vi'mahasaḥ | jigāti | śe'vadhaḥ | nṛbhiḥ || 4 ||
▼saḥ marudgaṇaḥ ▼mahataḥ pravṛddhāt ▼samānasmāt sarveṣāṃ sādhāraṇāt ▼sadasaḥ sthānāt anta- rikṣāt ▼urukramaḥ vistīrṇakramaṇaḥ san ▼niḥ ▼cakrame niragacchat |
▼evayāmarut ṛṣiḥ ▼yadā ▼svāt svakīyasthānāt |
▼adhi iti pañcamyarthānuvādī |
▼tmanā ātmanaiva ▼snubhiḥ gantṛbhiḥ ▼nṛbhiḥ svanetṛbhiraśvaiḥ |
▼ayukta yukte'bhavat āgamanaprārthanāya |
atha bahuvadyucyate |
tadānīṃ ▼vispardhasaḥ vividhaspardhāḥ |
ahaṃ purato gacchāgyahaṃ purato gacchāmīti teṣāṃ spardhā |
athavā vigataspardhāḥ |
na hyeṣāṃ sārdhaṃ spardhakāḥ santi |
▼vimahasaḥ viśiṣṭabalāḥ ▼śevadhaḥ śevasya sukhasya vardhayitāraḥ ▼jigāti nirgacchanti |
vyatyayena ekavacanam ||
svano na vo'mavāñjayadadhṛṣā tveṣo yuyistaviṣa evayāmarut | yenā sahanta ṛñjata svarociṣaḥ svāraśmāno hiraṇyayāḥ | svāyudhāsa iṣmiṇaḥ || 5 || svanaḥ | na | vaḥ | ama'vān | rejayat | vṛṣā | tveṣaḥ | yayiḥ | taviṣaḥ | evayāmarut | yena | sahantaḥ | ṛñjata | sva'rociṣaḥ | syāḥ'raśmānaḥ | hiraṇyayāḥ | su'āyudhāsaḥ | iṣmiṇaḥ || 5 ||
he mahataḥ ▼vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ ▼svanaḥ vegajanitaḥ śabdaḥ ▼na ▼rejayat na kampayatyevayāmarutaṃ mām ||
‘supāṃ suluk’ iti dvitīyāyāḥ2 luk2 ▼svano viśiṣyate |
▼amavān balavān ▼vṛṣā varṣitā vṛṣṭeḥ ▼tveṣaḥ dīptaḥ ▼yayiḥ3 gantā ▼taviṣaḥ pravṛddhaḥ3 |
▼yena svanena ▼sahantaḥ śatrūnabhibhavanto yūyam ▼ṛñjata prasādhayatha ||
laḍarthe loṭ ||
kīdṛśā yūyam ? ▼svarociṣaḥ svāyattaraśmayaḥ ▼syāraśmānaḥ sthiraraśmayaḥ ▼hiraṇyayāḥ hiraṇyābharaṇāḥ ▼svāyudhāsaḥ svāyattaāyudhāḥ ▼iṣmiṇaḥ aśvavanto gamanavanto vā ||
3||
1. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-ta-na-bha-ra- bahuvacanam |
2. na-śa- dvitīyāluk |
3. kha-ca-jha-ṇa-na-bha-ra- taviṣo gantā |
apāro vau mahimā vṛddhaśavasastveṣaṃ śavo'vatavevayāmarut |
sthātāro hi prasiṃtau saṃdṛśi sthan te na uruṣyatā nidaḥ śuśukāṃso nāmaryaḥ ||
6 ||
apāraḥ | vaḥ | mahimā | vṛddha'śavasaḥ | tveṣam | śavaḥ | avatu | evayāmarut | sthātāraḥ | hi | prasiṃtau | sama'dṛśi | sthan | te | naḥ | uruṣyata | nidaḥ | śuśukāṃsaḥ | na | amaryaḥ || 6 ||
he marutaḥ vaḥ yuṣmākaṃ mahimā apāraḥ anavadhikaḥ |
he vṛddhaśavasaḥ pravṛddhabalāḥ tveṣaṃ dīptaṃ śavaḥ yuṣmākaṃ balam avatu rakṣatu evayāmarut mām ||
dvitīyaikavacanasya luk ||
yūyaṃ prasiṃtau prabalabandhane niyamanavati yajñe saṃdṛśi saṃdarśane nimitte sthātāro hi sthānaniyamena tiṣṭhanto bhavatha khalu |
te naḥ asmān nidaḥ nindakāt śatroḥ sakāśāt uruṣyata rakṣata |
aprayaḥ iva śuśu- kāṃsaḥ yūyamiti ||
te rudrāsaḥ sumakhā amaryo yathā tuvidyumnā avantevayāmarut |
dīrghaṃ pṛthu praprathe sannā pārthivaṃ yeṣāmajeṣvā mahaḥ śardhāsyadbhutenasām ||
7 ||
te | rudrāsaḥ | su'makhāḥ | amaryaḥ | yathā | tuvi'dyumnāḥ | avantu | evayāmarut | dīrgham | pṛthu | praprathe | sannā | pārthivam | yeṣām | ajeṣu | ā | mahaḥ | śardhāṃsi | adbhuta'enasām || 7 ||
^te marutaḥ ^rudrāsaḥ rudraputrāḥ ^sumakhāḥ śobhanayajñāḥ ^aprayo yathā amraya iva ^tuvidyumnāḥ prabhūtadīptayaḥ |
athavā agnisadṛśāstuvidyumnāḥ prabhūtadhanāḥ ^avantu rakṣantu ^evayāmarut etannāmānamṛṣiṃ mām |
^pārthivam |
pṛthivyantārikṣam |
tatsaṃbandhi ^dīrgham āyataṃ ^pṛthu vistīrṇaṃ ^sannā sadanaṃ ^praprathe mahadbhiḥ prathitamabhūt |
^adbhutenasām apāpānāṃ ^yeṣām ^ajeṣu gamaneṣu ^mahaḥ mahānti ^śardhāṃsi ca ^ā gacchantīti śeṣaḥ ||
adveṣo nau maruto gātumetan śrotā haveṃ jariturevayāmarut |
viṣṇormahaḥ saṃmanyavo yuyotan smadradhyo na daṃsanāpa dveṣoṃsi sanutaḥ ||
8 ||
adveṣaḥ | naḥ | marutaḥ | gātum | ā | itana | śrota | havam | jarituḥ | evayāmarut | viṣṇoḥ | mahaḥ | sa'manyavaḥ | yuyotana | smat | radhyaḥ | na | daṃsanā | apa | dveṣoṃsi | sanutarti || 8 ||
1. kha-ca-jha-na-bha- evayāmarudevayāmarut |
he marutaḥ advepa: adveṣaso yūyaṃ na: asmākaṃ gātuṃ gamanasvabhāvaṃ stotraṃ prati śrutana āgacchata |
tadarthaṃ jaritu: stotu: evayāmṛta evayāmṛto mama havaṃ śrota śṛṇuta |
viṣṇo: vyāpakasyendrasya maha: mahata: he samanvaya: samānayajñā yūyaṃ smat |
ayaṃ praśastanāma |
praśastā: rathyo na rathino yodhdāra iva |
te yathā śatrūnapākarvanti tadvadasmākaṃ daṃsanā karmaṇā sanuta: |
antarhitanāmaitat |
antarhitān dveṣāṃsi dveṣṭṛn apa yuyotana dūre viyojayata pṛthakkurūta ||
adhyāyopākaraṇotsargayo: viniyuktā (āśva. gṛ. 3. 5 ) ||
gantā no yajñaṃ yajñiyā: suśami śrotā havamarakṣa evayāmṛta |
jyeṣṭhāso na parvatāso vyomani yūyaṃ tasya pracetasa: syāta durdhataiva nida: ||
9 ||
gantaṃ | na: | yajñam | yajñiyā: | su'śami | śrotā | havam | arakṣa: | evayāmṛta | jyeṣṭhāsa: | na | parvatāsa: | vi'omani | yūyam | tasya | pra'cetasa: | syāt | du:'dhataiva | nida: || 9 ||
he yajñiyā: marutaḥ yūyaṃ na: asmākaṃ yajñaṃ ganta āgacchata |
suśami śobhanakarma yathā bhavati tathā |
sukarmatvāyetyartha: |
tadarthaṃ na: havam asmadīyamāhvānaṃ śrota śṛṇuta |
arakṣa: rakṣo- varjitā yūyam ||
liṅgavacanayordhyatyaya: ||
jyeṣṭhāso na jyeṣṭhā iva parvatāsa: vindhyādaya ivāti- pravṛddhā: vyomani antarikṣe vartamānā: pracetasa: yūyaṃ tasya nida: nindakasya durdhataiva durdharā: syāta bhavata ||
34 ||
iti pañcame maṇḍale ṣaṣṭho'nuvāka: samāptaṃ cātraiyaṃ pañcamaṃ maṇḍalam ||
1. na- viniyuktā |
gantā no yajñamiti cottara ca |
athāto'dhyāyopākaraṇamiti khaṇḍe gantā no yajñaṃ yajñiyā: suśamityupakramya iti dṛcā iti tathotsarga iti ca sūtrāt |
śuddhipatram
pṛṣṭha |
paṅktā |
aśuddham |
